## `ZagreusField_0002a`

## `ZagreusField_0103`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What is it, Shade?

## `ZagreusField_0104`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

## `ZagreusField_0105`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tell me something, Wraith.

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_0106`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A gift.

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_0107`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The gods have spoken.

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_0108a`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**中文**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**中文**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What do we have here.

**中文**

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I accept this gift.

我收下这件礼物。

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Should come in handy.

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Noxious fields.

**中文**

恶毒之地。

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Best watch my step.

**中文**

最好小心行事。

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nothing here.

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got to be careful here.

**中文**

在这里必须小心。

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I hear something.

**中文**

我听到有动静。

## `ZagreusField_0125`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The damned are watching.

**中文**

被诅咒者都在看。

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Something's here.

**中文**

这里好像有什么东西。

## `ZagreusField_0127`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You. Come to fulfill my father's wishes?

**中文**

你。是过来了却我父亲的心愿的吗？

## `ZagreusField_0128`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You. Come, let's dance.

**中文**

你。过来，我们一起跳支舞。

## `ZagreusField_0129`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My fate is mine.

**中文**

我的命运，由我做主。

## `ZagreusField_0130`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I won't have it.

**中文**

我不会让这样的事情发生。

## `ZagreusField_0131`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I have a better idea.

**中文**

我有个更好的主意。

## `ZagreusField_0132`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's try this instead.

**中文**

我们来试试这样做。

## `ZagreusField_0133`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's better.

**中文**

这样更好。

## `ZagreusField_0134`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hmm.

**中文**

嗯。

## `ZagreusField_0135`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good.

**中文**

不错。

## `ZagreusField_0136`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Come on, Meg. Haven't we had more than enough of each other by now? Besides, I heard Sisyphus was feeling rather lonesome.

**中文**

拜托，墨纪。我们不是相互之间受够了对方吗？再说，我听说西西弗斯非常寂寞。

## `ZagreusField_0137`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I'll have to go with the painful way.

**中文**

……我选第二项。

## `ZagreusField_0138`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Charon, mate...? I don't suppose you've given any further thought to my proposal to, just, you know... ferry me up to the surface? Seeing as it's not out of your way.

**中文**

呃，卡戎，伙计…？你好像没有考虑过我的建议啊，就是，你直接…载我到凡界行不？果然还是不行吧。

## `ZagreusField_0139`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...Well, I had to ask.

**中文**

...好吧，我不得不说。

## `ZagreusField_0140`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Erm... all right then, carry on, I guess.

**中文**

呃...那好吧，我想也只能坚持下去。

## `ZagreusField_0141`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Are you Eurydice, by any chance...? A mutual friend told me to seek you out. I've a message from Orpheus. He says he's very sorry about what happened. And believe me, I can tell he is.

**中文**

您是不是欧律狄刻？我们一位共同的朋友让我找您。我有一份来自俄耳甫斯的信。他说他关于发生了的事很抱歉。相信我，我看得出他是真心的。

## `ZagreusField_0142`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, you know, just out on a little stroll is all. Do me a favor, though, you see the Fury Sisters, let them know I was headed down the way I came from, will you...? Though, here, let me give you a hand.

**中文**

哦，你也知道，出去散散心也不错。不过，请你帮个忙，如果你见到复仇女神三姐妹，告诉她们我打道回府了，好吗...？而我现在也可以给你搭把手。

## `ZagreusField_0143`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know it's been all right, all things considered. Dying now and then, and it's been hard, but hey, what can you do.

**中文**

还算可以吧，所有事情计划周全了。死来死去的话有点艰难，不过，也没有其他办法了。

## `ZagreusField_0144`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Feeling's mutual, Sir. Someday you'll have to tell me all about how you tricked Father. I might learn a thing or two. Now here, though, let me help you up.

**中文**

深有同感，先生。有时间你一定要告诉我你是怎么骗到我父亲的。我得跟你学学。而现在，让我来帮你一把。

## `ZagreusField_0145`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm... have we met?

**中文**

呃……我们见过面吗？

## `ZagreusField_0146`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Up there. Contract shipment must've gotten lost.

**中文**

看那里。契约的货物肯定不见了。

## `ZagreusField_0147`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to make some changes around here.

**中文**

是时候做些改变了。

## `ZagreusField_0148`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I need a weapon.

**中文**

我需要一件武器。

## `ZagreusField_0149`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Better arm myself.

**中文**

好好地武装自己。

## `ZagreusField_0150`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Where'd that come from...? Achilles?

**中文**

那是从哪里来的...？阿喀琉斯？

## `ZagreusField_0151`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Stygius, Blade of the Underworld; I need your strength.

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Look, I have to level with you, Sisyphus. I'm leaving the House. I've had quite enough. You understand, I'm sure. I'm going to the surface.

**中文**

我得跟你说实话，西西弗斯。我要离开圣殿。我受够了。你知道我是认真的，我要去凡间。

## `ZagreusField_0466`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sisyphus, don't take this the wrong way, but... why are you helping me? My father's stuck you here for an eternity of torment and even still, you greet me with a smile, time after time.

**中文**

西西弗斯，你不要会错意了啊，但是…你为什么要帮我？我父亲把你困在这里永远惩罚你，但你对我却依旧笑容满面。

## `ZagreusField_0467`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Sisyphus, you mind my asking what you did to warrant this particular arrangement you've got here? Pushing a boulder till the end of time and all. Never heard it in your own words.

**中文**

嗯，西西弗斯，你是做了什么才会被弄到这里来？你永远都要推着这块大石头。我从来没听你亲口谈过这些。

## `ZagreusField_0468`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Outsmarted Thanatos, that's amazing... what happened then?

**中文**

智胜塔纳托斯，真厉害…后来呢？

## `ZagreusField_0469`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So then, you're stuck forever, no reprieve. That's terrible... wish there was something I could do to help.

**中文**

所以，你就永远困在了这里，无法解脱。太可怕了…希望有什么我能帮到你的。

## `ZagreusField_0470`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey sir, I've always wondered, what were you like back when you were alive? Were you always this wise?

**中文**

嗨先生，我一直想知道，你生前是一个怎样的人？一直都是这样有智慧吗？

## `ZagreusField_0471`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Huh! I thought I sensed the royalty in you! Although, I can't imagine you as anything besides the gentleman I know you for, my friend.

**中文**

咳！我就知道你是有王室血统的人！但我还是不能想象你除了是我认识的那位绅士以外，还是什么样的人。

## `ZagreusField_0472`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, quick question, Sisyphus, you seen the other Fury Sisters lately?

**中文**

嘿，问你个问题，西西弗斯，最近有见过其他的复仇女神姐妹吗？

## `ZagreusField_0473`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sisyphus, how can you stand it, being here like this? You always seem in such good spirits, though.

**中文**

西西弗斯，你是怎么忍受的，受这种折磨？你看起来总是很精神。

## `ZagreusField_0474`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Does Bouldy really keep you company? Personally I would resent a giant boulder that keeps tumbling back down a hill after I push it all the way up there.

**中文**

这石头真的一直陪着你吗？我把它从山脚推到山顶，然后它又滚下来，如此反复这样。

## `ZagreusField_0475`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sisyphus, you ever lose somebody dear to you? There's someone out there whom I need to find. That's... all that I can say right now.

**中文**

西西弗斯，你有失去过自己爱的人吗？外面有一个我要去寻找的人。目前…我只能说这么多。

## `ZagreusField_0476`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You all right, there, sir?

**中文**

你还好吗，先生？

## `ZagreusField_0477`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

She's gone... You still in one piece, Sisyphus? Not hurt or anything?

**中文**

她走了……你还好吧，西西弗斯？有没有受伤？

## `ZagreusField_0478`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You've really never gotten Bouldy to the top before?

**中文**

您真的从没把巨石推到顶过吗？

## `ZagreusField_0479`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sisyphus, I don't know where you heard that, but... it's true. They're... they're just doing their jobs, and I... I know how it looks, but... you're my friend, as well, and I don't want you to forget that.

**中文**

西西弗斯，不知道你是从哪儿听来的，但…这是真的。他们…他们只是在做自己的工作，我……我知道的！但……你是我的朋友，我不想让你忘记这点。

## `ZagreusField_0480`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thank you for understanding, sir. Besides... maybe I can get them to cut you some slack.

**中文**

感谢你的理解，先生。另外…也许我能说服他们对你宽松些。

## `ZagreusField_0481`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sounds like Charon's breathing up ahead.

**中文**

听着好像卡戎就在那边。

## `ZagreusField_0482`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Think I hear the river.

**中文**

我觉得是地狱之河的声音。

## `ZagreusField_0483`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Should come in useful.

**中文**

应该用得上。

## `ZagreusField_0484`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I could use this.

**中文**

我可以用上它。

## `ZagreusField_0485`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cheers, mate.

**中文**

再见，伙计。

## `ZagreusField_0486`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll take it.

**中文**

它是我的了。

## `ZagreusField_0487`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Why not.

**中文**

当然。

## `ZagreusField_0488`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Here's the fee.

**中文**

给你钱。

## `ZagreusField_0489`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Just what I need.

**中文**

正是我需要的。

## `ZagreusField_0490`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Can't afford that.

**中文**

买不起。

## `ZagreusField_0491`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not enough coin.

**中文**

硬币不够。

## `ZagreusField_0492`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm short of coin.

**中文**

我的硬币不够用。

## `ZagreusField_0493`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Can't buy that.

**中文**

买不了。

## `ZagreusField_0494`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Poor me.

**中文**

我太可怜了。

## `ZagreusField_0495`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What's new, Charon?

**中文**

有什么新鲜事吗，卡戎？

## `ZagreusField_0496`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How goes it, mate?

**中文**

一切都还好吗，朋友？

## `ZagreusField_0497`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Greetings, Charon.

**中文**

好呀，卡戎。

## `ZagreusField_0498`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Why good to see you, Charon mate! Just minding my own business, taking in the sights, and, hey what's that you got, some sort of wares for sale? Then, I'll just have a look around.

**中文**

见到你真好，卡戎老伙计！我正做我自己的事呢，也看看风景什么的，那么，你这里有什么，看看你有什么好货。我就四处转转。

## `ZagreusField_0499`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Got to say, you are a sight for sore eyes, there, Charon! So, uh... looks like you're selling, is that it? You come to help me out?

**中文**

说真的，你是真的让人着迷呢，卡戎！那么，呃…你要卖东西了吗？你也要来帮助我了吗？

## `ZagreusField_0500`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey good to see you out here, mate! And looks as though you've got some fine wares up for sale, don't you?

**中文**

很高兴看见你，伙计！看起来你好像又有些好货可以卖了！

## `ZagreusField_0501`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Fine day to you as well, there, mate! Now let's have a look at your supply, shall we?

**中文**

也祝你愉快，伙计！现在让我们看看你有什么好货吧！

## `ZagreusField_0502`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Glad you like it, mate. Thank you for helping me out with your supply. Even if you fleece me with those prices!

**中文**

你喜欢就好，伙计。谢谢你和你的好货，帮了我大忙。就算你的价格是那样离谱。

## `ZagreusField_0503`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You enjoy that, mate! And, when it comes time to reconsider your price structure, hope you think of me!

**中文**

你喜欢就好，伙计！还有，如果你考虑重新定价的时候，希望你可以想到我！

## `ZagreusField_0504`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Enjoy it, Charon mate! Don't go thinking I'm just being kind to get you to lower your prices for me, that would be unconscionable!

**中文**

尽情享受吧，卡戎老伙计！不过，别以为我是为了让你降价才对你这么好，我可不会干这种没良心的事！

## `ZagreusField_0505`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm sure he'd welcome all this feedback from you, Meg. Or maybe you're just pining for my father's job?

**中文**

那我想他应该蛮乐意听到你的这些报告的，墨纪。还是说你想坐上我父亲的位置？

## `ZagreusField_0506`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Admit it, Meg. You can't stop thinking about me.

**中文**

承认吧，墨纪。你就是一直在想我。

## `ZagreusField_0507`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How about... a swift and painful death?

**中文**

死得痛...快一点如何？

## `ZagreusField_0508`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

While that's probably the case... maybe I will this time.

**中文**

那么问题来了，也许这次我就能超过你。

## `ZagreusField_0509`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have some bad news for you, Meg. Little by little, I'm getting stronger.

**中文**

那我要告诉你坏消息了，墨纪。我正在一点一点地变强噢。

## `ZagreusField_0510`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I may be slow to learn, but... least I'm rather patient!

**中文**

尽管我是愚钝了，但……至少我有耐心！

## `ZagreusField_0511`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's not a matter of want. The way out is there in that direction, past the river of flame.

**中文**

这不是想不想的问题。出去的路就在那个方向，渡过地狱之河。

## `ZagreusField_0512`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I've hope enough. Not that it's any of your business anymore.

**中文**

我当然有把握。然而这与你无关。

## `ZagreusField_0513`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'll get around to that in due time. They're family. They'll understand no doubt.

**中文**

这个我到时候自然会解释。我们都是一家人。他们一定会理解我的。

## `ZagreusField_0514`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I left when it was necessary, Than. I thought of you and hoped you'd understand. I have to do this.

**中文**

我是因为有必要才离开的，塔纳。我的确想到了你，但我希望你能理解。我必须这样做。

## `ZagreusField_0515`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh they won't fear me once they get to know me.

**中文**

噢，如果他们了解我的话，就不会怕我了。

## `ZagreusField_0516`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I don't know what I'm supposed to say when he gets like this...

**中文**

他这样的时候，我真不知道该说些什么……

## `ZagreusField_0517`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thanks for the vote of confidence there, Than.

**中文**

谢谢你的鼓励，塔纳。

## `ZagreusField_0518`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Than. Home is never going to be the same for me. There's no use trying to pretend. I have to go.

**中文**

…塔纳。这个家再不能回到从前了。再怎么掩饰都没用。我得走了。

## `ZagreusField_0519`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm searching for my mother. You still live at home with yours. You don't know what it's like.

**中文**

在寻找我的母亲。你跟自己的母亲住在一块。你不会理解的。

## `ZagreusField_0520`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I told you I don't need your help here, Thanatos.

**中文**

我说了我真的不需要你帮我，塔纳托斯。

## `ZagreusField_0521`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Feeling's mutual, sir. Someday you'll have to tell me all about how you tricked Father. I might learn a thing or two.

**中文**

深有同感，先生。有时间你一定要告诉我你是怎么骗到我父亲的。我得跟你学学。

## `ZagreusField_0522`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, you know, just out on a little stroll is all. Do me a favor, though, you see the Fury Sisters, let them know I was headed down the way I came from, will you...?

**中文**

噢，没什么，就是出来逛两圈。不过，请你帮个忙，如果你见到复仇女神三姐妹，告诉她们我打道回府了，好吗…？

## `ZagreusField_0523`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't think she'll be out of commission for very long. She'd best find you minding that boulder, though at least you managed to catch a break for now. Be seeing you, Sisyphus.

**中文**

我觉得她应该很快就会回来的。她来的时候你最好在推石头，不过现在你可以稍微休息一下。回头见，西西弗斯。

## `ZagreusField_0524`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You mean Megaera. She's on my case all right. Under strict orders not to let me out of Tartarus. I guess that means you've got yourself a break! About damn time they gave one to you, sir.

**中文**

你指墨纪拉。她在处理我的事呢。严令禁止我离开塔耳塔罗斯。这意味着你可以休息一下了！现在，也该是时候许你一个了，大人。

## `ZagreusField_0525`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I think I might have gotten lucky.

**中文**

我觉得只是运气好而已。

## `ZagreusField_0526`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You better believe it, sir. Now you take care, be seeing you around.

**中文**

你最好相信，先生。保重，下次再见。

## `ZagreusField_0527`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You deserve better, Sisyphus! This really isn't much, but maybe it'll help you while away the days. Nights. Whatever time it is.

**中文**

你配得上，西西弗斯！这真的没什么，但是应该可以帮你消磨时间。晚上也行。不论时间。

## `ZagreusField_0528`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're always a sight for sore eyes out here, Sisyphus. Thank you for always believing in me.

**中文**

你永远是一道靓丽的风景线，西西弗斯。谢谢你一直相信我。

## `ZagreusField_0529`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Built to ensure no one gets out. We'll see about that.

**中文**

保证没人跑得了。我们走着瞧。

## `ZagreusField_0530`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good thing I'm not dead.

**中文**

还好我没死。

## `ZagreusField_0531`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Too bad it can't just bring me to the surface.

**中文**

很遗憾，没办法回到地表。

## `ZagreusField_0532`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

And not a moment too soon.

**中文**

差一点就迟了。

## `ZagreusField_0533`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lovely.

**中文**

很可爱。

## `ZagreusField_0534`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Or until I crush their bones to dust.

**中文**

不过，等我把他们的骨头碾成碎片之后就更可爱了。

## `ZagreusField_0535`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Could you keep it down?

**中文**

你能小声点吗？

## `ZagreusField_0536`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hoh, what's this.

**中文**

嚯，这是什么。

## `ZagreusField_0537`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Already.

**中文**

已经。

## `ZagreusField_0538`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh good.

**中文**

哦，好。

## `ZagreusField_0539`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Let's see, how about a swift and painful death, would that be possible?

**中文**

我想想，来个又快又疼的死法，怎么样？

## `ZagreusField_0540`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'll never get out of here if not for their help, but... I can't let them know about Mother. She wants to remain hidden from them.

**中文**

如果没有他们的帮助，我永远都无法离开这个地方。但……我不能让他们知道母亲的事情，她不想在他们面前露面。

## `ZagreusField_0541`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

She isn't my birthmother, no, but she is like a mother to me. Do you know her?

**中文**

她并不是我的亲生母亲，对，但她也像我的母亲一样对待我。她您认识她吗？

## `ZagreusField_0542`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Master Chaos? The goddess Nyx, she... seems to want to speak with you again. I wonder, would you maybe contact her sometime? She seems convinced it is your move to make if you so choose.

**中文**

嗯，卡俄斯君主？女神倪克斯，她……好像想和你再谈一次。我想，你能不能找个时间和她联系一下？她似乎确信，如果你愿意的话，你可以自己决定。

## `ZagreusField_0543`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Say, Charon, everything all right with the old boat lately? I've not seen it around, you're just... here on your own. How did you get here, anyway?

**中文**

卡戎，你的那只旧船最近还好吧？我在附近也没看到它，就...你自己在这边。话说回来，你是怎么到这儿来的？

## `ZagreusField_0544`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I see, I see, makes sense, I'm glad I asked, there, mate.

**中文**

明白，这样就讲得通了。我很庆幸自己问了，朋友。

## `ZagreusField_0545`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whoops.

**中文**

哎呀。

## `ZagreusField_0546`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm in for it.

**中文**

算上我。

## `ZagreusField_0547`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Found me, huh?

**中文**

发现我了？

## `ZagreusField_0548`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got their attention.

**中文**

吸引到他们的注意了。

## `ZagreusField_0549`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

OK.

**中文**

很好。

## `ZagreusField_0550`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Father.

**中文**

父亲。

## `ZagreusField_0551`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Father...!

**中文**

父亲...！

## `ZagreusField_0552`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Made them mad.

**中文**

把他们逼疯了。

## `ZagreusField_0553`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Almost...!

**中文**

几乎...！

## `ZagreusField_0554`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Just a bit longer...!

**中文**

再久一点点...！

## `ZagreusField_0555`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

So close...!

**中文**

就差一点...！

## `ZagreusField_0556`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nearly made it...!

**中文**

差点就成功了...！

## `ZagreusField_0557`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Here goes nothing.

**中文**

没什么大不了的。

## `ZagreusField_0558`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Away we go.

**中文**

我们走。

## `ZagreusField_0559`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Moving right along.

**中文**

我们不能停下来。

## `ZagreusField_0560`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm well past wondering.

**中文**

我早已不再疑惑。

## `ZagreusField_0561`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

At last I know the gods more than by name.

**中文**

最后，我知道了众神不只是空洞的名字而已。

## `ZagreusField_0562`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Blood and darkness, well indeed.

**中文**

血与黑暗，毫无疑问。

## `ZagreusField_0563`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This one's for you, Achilles.

**中文**

这是给你的，阿喀琉斯。

## `ZagreusField_0564`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's what you think.

**中文**

这是你的想法。

## `ZagreusField_0565`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

At least Zeus is helping me.

**中文**

至少宙斯在帮助我。

## `ZagreusField_0566`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll find my place all right.

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_0722`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Need all the firepower I can get.

**中文**

尽量拿到更多武器。

## `ZagreusField_0723`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That should do it.

**中文**

应该可以了。

## `ZagreusField_0724`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks, Daedalus.

**中文**

谢谢，狄德勒斯。

## `ZagreusField_0725`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There, that's it.

**中文**

就是它了。

## `ZagreusField_0726`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Done and done.

**中文**

成交。

## `ZagreusField_0727`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A Pom of Power...

**中文**

力量石榴...

## `ZagreusField_0728`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

They must have lots of these upon Olympus.

**中文**

奥林匹斯山上肯定有很多。

## `ZagreusField_0729`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

An Ambrosia bottle...

**中文**

仙酒瓶子...

## `ZagreusField_0730`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Someone back home would practically die for this stuff.

**中文**

在我来的地方，有人愿意为了这东西而去死。

## `ZagreusField_0731`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's a Skeleton Key...

**中文**

这是一把万能钥匙...

## `ZagreusField_0732`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Gathered Darkness... I could use that.

**中文**

浓缩了黑暗的精髓...我可以用到它。

## `ZagreusField_0733`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Underworld's power.

**中文**

冥界的力量。

## `ZagreusField_0734`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey, it's food!

**中文**

嘿，是吃的！

## `ZagreusField_0735`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Five-second rule. That was five seconds right?

**中文**

五秒定律。是五秒没错吧？

## `ZagreusField_0736`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A Titan Heart...

**中文**

泰坦之心...

## `ZagreusField_0737`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That ought to keep me going for a bit.

**中文**

它应该能让我再坚持一会儿。

## `ZagreusField_0738`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Coin from the dead...

**中文**

死者的硬币...

## `ZagreusField_0739`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I bet Charon's going to want all this.

**中文**

我敢打赌，卡戎会想要所有这些东西。

## `ZagreusField_0740`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Why good to see you, Charon mate! Just minding my own business, taking in the sights, and, sure is awfully convenient to be running into you like this! I'd all this coin I wanted to get rid of.

**中文**

见到你真好，卡戎老伙计！我正做我自己的事呢，也看看风景什么的，就这样碰见你可真是太好了啊！我正想把身上这些钱币给花了呢。

## `ZagreusField_0741`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll take that as a no.

**中文**

我就把它当做否定的答案了。

## `ZagreusField_0742`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You must have a lot of stops around these parts, don't you, Charon mate. Most of the dead are here in Asphodel, correct?

**中文**

你一定停靠了很多次吧，卡戎老伙计。很多死人都在水仙花平原了，对不对？

## `ZagreusField_0743`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, I appreciate your time.

**中文**

好了，谢谢你花了这么多时间。

## `ZagreusField_0744`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Charon, I'm in Asphodel now, aren't I? I'd always heard this was some flower-covered meadow type place, not such a... terrifying hellscape.

**中文**

嗨，卡戎，我是在水仙花平原吧？我印象里，这里应该是那种遍地鲜花的草场才对，不是这种……可怕的地狱景象。

## `ZagreusField_0745`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, thanks anyway.

**中文**

无论如何，还是要谢谢你。

## `ZagreusField_0746`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Charon. If there happens to be something really horrible awaiting me beyond that door there, then... give me a sign, won't you?

**中文**

喂，卡戎。如果那扇门后面有什么非常恐怖的东西等着我，你…你给我点提示好吗？

## `ZagreusField_0747`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Great.

**中文**

很好。

## `ZagreusField_0748`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Care to provide me any insight as to just what sort of hideous monstrosity awaits me over on the next infernal island, Charon mate?

**中文**

能告诉我下一个可怕的岛上有什么恐怖的怪物在等着我吗，卡戎老伙计？

## `ZagreusField_0749`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That bad, huh.

**中文**

很糟糕，对吧？

## `ZagreusField_0750`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Couldn't agree more, mate.

**中文**

举双手赞成，伙计。

## `ZagreusField_0751`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hello to you as well, mate!

**中文**

你也好呀，伙计！

## `ZagreusField_0752`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll just have a look around.

**中文**

我只是四处转转。

## `ZagreusField_0753`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Fine day or night to you as well, Charon.

**中文**

也祝你早安、午安或晚安，卡戎。

## `ZagreusField_0754`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You're absolutely right, there, mate.

**中文**

完全正确，伙计。

## `ZagreusField_0755`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Truer words have not been spoken, mate.

**中文**

还没有说真话，伙计。

## `ZagreusField_0756`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Couldn't have said it better myself.

**中文**

我完全同意。

## `ZagreusField_0757`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh I'm just fine thank you for asking, mate.

**中文**

哦，我很好，谢谢你的关心，伙计。

## `ZagreusField_0758`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's good to see you too, mate.

**中文**

我也很高兴见到你，伙计。

## `ZagreusField_0759`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Always a pleasure, Charon.

**中文**

我的荣幸，卡戎。

## `ZagreusField_0760`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What's this doing all the way out here?

**中文**

它在这里做什么？

## `ZagreusField_0761`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Is that a sight for sore eyes.

**中文**

这是赏心悦目的场面吗？

## `ZagreusField_0762`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What's that thing doing here?

**中文**

那玩意儿在这里做什么？

## `ZagreusField_0763`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How'd that get here?

**中文**

它是怎么到这里来的？

## `ZagreusField_0764`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Looks clear.

**中文**

看起来很安全。

## `ZagreusField_0765`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Anyone there...?

**中文**

有人在那边吗...？

## `ZagreusField_0766`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A fountain chamber.

**中文**

是泉水房间。

## `ZagreusField_0767`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Fates are being kind I guess.

**中文**

我猜，命运之神是仁慈的。

## `ZagreusField_0768`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Guess I get a break.

**中文**

貌似我要休息一会儿了。

## `ZagreusField_0769`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A welcome sight.

**中文**

赏心悦目的景象。

## `ZagreusField_0770`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Clear for now.

**中文**

现在很清楚。

## `ZagreusField_0771`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whew.

**中文**

哟。

## `ZagreusField_0772`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There she is.

**中文**

她来了。

## `ZagreusField_0773`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's her.

**中文**

是她。

## `ZagreusField_0774`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's got to be her. Then, here goes nothing... Ahem. In the name of Hades! Olympus, I accept this message.

**中文**

一定是她。嗯，就不管那么多了……啊哼。以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山，我收到了这条消息。

## `ZagreusField_0775`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thunder. Is that...?

**中文**

雷神。那是...？

## `ZagreusField_0776`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Is this really him? OK. In the name of Hades! Olympus! I accept this message.

**中文**

这真的是他吗？好吧。以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山！我收到了这条消息。

## `ZagreusField_0777`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lord Uncle Zeus lending his support... I never thought I'd see the day. Or night. Whenever.

**中文**

众神之王宙斯叔叔伸出了援手...我从没想过会有这么一天。或晚上。从没想过。

## `ZagreusField_0778`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Crashing waves... a trident...

**中文**

汹涌的海浪...三叉戟...

## `ZagreusField_0779`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's uncle number two, it's got to be. Then, in the name of Hades! Olympus, I accept this message.

**中文**

一定是二叔。然后，以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山，我收到了这条消息。

## `ZagreusField_0780`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Looks like I caught a big one! The power of the seas should be of help.

**中文**

我好像找到个大靠山！海神的力量应该能帮上忙。

## `ZagreusField_0781`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, it's her.

**中文**

哦，是她。

## `ZagreusField_0782`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I must be a mess, let's see here. Hi there! In the name of Hades! Olympus, I accept this message.

**中文**

我一定是一团糟，我们来看看这里。你好！以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山，我收到了这条消息。

## `ZagreusField_0783`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Interested? In the power to break hearts? Sure, I don't see why not.

**中文**

有兴趣？有能力让人心碎？当然，为什么不呢。

## `ZagreusField_0784`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Yes, yes I am. Let's break some hearts.

**中文**

是的，我有兴趣。我们来让一些人心碎吧。

## `ZagreusField_0785`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A divine arrow...

**中文**

神圣之箭...

## `ZagreusField_0786`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

There's only one heavenly archer I know of. Well, several, really. Anyway! In the name of Hades! Olympus? I accept this message.

**中文**

据我所知，天界只有一位弓箭手。好吧，其实有好几位！以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山？我收到了这条消息。

## `ZagreusField_0787`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I sure could use some company now that she mentions it. The power of the hunt sounds handy too!

**中文**

既然她这么说了，我当然需要个伴。狩猎女神的力量听起来也很不错！

## `ZagreusField_0788`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The cries of battle...

**中文**

战吼...

## `ZagreusField_0789`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This one was bound to show up I guess. In the name of Hades! Olympus! I accept this message.

**中文**

我猜这一天一定会出现。以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山！我收到了这条消息。

## `ZagreusField_0790`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

With your power, Lord Ares, maybe I'll get out of here yet.

**中文**

借助你的力量，战神阿瑞斯，我或许可以离开这里。

## `ZagreusField_0791`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That sound...

**中文**

这声音...

## `ZagreusField_0792`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That was... revelry? People having a good time? Well, sure, then! In the name of Hades! Olympus, I'll accept this message.

**中文**

那是……狂欢？人们玩得很开心。好吧，当然，那么：以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山，我收到了这条消息。

## `ZagreusField_0793`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You know, that sounds fantastic right about now. Pleasure to make your acquaintance, Dionysus mate.

**中文**

这声音现在听起来多么美妙。很高兴能认识你，迪奥尼索司，我的朋友。

## `ZagreusField_0794`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Athena's power...

**中文**

雅典娜的力量...

## `ZagreusField_0795`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The shield of light.

**中文**

光明之盾。

## `ZagreusField_0796`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The wisdom of Athena.

**中文**

雅典娜的智慧。

## `ZagreusField_0797`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Divine protection.

**中文**

神圣保护。

## `ZagreusField_0798`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The might of Athena.

**中文**

雅典娜的力量。

## `ZagreusField_0799`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Strength of the goddess.

**中文**

女神的力量。

## `ZagreusField_0800`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lord Zeus' strength.

**中文**

众神之王宙斯的力量。

## `ZagreusField_0801`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The thunder-god's might.

**中文**

雷神的力量。

## `ZagreusField_0802`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thunder and lightning.

**中文**

雷鸣电闪。

## `ZagreusField_0803`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thunder time.

**中文**

雷神登场。

## `ZagreusField_0804`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I feel all tingly.

**中文**

我浑身都有触电的感觉。

## `ZagreusField_0805`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Is my hair sticking up?

**中文**

我的头发竖起来了吗？

## `ZagreusField_0806`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's make some waves, right, Uncle?

**中文**

我们来掀起些海浪吧，叔叔？

## `ZagreusField_0807`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's make some waves.

**中文**

我们来掀起些海浪。

## `ZagreusField_0808`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The power of the sea.

**中文**

海洋的力量。

## `ZagreusField_0809`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The ocean's fury.

**中文**

海洋的愤怒。

## `ZagreusField_0810`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The sea-god's might.

**中文**

海神的力量。

## `ZagreusField_0811`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's high tide.

**中文**

惊涛骇浪。

## `ZagreusField_0812`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Love and loss.

**中文**

爱与失去。

## `ZagreusField_0813`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Strength and beauty.

**中文**

力量与美。

## `ZagreusField_0814`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Heartbreak time.

**中文**

心碎时刻。

## `ZagreusField_0815`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No love without pain.

**中文**

爱与痛相随。

## `ZagreusField_0816`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's break some hearts.

**中文**

我们来让几颗心破碎吧。

## `ZagreusField_0817`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I feel fabulous.

**中文**

我感觉好极了。

## `ZagreusField_0818`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The might of the huntress.

**中文**

女猎手的力量。

## `ZagreusField_0819`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to hunt.

**中文**

狩猎时间到。

## `ZagreusField_0820`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My senses sharpen.

**中文**

我的感觉更加敏锐。

## `ZagreusField_0821`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The dead had better hide.

**中文**

死者最好回避。

## `ZagreusField_0822`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Artemis guide my hand.

**中文**

阿尔忒弥斯指引着我。

## `ZagreusField_0823`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll make every strike count.

**中文**

我会百发百中。

## `ZagreusField_0824`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The war-god's power.

**中文**

战胜的力量。

## `ZagreusField_0825`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The will to fight.

**中文**

战斗的意志。

## `ZagreusField_0826`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I will keep fighting.

**中文**

我会一直战斗下去。

## `ZagreusField_0827`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Violence...

**中文**

暴力...

## `ZagreusField_0828`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's me or them.

## `ZagreusField_0829`

**EN**

This means war.

## `ZagreusField_0830`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

To my health.

为我的健康。

## `ZagreusField_0831`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's liven things up.

**中文**

让气氛活跃起来。

## `ZagreusField_0832`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to feast.

**中文**

现在是宴会时间。

## `ZagreusField_0833`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cheers to that.

为此干杯。

**EN**

Strong stuff.

**EN**

Wretches!

More Wretches?

Louts!

## `ZagreusField_0868`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's you.

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, no.

**中文**

哦，不。

## `ZagreusField_0870`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, this is awkward.

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_0871`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Should be useful.

**中文**

应该有用。

## `ZagreusField_0872`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll just take this one.

**中文**

我就拿走这件。

## `ZagreusField_0873`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Coin goes in, that comes out.

**中文**

一手交钱，一手交货。

## `ZagreusField_0874`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll pay for that.

**中文**

我会付钱的。

## `ZagreusField_0875`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Should help keep me going.

**中文**

应该能让我坚持下去。

## `ZagreusField_0876`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Payment in full.

**中文**

全额付清。

## `ZagreusField_0877`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll take this one.

**中文**

我要拿走这件。

## `ZagreusField_0878`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sold out.

**中文**

卖光了。

## `ZagreusField_0879`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Out of stock.

**中文**

没有现货。

## `ZagreusField_0880`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

They're fresh out.

**中文**

刚刚售罄。

## `ZagreusField_0881`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bought them out.

**中文**

全部买光。

## `ZagreusField_0882`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nothing left.

**中文**

什么都没剩下。

## `ZagreusField_0883`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bought it all up.

**中文**

全部买下来。

## `ZagreusField_0884`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't mind me, Charon.

**中文**

不要介意，卡戎。

## `ZagreusField_0885`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Just tidying up a bit.

**中文**

只是稍微整理了一下。

## `ZagreusField_0886`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Must have slipped.

**中文**

肯定忘记了什么。

## `ZagreusField_0887`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, Father? I'm trespassing through your secret hall, and going up.

**中文**

对了，父亲、我擅自闯进了秘殿，而且会一路往上走。

## `ZagreusField_0888`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't mind me, Father.

**中文**

别管我，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_0889`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll just go on up.

**中文**

我会继续向上走。

## `ZagreusField_0890`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Going up.

**中文**

向上走。

## `ZagreusField_0891`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll just be a moment, Father.

**中文**

我马上就好了，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_0892`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's get out of here.

**中文**

我们离开这里吧。

## `ZagreusField_0893`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

On to the next one.

**中文**

继续前往下一个地方。

## `ZagreusField_0894`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All aboard.

**中文**

所有人都上船。

## `ZagreusField_0895`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Onward.

**中文**

前进。

## `ZagreusField_0896`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Course is set.

**中文**

航线已设定妥当。

## `ZagreusField_0897`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Get me out of here.

**中文**

带我离开这里。

## `ZagreusField_0898`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Next island.

**中文**

下一座岛屿。

## `ZagreusField_0899`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hi, Charon.

**中文**

嗨，卡戎。

## `ZagreusField_0900`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Greetings, Charon.

**中文**

好呀，卡戎。

## `ZagreusField_0901`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's Charon.

**中文**

是卡戎。

## `ZagreusField_0902`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good old Charon.

**中文**

还是那个善良的卡戎。

## `ZagreusField_0903`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Charon must be nearby.

**中文**

卡戎肯定就在附近。

## `ZagreusField_0904`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm at the docks.

**中文**

我在码头上。

## `ZagreusField_0905`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's see what's up for sale.

**中文**

我们看看这里都卖什么东西。

## `ZagreusField_0906`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

They got me. No way to patch up. Got to keep going.

**中文**

他们把我打倒了。没办法补救。必须继续前进。

## `ZagreusField_0907`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm hurt. Can't heal myself. But I can fight.

**中文**

我受伤了。无法为自己治疗。但我还可以战斗。

## `ZagreusField_0908`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nngh, what I wouldn't give for, say, a healing flask of some sort.

**中文**

嗯，我不会拿出来交换的东西，比如说治疗瓶之类的。

## `ZagreusField_0909`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wonder where I can use this.

**中文**

好奇我什么时候可以用上这个。

## `ZagreusField_0910`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Need more of those keys.

**中文**

需要更多这样的钥匙。

## `ZagreusField_0911`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This needs more keys to open.

**中文**

需要更多钥匙才能打开它。

## `ZagreusField_0912`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't have enough keys.

**中文**

钥匙不够。

## `ZagreusField_0913`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's going to take more keys.

**中文**

它需要更多钥匙。

## `ZagreusField_0914`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Need more keys.

**中文**

需要更多钥匙。

## `ZagreusField_0915`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

One for the stash.

**中文**

一把要藏起来。

## `ZagreusField_0916`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's one more.

**中文**

又有一把。

## `ZagreusField_0917`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How many have I got now?

**中文**

现在我有多少把了？

## `ZagreusField_0918`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Another key.

**中文**

有一把钥匙。

## `ZagreusField_0919`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh I'll get her for the both of us one of these days. Nights? Whenever.

**中文**

不光是替你，我也要替我自己这样做，总有一天。晚上呢？都行。

## `ZagreusField_0920`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What's my collection doing here?

**中文**

我的收藏品怎么会在这里？

## `ZagreusField_0921`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey what's that doing here?

**中文**

嗨，这东西怎么会出现在这里？

## `ZagreusField_0922`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My keepsakes...

**中文**

我的纪念品...

## `ZagreusField_0923`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Won't bother asking how come all my keepsakes are here.

**中文**

懒得费工夫问我的全部纪念品为什么会在这里。

## `ZagreusField_0924`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey it's my keepsakes.

**中文**

嘿，是我的纪念品。

## `ZagreusField_0925`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Maybe I should switch things up.

**中文**

也许我该想办法解决这个问题。

## `ZagreusField_0926`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tartarus... I won't be stuck here all my life.

**中文**

塔耳塔罗斯...我不会一辈子都被困在这里的。

## `ZagreusField_0927`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Just need to get from here across all that.

**中文**

只是需要从这里一路横跨过去。

## `ZagreusField_0928`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I've gone farther than I can see from here.

**中文**

我走过的路，比我站在这里目力所及的地方更远。

## `ZagreusField_0929`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The chambers of Tartarus have probably all rearranged themselves by now.

**中文**

塔耳塔罗斯的密室很有可能已经全部变换了出口。

## `ZagreusField_0930`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm going to get through all this.

**中文**

我要穿过所有这些。

## `ZagreusField_0931`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

And I can reckon quite a bit.

**中文**

而且我可以好好地思量思量。

## `ZagreusField_0932`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Way to spoil the surprise, old man.

**中文**

你剧透了，老头。

## `ZagreusField_0933`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I do enjoy dead people's fineries.

**中文**

我确实喜欢死人的衣服。

## `ZagreusField_0934`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You feeling lucky, too, old man? Then show yourself! No...?

**中文**

你也感觉很幸运，对吗老头？那就现身吧！不要...？

## `ZagreusField_0935`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lots of wretchedness to go around, I guess.

**中文**

我想，会散播很多不幸。

## `ZagreusField_0936`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm only flame-resistant not flame-proof.

**中文**

我只是耐火烧，又不能防火。

## `ZagreusField_0937`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It wasn't all that hard to find really.

**中文**

其实并没有那么难找。

## `ZagreusField_0938`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'd best take all the evidence I find.

**中文**

我最好带上所有找到的证据。

## `ZagreusField_0939`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A Centaur Heart...

**中文**

半人马之心...

## `ZagreusField_0940`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Strength of the Centaurs...

**中文**

半人马的力量...

## `ZagreusField_0941`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's a lot of coin.

**中文**

好多硬币。

## `ZagreusField_0942`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Look at all that coin.

**中文**

看看这些硬币。

## `ZagreusField_0943`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey, money!

**中文**

嘿，钱！

## `ZagreusField_0944`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lucky find!

**中文**

幸运的发现！

## `ZagreusField_0945`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hello, coin!

**中文**

你好，硬币！

## `ZagreusField_0946`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lots of coin there.

**中文**

这里有好多硬币。

## `ZagreusField_0947`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How'd that happen?

**中文**

这是怎么发生的？

## `ZagreusField_0948`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Fixed that for you.

**中文**

帮你把事情解决了。

## `ZagreusField_0949`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How careless of me.

**中文**

我真的太粗心了。

## `ZagreusField_0950`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Who keeps fixing that?

**中文**

是谁一直在解决问题？

## `ZagreusField_0951`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No you don't.

**中文**

不，你没有。

## `ZagreusField_0952`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not. Yet.

**中文**

不，还没有。

## `ZagreusField_0953`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We're not finished.

**中文**

我们还没结束呢。

## `ZagreusField_0954`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Never.

**中文**

从来没有。

## `ZagreusField_0955`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hrrrrr!!

**中文**

呃！！

## `ZagreusField_0956`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not so fast!

**中文**

别这么快！

## `ZagreusField_0957`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I don't think so.

**中文**

我不这么认为。

## `ZagreusField_0958`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Damn it.

**中文**

该死。

## `ZagreusField_0959`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Here we go again.

**中文**

我们再来一次。

## `ZagreusField_0960`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Blood and darkness...

**中文**

血与黑暗...

## `ZagreusField_0961`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What is it now.

**中文**

又怎么了。

## `ZagreusField_0962`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, no.

**中文**

哦，不。

## `ZagreusField_0963`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, gods.

**中文**

哦，天。

## `ZagreusField_0964`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What...

**中文**

什么…

## `ZagreusField_0965`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Still alive.

**中文**

还活着。

## `ZagreusField_0966`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Have to keep going.

**中文**

必须坚持下去。

## `ZagreusField_0967`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not dead yet.

**中文**

还没死呢。

## `ZagreusField_0968`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

One more chamber?

**中文**

再坚持一个房间？

## `ZagreusField_0969`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not finished yet.

**中文**

还没结束。

## `ZagreusField_0970`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I can still fight.

**中文**

我还可以战斗。

## `ZagreusField_0971`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Here goes nothing.

**中文**

没什么大不了的。

## `ZagreusField_0972`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got to keep pushing.

**中文**

必须继续努力。

## `ZagreusField_0973`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Can't give up yet.

**中文**

还不能放弃。

## `ZagreusField_0974`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I can do this.

**中文**

我可以做到。

## `ZagreusField_0975`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got to focus.

**中文**

保持专注。

## `ZagreusField_0976`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's for last time.

**中文**

这是最后一次。

## `ZagreusField_0977`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That was for last time.

**中文**

已经是最后一次了。

## `ZagreusField_0978`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I always come back.

**中文**

我会一直回来的。

## `ZagreusField_0979`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's what you get.

**中文**

这就是你的本事？

## `ZagreusField_0980`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Payback.

**中文**

报仇。

## `ZagreusField_0981`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got you back.

**中文**

吃我这一记。

## `ZagreusField_0982`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You kill me, I kill you.

**中文**

你杀我，我杀你。

## `ZagreusField_0983`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's call it even.

**中文**

算是扯平了。

## `ZagreusField_0984`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

In the name of Hades!

**中文**

以哈迪斯之名！

## `ZagreusField_0985`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

In the name of Hades.

**中文**

以哈迪斯之名。

## `ZagreusField_0986`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

In the name of Hades...!

**中文**

以哈迪斯之名...！

## `ZagreusField_0987`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Olympus! I accept this message!

**中文**

奥林匹斯山！我收到了这条消息！

## `ZagreusField_0988`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Olympus, I accept this message.

**中文**

奥林匹斯山，我收到了这条消息。

## `ZagreusField_0989`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Olympus? I accept this message...!

**中文**

奥林匹斯山？我收到了这条消息...

## `ZagreusField_0990`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wretches of the damned, beware. I'm coming back.

**中文**

该死的暗灵们，小心点。我会回来的。

## `ZagreusField_0991`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tartarus... strong enough to seal away the Titans. But not me.

**中文**

塔耳塔罗斯...拥有封印泰坦的力量。但对我没用。

## `ZagreusField_0992`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Fine day or whenever to get me out of here.

**中文**

带我离开这里，不管什么时候都行。

## `ZagreusField_0993`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I won't back down. Not now or ever.

**中文**

我不会再回到这里。永远不会。

## `ZagreusField_0994`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I know you're out there, Mother.

**中文**

我知道你在那里，母亲。

## `ZagreusField_0995`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Heads up.

**中文**

小心。

## `ZagreusField_0996`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's what you get.

**中文**

这就是你的本事？

## `ZagreusField_0997`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You're nothing.

**中文**

弹药不足...！

**EN**

Chamber's empty.

密室是空的。

Chamber's empty...!

**中文**

密室是空的...！

I'm empty.

用光了。

## `ZagreusField_1307`

**EN**

I'm empty...!

**中文**

用光了...！

**EN**

Nice.

**中文**

不错。

## `ZagreusField_1309`

**EN**

Heh.

**中文**

嘿。

## `ZagreusField_1310`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hm.

**中文**

嗯。

## `ZagreusField_1311`

**EN**

Hm!

**中文**

嗯！

## `ZagreusField_1312`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ready.

**中文**

准备好了。

## `ZagreusField_1313`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ready!

**中文**

准备好了！

## `ZagreusField_1314`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

OK!

**中文**

好了！

## `ZagreusField_1315`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Set.

**中文**

准备就绪。

## `ZagreusField_1316`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Right there.

**中文**

就在那里。

## `ZagreusField_1317`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Rearmed.

**中文**

重填完毕。

## `ZagreusField_1318`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Yes.

**中文**

太好了。

## `ZagreusField_1319`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There we go.

**中文**

我们走。

## `ZagreusField_1320`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good.

**中文**

不错。

## `ZagreusField_1321`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Loaded up.

**中文**

装填好了。

## `ZagreusField_1322`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

More target practice.

**中文**

更多射击练习。

## `ZagreusField_1323`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's fire it up.

**中文**

我们开始吧。

## `ZagreusField_1324`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Multiple foes sighted.

**中文**

发现多个敌人。

## `ZagreusField_1325`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Exagryph, prepare to fire.

**中文**

伊格瑞菲，准备开炮。

## `ZagreusField_1326`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Some modifications to the rail are in order.

**中文**

对坚炮做了些改进。

## `ZagreusField_1327`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's see how we can modify the rail.

**中文**

我们来看看怎么改进坚炮。

## `ZagreusField_1328`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The rail's hidden power.

**中文**

坚炮的隐藏力量。

## `ZagreusField_1329`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My weapon's hidden talents...

**中文**

我的武器的潜力...

## `ZagreusField_1330`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Stygius grows stronger.

**中文**

斯第格思变得更强了。

## `ZagreusField_1331`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A blade fit for a god.

**中文**

一把与神的身份相符的刀刃。

## `ZagreusField_1332`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Coronacht grows stronger.

**中文**

科罗纳赫特变得更强了。

## `ZagreusField_1333`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The deadliest bow of all.

**中文**

它是最致命的弓。

## `ZagreusField_1334`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Varatha grows stronger.

**中文**

瓦拉塔变得更强了。

## `ZagreusField_1335`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

They'll fear me with this spear.

**中文**

他们会因为这支矛而对我心怀恐惧。

## `ZagreusField_1336`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Aegis grows stronger.

**中文**

埃癸斯变得更强了。

## `ZagreusField_1337`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Never been a finer shield.

**中文**

从没有像现在这么好过。

## `ZagreusField_1338`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Exagryph grows stronger.

**中文**

伊格瑞菲变得更强了。

## `ZagreusField_1339`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Beware the adamant rail.

**中文**

小心这门狮鹫坚炮。

## `ZagreusField_1340`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I need a higher Pain Level with the Pact of Punishment to go in this.

**中文**

我需要提高惩罚契约中的痛苦等级才能这样做。

## `ZagreusField_1341`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I need to power up the Pact of Punishment back home to go in this.

**中文**

我需要对在家订的惩罚契约做些升级才能这样做。

## `ZagreusField_1342`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My Pain Level isn't high enough.

**中文**

我的痛苦等级不够高。

## `ZagreusField_1343`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My Pain Level's too low.

**中文**

我的痛苦等级太低了。

## `ZagreusField_1344`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I need a higher Pain Level to go in.

**中文**

我需要提高痛苦等级才能这样做。

## `ZagreusField_1345`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My Pain Level's too low to get in.

**中文**

无法参与其中，我的痛苦等级太低了。

## `ZagreusField_1346`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Need a higher Pain Level to go in there.

**中文**

需要更高的痛苦等级才能进入。

## `ZagreusField_1347`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not getting in without a higher Pain Level.

**中文**

因为痛苦等级太低而不能进入。

## `ZagreusField_1348`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm not authorized.

**中文**

我不符合条件。

## `ZagreusField_1349`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not letting me in.

**中文**

把我排除在外了。

## `ZagreusField_1350`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I don't have access.

**中文**

我没有资格。

## `ZagreusField_1351`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I can't get in.

**中文**

我不能进入。

## `ZagreusField_1352`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not authorized to go in there.

**中文**

不符合进入的条件。

## `ZagreusField_1353`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Pact back home's the key to getting into this.

**中文**

在家订的契约是参与其中的关键。

## `ZagreusField_1354`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well here goes nothing.

**中文**

好了，这里什么都没有。

## `ZagreusField_1355`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Punishment has its perks.

**中文**

惩罚也有它的好处。

## `ZagreusField_1356`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Exclusive access.

**中文**

专属体验。

## `ZagreusField_1357`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time for more pain.

**中文**

现在来体验更多痛苦。

## `ZagreusField_1358`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Erebus, here I come.

**中文**

厄瑞玻斯，我来了。

## `ZagreusField_1359`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm in.

**中文**

我进来了。

## `ZagreusField_1360`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm authorized.

**中文**

我得到授权了。

## `ZagreusField_1361`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let me in.

**中文**

让我进去。

## `ZagreusField_1362`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's go make Father mad.

**中文**

我们把父亲逼疯吧。

## `ZagreusField_1363`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Erebus...

**中文**

厄瑞玻斯...

## `ZagreusField_1364`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I bear the Cosmic Egg.

**中文**

我带着宇宙之蛋。

## `ZagreusField_1365`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's go meet Master Chaos.

**中文**

去见卡俄斯君主吧。

## `ZagreusField_1366`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's check on Master Chaos.

**中文**

探望一下卡俄斯君主吧。

## `ZagreusField_1367`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Can't hurt me.

**中文**

伤不到我。

## `ZagreusField_1368`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My keepsake from Chaos will protect me.

**中文**

卡俄斯给我的纪念物会保护我。

## `ZagreusField_1369`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's pay Chaos a visit then.

**中文**

去拜访卡俄斯吧。

## `ZagreusField_1370`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Miss me, Meg?

**中文**

想我了吗，墨纪？

## `ZagreusField_1371`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What's new, Meg.

**中文**

有什么新鲜事吗，墨纪。

## `ZagreusField_1372`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hello.

**中文**

好呀。

## `ZagreusField_1373`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hi there.

**中文**

嗨。

## `ZagreusField_1374`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Shall we?

**中文**

我们可以吗？

## `ZagreusField_1375`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sorry I'm late.

**中文**

抱歉，我迟到了。

## `ZagreusField_1376`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got here as fast as I could.

**中文**

以最快的速度到这里来了。

## `ZagreusField_1377`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Megaera.

**中文**

墨纪拉。

## `ZagreusField_1378`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Here we are.

**中文**

我们又见面了。

## `ZagreusField_1379`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll get you for last time.

**中文**

这会是我最后一次见你。

## `ZagreusField_1380`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I see you grew your heads back.

**中文**

我发现你的头又长出来了。

## `ZagreusField_1381`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Remember me?

**中文**

记得我吗？

## `ZagreusField_1382`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bone Hydra! It's been too long.

**中文**

骸骨九头蛇！好久不见。

## `ZagreusField_1383`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I will keep coming back just like your stupid heads.

**中文**

我会不断回来，就像你那愚蠢的脑袋一样。

## `ZagreusField_1384`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't you have something to say to me?

**中文**

你没有什么话要对我说吗？

## `ZagreusField_1385`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I left them at home. And, I sort of know what I have, inasmuch as it's largely responsible for getting me this far. Would you like to see a demonstration?

**中文**

扔家里了。我知道自己拿着什么，有了这个我才能一路杀到这里来。要不要我演示下给你看看？

## `ZagreusField_1386`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Outer reaches of Asphodel. And I have to say, Meg, you are positively a sight compared with that blasted Bone Hydra stewing away up there.

**中文**

到了水仙花平原外围。说实话，墨纪，跟那个泡在水里骸骨九头蛇比起来，你可真是太美了。

## `ZagreusField_1387`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That Hydra was a total bastard, though. Bastards. But, you sound practically impressed with me, there, Meg! I feel so flattered!

**中文**

那个九头蛇真的太难缠了，简直是个混蛋。不过墨纪，你的语气听起来相当钦佩啊！啊，我飘了！

## `ZagreusField_1388`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This place can't stay sealed shut forever, Meg. In the meantime, I thought I'd get some practice in, you know?

**中文**

这个地方不可能永远一直封闭下去，墨纪。这段时间里，我觉得我能多练习练习，懂我意思吧？

## `ZagreusField_1389`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well if you're going to be so rude about it, maybe a little of each? Though, what do you even care which pacts I sign or don't?

**中文**

既然你都这么说了，可能两种原因都有吧。我签什么契约跟你也没什么关系吧？

## `ZagreusField_1390`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Now that you mention it, you can, Meg. I was wondering what was Father carrying on with you about back home?

**中文**

既然你都问了，当然有，墨纪。我想知道父亲在家里到底跟你说了什么。

## `ZagreusField_1391`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No hard feelings. I think she feels partly responsible for all of this.

**中文**

你别见怪啊。我认为她感觉自己要为所有这些事负责。

## `ZagreusField_1392`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We're both immortals, Meg. We'll be seeing plenty of each other for a while yet. And honestly I've come to welcome these occasions, yes.

**中文**

我们俩都不会死，墨纪。以后我们还要见很多次面呢。老实说，我还挺喜欢这样子的。

## `ZagreusField_1393`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Just because Chaos is giving me the power to crush you doesn't mean they're getting involved with this, does it?

**中文**

卡俄斯仅是给了我打败你的力量，不代表祂也涉足了吧？

## `ZagreusField_1394`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hit a few snags on my way to meet you, Meg. So, if you wouldn't mind going easy on me this one?

**中文**

为了来见你，在路上我摔了几跤，墨纪。所以，这次可以下手轻点吗？

## `ZagreusField_1395`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I figured I'd give you a head start this time, so I let the wretched shades bludgeon me repeatedly along the way.

**中文**

这次我为了让你能获得优势，来这里的路上一直主动被暗灵们围着打呢。

## `ZagreusField_1396`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You sound like Hypnos. He's getting to you, isn't he. Isn't he!

**中文**

你说话跟修普诺斯似的。他在接近你，是不是。是不是！

## `ZagreusField_1397`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have my reasons. Pitying you isn't one of them. Although I guess you don't believe me, do you?

**中文**

我有我的理由。但是同情你不包含在内。不过我猜你大概是不会相信我的，对吧？

## `ZagreusField_1398`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Now that's a rather ominous thing to say to someone, Meg. Care to explain in terms an idiot like me could understand?

**中文**

等下，你这是话里有话啊，墨纪。可以向我这个笨蛋解释一下吗？

## `ZagreusField_1399`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I can't imagine why not! We had a lovely time getting to know each other. We laughed, we cried! My only regret was that you couldn't be there, yourself.

**中文**

我真的想不清为什么如此！我们互相认识得真高兴，又笑又哭！我唯一的遗憾就是你自己无法到场。

## `ZagreusField_1400`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Is it wrong of me that I am somewhat honored to have garnered the attention of the Erinyes?

**中文**

我还以为受到厄里倪厄斯的关注是件很荣幸的事呢，看来是我错啦。

## `ZagreusField_1401`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I almost want to say you're very welcome. Have you and she always been on bad terms like this?

**中文**

你真的不用客气。你跟她的关系一直这样差吗？

## `ZagreusField_1402`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He's difficult to please, that much is true. What happened to Tisiphone, anyway? She isn't much for conversation.

**中文**

他本来就是个很难去讨好的人。提西福涅怎么样了？她没什么可说的。

## `ZagreusField_1403`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Three against one. Doesn't seem all that sporting, now, does it?

**中文**

三个打一个。这样是不是不太符合体育精神？

## `ZagreusField_1404`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You sure about this, Meg? What's with the change of heart?

**中文**

你确定要这样吗，墨纪？换心是怎么回事？

## `ZagreusField_1405`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm sure you'll have a chance to tell him back at home.

**中文**

回屋以后你会有机会告诉他的。

## `ZagreusField_1406`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You mistook him for someone who blindly follows orders without considering the implications.

**中文**

你以为的正直只不过是不计后果盲目服从命令而已。

## `ZagreusField_1407`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, you'll soon find that it's the real thing, Meg. And as for how I got it, well, I know a guy. Maybe I'll introduce you at some point?

**中文**

哦，你很快就会发现这是真的，墨纪。至于我是怎么做到的，是这样的，我认识个人。要不我介绍给你认识认识？

## `ZagreusField_1408`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You must be one of Megaera's lovely sisters, I don't believe we've had the pleasure. Waiting for what, exactly?

**中文**

你一定是墨纪拉的好姐妹之一吧，见到你很不荣幸。是在等什么呢？

## `ZagreusField_1409`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I get the feeling we're starting off on the wrong foot.

**中文**

初次见面就这样会不会给人不好的印象。

## `ZagreusField_1410`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You seem to have me all figured out. And here I thought we were still getting to know each other.

**中文**

你还挺了解我的。我还以为我们还在互相了解阶段呢。

## `ZagreusField_1411`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That... is not the type of flattery I'm used to. You're making me a bit uncomfortable, I'll have you know.

**中文**

你这个……我不太接受你这种撩法。你让我感觉有点不自在了，你知道吧。

## `ZagreusField_1412`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, I say I don't appreciate you calling me redblood. You're making me rather self-conscious about it, you know.

**中文**

要我说我不太喜欢“流红血的”这个称呼。这样会让我感觉不太舒服。

## `ZagreusField_1413`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, you mean to say Meg never told you? And here I thought you two were close.

**中文**

什么，难道墨纪从来没告诉过你吗？我还以为你俩走得挺近呢。

## `ZagreusField_1414`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

They say both gods and mortals are notoriously poor at estimating how long it takes to get anything done.

**中文**

人们说不管是神还是人，在预估事情完成时间的时候都做得非常差劲。

## `ZagreusField_1415`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, you planning to kill me with insults this time?

**中文**

干嘛，这回你想用嘴说死我吗？

## `ZagreusField_1416`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I can imagine that, it's just, I've really come to appreciate these little back-and-forth exchanges that we have.

**中文**

可以理解，不过，我还挺喜欢这种反反复复见面交流的方式的。

## `ZagreusField_1417`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You see, Alecto? Turns out we do have a common interest after all.

**中文**

你看，阿勒克图。看样子我们还是有共同爱好的。

## `ZagreusField_1418`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Forgiveness can take time. Though self-reflection is a healthy step. We're making progress!

**中文**

原谅是需要时间的。不过自我反省也是一种健康的做法。可算有进展了！

## `ZagreusField_1419`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Have you given any thought to just... leaving me alone, and going back to wherever it is you came from?

**中文**

你难道就不能……别来，回你自己的地方去吗？

## `ZagreusField_1420`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Alecto, I didn't ask you to repeatedly attempt to kill me. How did you expect me to respond?

**中文**

阿勒克图，我并没有要求你重复不停地要杀我。你让我怎么回答？

## `ZagreusField_1421`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sorry to disappoint you, Alecto. I know you enjoy being responsible for a hundred percent of my bloodshed.

**中文**

不好意思让你失望了，阿勒克图。我知道你非常乐意对我流血而亡负百分之百的责任。

## `ZagreusField_1422`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I've saved some of my blood, yes! Though I was thinking I'd hang onto it for now, if you don't mind.

**中文**

我当然多留了一点！不过我觉得现在还得留着，希望你别介意。

## `ZagreusField_1423`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I ran into some setbacks, if you have to know. But don't worry, I'm sure I've plenty left to bleed.

**中文**

来之前遇到了点困难，告诉你也无妨。不过没事，我的血量管够。

## `ZagreusField_1424`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, you must be one of Meg's sisters! Nice to finally meet you. My name's Zagreus. Not Murderer. Definitely not.

**中文**

你应该就是墨纪的姐妹之一吧！很高兴终于见到你了。我的名字是扎格列欧斯。不是杀手。绝对不是。

## `ZagreusField_1425`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm... look, I've done some things that maybe aren't great, but I am very, very sure I haven't murdered anyone.

**中文**

呃……你听我说，我确实做过一些不好的事，但是我确定一定以及肯定，我没有杀过任何人。

## `ZagreusField_1426`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm... how about, no? No murder. No murder!

**中文**

嗯……试试说不，好吗？不杀。不杀！

## `ZagreusField_1427`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I was really hoping we could change the subject. Please?

**中文**

我真的觉得我们可以换一个词。谢谢你？

## `ZagreusField_1428`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Let me guess. Murder? Murderer, perhaps? One of those two.

**中文**

容我想想。杀？杀手？就这两个词。

## `ZagreusField_1429`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Please, I'm not a murderer, you don't have to do this.

**中文**

求求你，我真不是杀手，你不用老是这样说。

## `ZagreusField_1430`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

We're not getting through to one another, are we. I'm sorry that it has to be this way.

**中文**

看样子确实是没法正常沟通了。对于接下来要发生的事情我很抱歉。

## `ZagreusField_1431`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't understand. But I'm not who you think I am. My father sent you to stop me, that's all.

**中文**

我真的不懂。但是我真的不是你想的那样。我父亲派你来阻止我，是这样的。

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanatos.

塔纳托斯。

**EN**

Thanatos...

**中文**

塔纳托斯...

**EN**

Thanatos!

**中文**

塔纳托斯！

**EN**

Than!

**中文**

塔纳！

**EN**

Thanatos...!

**中文**

塔纳托斯...！

Thanatos...?

塔纳托斯...？

**EN**

Wave-Makers.

**EN**

**EN**

**中文**

更多暗波投手僵尸！

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**中文**

更多暗波投手僵尸？

**EN**

烈焰投手僵尸。

**EN**

Burn-Flingers!

烈焰投手僵尸！

**EN**

更多烈焰投手僵尸！

**EN**

More Burn-Flingers?

更多烈焰投手僵尸？

**EN**

Oh, um. Hi there!

**中文**

哦，呃。好呀！

## `ZagreusField_1447`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Alecto.

**中文**

阿勒克图。

## `ZagreusField_1448`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh great.

**中文**

哦，很好。

## `ZagreusField_1449`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm back!

**中文**

我回来了。

## `ZagreusField_1450`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, hi.

**中文**

哦，嗨。

## `ZagreusField_1451`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Greetings, Alecto.

**中文**

你好，阿勒克图。

## `ZagreusField_1452`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Erm... nice to meet you?

**中文**

呃...很高兴见到你？

## `ZagreusField_1453`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Think I know what you're going to say.

**中文**

我觉得自己知道你要说什么。

## `ZagreusField_1454`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's hear it.

**中文**

我们来听听看。

## `ZagreusField_1455`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hello, Tisiphone.

**中文**

你好，提西福涅。

## `ZagreusField_1456`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tisiphone.

**中文**

提西福涅。

## `ZagreusField_1457`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh good.

**中文**

哦，好。

## `ZagreusField_1458`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Get out.

**中文**

滚开。

## `ZagreusField_1459`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's that, then.

**中文**

那就这样吧。

## `ZagreusField_1460`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

She wasn't very nice.

**中文**

她不大好相处。

## `ZagreusField_1461`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tell Meg I said hello.

**中文**

代我向墨纪问好。

## `ZagreusField_1462`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See you later.

**中文**

再见。

## `ZagreusField_1463`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I don't like her very much.

**中文**

我不太喜欢她。

## `ZagreusField_1464`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Peace, Tisiphone.

**中文**

淡定，提西福涅。

## `ZagreusField_1465`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's one way to prove I'm innocent.

**中文**

它可以证明我是无辜的。

## `ZagreusField_1466`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Glad that's over.

**中文**

终于结束了。

## `ZagreusField_1467`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hope she won't be back anytime soon.

**中文**

希望她不会再回来。

## `ZagreusField_1468`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Always disturbing.

**中文**

总是让人心烦。

## `ZagreusField_1469`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Go wrongfully accuse somebody else.

**中文**

还错怪别人。

## `ZagreusField_1470`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hoh, what's this.

**中文**

嚯，这是什么。

## `ZagreusField_1471`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right, then, let's get mad.

**中文**

好吧，要把我们逼疯了。

## `ZagreusField_1472`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Is this where Father's goons assemble to come after me?

**中文**

父亲的小喽啰们就是在这里集合，然后对我紧追不舍的吗？

## `ZagreusField_1473`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Too bad I can't just take the ferry to the surface.

**中文**

很遗憾，没办法坐船回到地表。

## `ZagreusField_1474`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

By the power of Skelly!

**中文**

以骨头的力量！

## `ZagreusField_1475`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

By Skelly's Lucky Tooth!

**中文**

凭借骨头的幸运牙齿！

## `ZagreusField_1476`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Skelly power!

**中文**

骨头的力量！

## `ZagreusField_1477`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Yes, let's.

**中文**

好的，我们一起。

## `ZagreusField_1478`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's not my style, Than.

**中文**

这不是我的风格，塔纳。

## `ZagreusField_1479`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

One way to find out.

**中文**

有办法找出真相。

## `ZagreusField_1480`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's, by all means.

**中文**

我们一起，用尽一切办法。

## `ZagreusField_1481`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's find out.

**中文**

我们一起去寻找。

## `ZagreusField_1482`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sounds good.

**中文**

听起来不错。

## `ZagreusField_1483`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Absolutely.

**中文**

毫无疑问。

## `ZagreusField_1484`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let them come.

**中文**

让他们过来。

## `ZagreusField_1485`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got it under control, thanks.

**中文**

一切尽在掌握，谢谢。

## `ZagreusField_1486`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That was my plan.

**中文**

这原是我的计划。

## `ZagreusField_1487`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Occasionally, yes.

**中文**

偶尔，是的。

## `ZagreusField_1488`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hopefully not me.

**中文**

但愿不是我。

## `ZagreusField_1489`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No, it will not.

**中文**

不，不会这样。

## `ZagreusField_1490`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

He's not kidding.

**中文**

他没在开玩笑。

## `ZagreusField_1491`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll also do that!

**中文**

我也会这样做！

## `ZagreusField_1492`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You better!

**中文**

你最好是！

## `ZagreusField_1493`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You're too kind.

**中文**

你太好了。

## `ZagreusField_1494`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Excluding me I hope.

**中文**

希望不包括我。

## `ZagreusField_1495`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Yeah.

**中文**

耶。

## `ZagreusField_1496`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ready for Wrath.

**中文**

做好应对愤怒的准备。

## `ZagreusField_1497`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wrath time.

**中文**

愤怒时间。

## `ZagreusField_1498`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, I'm mad.

**中文**

哦，我要疯了。

## `ZagreusField_1499`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Grrr...!

**中文**

呃...！

## `ZagreusField_1500`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Gods help you.

**中文**

愿众神助你一臂之力。

## `ZagreusField_1501`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Anger...

**中文**

怒火...

## `ZagreusField_1502`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time for Wrath.

**中文**

愤怒时间。

## `ZagreusField_1503`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's it.

**中文**

就这样。

## `ZagreusField_1504`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Off to Asphodel.

**中文**

出发前往水仙花平原。

## `ZagreusField_1505`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's hear it, Father.

**中文**

我们来听听看，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_1506`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, Father?

**中文**

好吧，父亲？

## `ZagreusField_1507`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Moving on.

**中文**

继续前进。

## `ZagreusField_1508`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Next stop, Asphodel.

**中文**

下一站，水仙花平原。

## `ZagreusField_1509`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

She's powering up.

**中文**

她的力量大增。

## `ZagreusField_1510`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

She's getting angry.

**中文**

她生气了。

## `ZagreusField_1511`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'd better stop her.

**中文**

我最好劝劝她。

## `ZagreusField_1512`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

She's getting mad.

**中文**

她要发飙了。

## `ZagreusField_1513`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

She's psyching up.

**中文**

她要发作了。

## `ZagreusField_1514`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

She's charging up.

**中文**

她在酝酿情绪。

## `ZagreusField_1515`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thanatos. I figured it was only a matter of time before Father sent you after me to do his dirty work.

**中文**

塔纳托斯。我早知道，父亲派你来阻止我，恐怕只是时间问题。

## `ZagreusField_1516`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not again.

**中文**

不会又来吧？

## `ZagreusField_1517`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lights out.

**中文**

没有光线。

## `ZagreusField_1518`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The chamber....

**中文**

密室...

## `ZagreusField_1519`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The walls....

**中文**

墙...

## `ZagreusField_1520`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not good.

**中文**

不好。

## `ZagreusField_1521`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What is she...

**中文**

她在...

## `ZagreusField_1522`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This again.

**中文**

又是这样。

## `ZagreusField_1523`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I can hold my own in case you haven't noticed, Than.

**中文**

也许你不知道，我还是有两把刷子的，塔纳。

## `ZagreusField_1524`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What's Uncle Poseidon doing here?

**中文**

波塞冬叔叔在这里做什么？

## `ZagreusField_1525`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll be the judge of that.

**中文**

由我来决定。

## `ZagreusField_1526`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We gods are all alike.

**中文**

众神都差不多。

## `ZagreusField_1527`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll leave my mark all right.

**中文**

我的所作所为都是对的。

## `ZagreusField_1528`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You got that right.

**中文**

你说对了。

## `ZagreusField_1529`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

File this, Father.

**中文**

记住，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_1530`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nyx, give me strength.

**中文**

倪克斯，给我力量。

## `ZagreusField_1531`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Who's ready for the real afterlife experience?

**中文**

谁想要体验死后的感觉？

## `ZagreusField_1532`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll show you willfulness.

**中文**

我会让你知道任性的后果。

## `ZagreusField_1533`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I take some pride in making messes, yes.

**中文**

制造混乱让我骄傲，是的。

## `ZagreusField_1534`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How about I take a closer look.

**中文**

让我凑近点看看如何？

## `ZagreusField_1535`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You don't need me, and I don't need you.

**中文**

你不需要我，我也不需要你。

## `ZagreusField_1536`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm no mere mortal.

**中文**

我不是籍籍无名的凡胎肉骨。

## `ZagreusField_1537`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

If only you could see me now, Father.

**中文**

只要你现在可以看到我，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_1538`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time for another blood spree, then.

**中文**

现在，再来一次鲜血的狂欢。

## `ZagreusField_1539`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I sure did, Father.

**中文**

当然了，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_1540`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You are correct, Father.

**中文**

你是正确的，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_1541`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It most certainly did.

**中文**

确实是这样。

## `ZagreusField_1542`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It fell to me, indeed.

**中文**

它确实发生在我身上。

## `ZagreusField_1543`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I think I'm going to Elysium.

**中文**

我想我要去至福乐土了。

## `ZagreusField_1544`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You know exactly where.

**中文**

你知道它在什么地方。

## `ZagreusField_1545`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I figured, thanks.

**中文**

我猜到了，谢谢。

## `ZagreusField_1546`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh I'm sure it's fine.

**中文**

哦，我肯定没问题的。

## `ZagreusField_1547`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks for the tip.

**中文**

谢谢你的建议。

## `ZagreusField_1548`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't know any other way.

**中文**

不知道还有其他什么办法。

## `ZagreusField_1549`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good.

**中文**

不错。

## `ZagreusField_1550`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sounds like progress to me.

**中文**

在我看来好像有进展。

## `ZagreusField_1551`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I am, indeed.

**中文**

确实，我是。

## `ZagreusField_1552`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Seems that way.

**中文**

似乎是这样。

## `ZagreusField_1553`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We'll see.

**中文**

我们等着瞧。

## `ZagreusField_1554`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't count on it.

**中文**

别指望它。

## `ZagreusField_1555`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Or else what, Father?

**中文**

或其他什么东西，父亲？

## `ZagreusField_1556`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sorry, can't turn back.

**中文**

抱歉，没办法回头。

## `ZagreusField_1557`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

So do I.

**中文**

我也是。

## `ZagreusField_1558`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not in the mood for your jesting, Father.

**中文**

没心情听你开玩笑，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_1559`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There's always the next one.

**中文**

总会有下一个。

## `ZagreusField_1560`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What are you, the Three Fates?

**中文**

你们是谁，命运三女神吗？

## `ZagreusField_1561`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't worry, won't be long.

**中文**

别担心，用不了很长时间。

## `ZagreusField_1704`

**EN**
