...好。

## `ZagreusField_1711`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, see you.

**中文**

好吧，再见。

## `ZagreusField_1712`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bye.

**中文**

再见。

## `ZagreusField_1713`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Think nothing of it, sir. Though if there's something I could do to help, you'd tell me, wouldn't you?

**中文**

没事，先生。如果有什么事我可以帮上忙，请尽管说，好吗？

## `ZagreusField_1714`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No need to go out of your way, there, sir. We're just acquaintances. I don't know anything about you, either.

**中文**

不用麻烦了，先生。我们只是刚认识而已。我对你也是一无所知。

## `ZagreusField_1715`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

The ever-shifting chambers of the Underworld leave quite a bit to chance. I took our meetings merely for coincidence.

**中文**

不断变化的冥界房间包含了相当多的可能性。我以为我们的会面只是巧合罢了。

## `ZagreusField_1716`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I please, sir. What's your question? Ask away.

**中文**

没问题，先生。你想知道什么？问吧。

## `ZagreusField_1717`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You've done a lot for me. The least I can do is ask around. Who is it that you're looking for?

**中文**

那样的话真的帮了我大忙。至少我可以帮你四处打听一下。你要找的人是谁？

## `ZagreusField_1718`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Achilles... I've heard the name before. And, erm, can I ask why you wish to know of him? In case it happens to come up.

**中文**

…阿喀琉斯…我听过这个名字。那么，唔，请问你为何要找到他呢？以防万一。

## `ZagreusField_1719`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...I... will mention if I see him, sir.

**中文**

如果我见到他，我...就会提起的，先生。

## `ZagreusField_1720`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know Achilles, don't you, sir. You're Patroclus. Achilles... serves us at my home. He trained me. He sends you his regards and love. He says to tell you he is thinking of you always.

**中文**

你认识阿喀琉斯，对吧，先生。你是普特洛克勒斯。阿喀琉斯…现在在我家中工作。他训练了我。他托我向你带来他的问候与爱。他说他无时无刻不在想念你。

## `ZagreusField_1721`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But, that can't possibly be true. There must be some misunderstanding here. You haven't spoken since you died?

**中文**

但是，这不可能啊。这其中一定有什么误会。你们死后就一直没有说过话吗？

## `ZagreusField_1722`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He's forced to do my father's bidding. And sworn to secrecy, apparently. I don't pretend to fully understand. But, all in all, I think he's doing well. I hope... you'll see him for yourself.

**中文**

我父亲强迫他做事。并且发誓要保守秘密，很明显。我不会装成我完全理解一样。不过，总而言之，我觉得他过得还可以。我希望…你可以自己去看他。

## `ZagreusField_1723`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'll... see what I can do about it, sir.

**中文**

我会…看看我能做点什么，先生。

## `ZagreusField_1724`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No. That's not acceptable to me. None of it.

**中文**

不，这我无法接受。全都无法接受。

## `ZagreusField_1725`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'll figure something out. But you do something for me, in the mean time: Don't give in to what you're feeling now, all right? Achilles loves you still, like you don't know.

**中文**

我要弄明白一些事。但你现在要帮我点忙，与此同时：不要被自己所谓的感觉给骗了。好吗？阿喀琉斯依然爱着你，但你不知道。

## `ZagreusField_1726`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Just bide your time here, sir.

**中文**

在这里等待时机，先生。

## `ZagreusField_1727`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, this? How did you know? It's just a keepsake from a friend.

**中文**

等等，这个吗？你怎么知道？这只是一个朋友送的纪念品。

## `ZagreusField_1728`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You recognize it, then. Of course. It's from Achilles, sir. He gifted it to me. He trained me.

**中文**

你认得这个。当然认得。这是阿喀琉斯的东西，先生。是他送给我的礼物。他训练了我。

## `ZagreusField_1729`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Just on one of my little jaunts through paradise is all, sir. Happened to be on the way toward my destination.

**中文**

就当是我在这仙境里的一次小旅行吧，先生。正好在去目的地的路上碰到你了。

## `ZagreusField_1730`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, you know, it's going. That I've been able to make it this far means I am getting somewhere, I suppose. No thanks to all your brethren in Elysium.

**中文**

噢，没什么，正在进行呢。既然我能走到这里，我觉得应该离成功不远了。感谢你在至福乐土里的所有弟兄。

## `ZagreusField_1731`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, look, I can tell you're struggling right now, and... maybe this isn't the way to help you through it all, but... I want you to have this.

**中文**

我知道你现在内心煎熬……这也许不能帮你解决问题，但…我想让你收下这个。

## `ZagreusField_1732`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I am most grateful to you, sir.

**中文**

我对你充满了感恩，先生。

## `ZagreusField_1733`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

If this eases your burden even for a little while, then it's in good hands, as far as I'm concerned.

**中文**

如果这些东西能稍微减轻哪怕一丁点你的负担，我的目的也就达到了。

## `ZagreusField_1734`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's nothing. Thank you for not trying to slay me where I stand and boast about it, like seemingly everyone else around here.

**中文**

没什么的。感谢你没有跟这里其他人一样一个劲想杀死我，然后到处吹嘘。

## `ZagreusField_1735`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'd say you earned it more than anybody else I've seen around here, lately. Though I don't make the rules.

**中文**

要我说，你比这里的其他任何人都配得上这东西。可惜，规矩也不是我定的。

## `ZagreusField_1736`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You don't attack me indiscriminately, I give you Ambrosia. I don't think I'm being over-generous!

**中文**

你并没有无缘无故攻击我。我送你这仙馔密酒，我可不认为我这是慷慨过头！

## `ZagreusField_1737`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's both very dejected and very astute of you to say. Take care of yourself, will you?

**中文**

你这种说法，沮丧中又透露出一丝狡猾。自己保重，好吗？

## `ZagreusField_1738`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's not a matter of owe, sir. Please open your mind to the possibility, nay the fact, that there are those who care about your wellbeing.

**中文**

这不是什么欠不欠的问题，先生。还请你敞开心扉，接受那些关心你的人。

## `ZagreusField_1739`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm not going out of my way, sir. Look, I don't pretend to know the depths of your despair. But when I look at you, I'm reminded of how I've often felt. When I was in that dark place, someone said to me: Don't give up. If nothing else... it gives you something to do.

**中文**

没你说的那么严重，先生。我不是假装知道你有多绝望。但是当我看到你的时候，我就会想起我曾经常有过的感觉。我在那黑暗的地方，有人对我说：不要放弃。哪怕没别的…这也会让你有事可做。

## `ZagreusField_1740`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thank you.

**中文**

谢谢你。

## `ZagreusField_1741`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks.

**中文**

谢谢。

## `ZagreusField_1742`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks a lot.

**中文**

非常感谢。

## `ZagreusField_1743`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thank you sir.

**中文**

谢谢你，先生。

## `ZagreusField_1744`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks for this.

**中文**

为此心怀感激。

## `ZagreusField_1745`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks sir.

**中文**

谢谢你，先生。

## `ZagreusField_1746`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Agreed on both counts! Just passing through, so if you'll excuse me...

**中文**

我们双方都同意这一观点！我只是路过，如果可以的话我想……

## `ZagreusField_1747`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, you're not the Champion of Elysium...? You're the Bull of Minos! I can't imagine who's even stronger than you. Is it Heracles? Tell me it's Heracles.

**中文**

等下，至福乐土最强战士怎么不是你……？你是米诺斯公牛！还有谁能壮得过你啊？赫拉克勒斯？肯定是赫拉克勒斯。

## `ZagreusField_1748`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Short one?! That's a low blow, sir. I thought you were the big-honorable type.

**中文**

小矮个？！真是没想到啊，先生。我还以为你是那种高大威猛讲荣誉的类型。

## `ZagreusField_1749`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Short order? That was another jab about my height, wasn't it!

**中文**

你说短？你又拿我的身高刺激我，是不是！

## `ZagreusField_1750`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, I never challenged you! The entrance to your chamber looks like any other pathway in Elysium. I have no quarrel with you, sir!

**中文**

你等下，我没有挑战你！只是你房间的入口看上去像是通往至福乐土的。我并没有针对你，先生！

## `ZagreusField_1751`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It means I've challenged you to a battle of strength and glory, because apparently that's all there is to do for fun around here for those such as yourself?

**中文**

这意味着我要挑战你的力量和荣耀，因为很明显这是你在这里仅有的娱乐活动。

## `ZagreusField_1752`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Ah, yes, my favorite pastime: Getting butchered by a giant double-bladed axe. And sometimes trampled.

**中文**

啊，是啊，我的最爱：被人拿超大的双头斧子追着砍。那人还用脚踩我。

## `ZagreusField_1753`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So then, why bother fighting me? You don't have to do this. That Theseus is a highly questionable influence on you!

**中文**

既然如此，那干嘛还要跟我打？你不用老是这样说。这个忒修斯，他对你造成的影响太大了！

## `ZagreusField_1754`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You mean face both of you, together. Which doesn't seem particularly fair.

**中文**

你意思是你俩一起打我一个。这样真的不是很公平。

## `ZagreusField_1755`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're nothing like Theseus. Why go along with everything he says and does? You're your own man. And bull.

**中文**

你和忒修斯完全不是一路人。你干嘛要这样一直遵从他？你是一个有思想的人。或者说牛。

## `ZagreusField_1756`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's nice of him and all, but sounds like you're even to me. Seeing as he killed you in the first place.

**中文**

那他可真是个好人啊，不过听起来咱俩差不多。毕竟是他先杀的你。

## `ZagreusField_1757`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, you hurt me with this 'short one' business almost as much as with that axe. Why isn't Theseus 'short one'? We're the same height!

**中文**

你知不知道，你每次管我叫“小矮个”的时候都跟你那斧子一样伤人。为什么忒修斯就不是“小矮个”？我们明明一样高！

## `ZagreusField_1758`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Uhh, oh! It's just, I have an odd request. You see, the god of slumber, Hypnos? Colleague of mine. Huge fan. He's in desperate need of your autograph. Little personalized message? I don't suppose you'd be so kind. It'd mean a lot to him.

**中文**

呃，噢！我就是，有一个奇怪的问题。睡神，修普诺斯你知道吧？我的同事。他是你粉丝。他特别想要你的亲笔签名。专门写给他的话之类的。你可能不会这样轻易就给他。但是这对他来说非常重要。

## `ZagreusField_1759`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Shall we get started then?

**中文**

我们可以开始了吗？

## `ZagreusField_1760`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yeah, well. You're kind of in my way. What choice do I have?

**中文**

其实。是你挡我路了。我又能怎么办啊？

## `ZagreusField_1761`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're going to force a rematch each time I manage to defeat you, aren't you?

**中文**

每次我要打赢你的时候你就要再来一场，是不是啊？

## `ZagreusField_1762`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

An unfair fight is not worth having, then, wouldn't you say?

**中文**

那不公平的战斗，还有什么好打的，你说对吧？

## `ZagreusField_1763`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Theseus?! The Bull of Minos?! You two are legends! I'd be honored if we had a sporting contest here!

**中文**

忒修斯？！米诺斯公牛？！你们是传说级人物啊！如果能与两位公平竞技的话我会非常荣幸。

## `ZagreusField_1764`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Theseus?! No way! You two are legends! I'd be honored if we had a sporting contest here!

**中文**

忒修斯？！不是吧！你们是传说级人物啊！如果能与两位公平竞技的话我会非常荣幸。

## `ZagreusField_1765`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know full well that wasn't a fair fight. Face me yourself and we'll see how you fare.

**中文**

你明白这不是一场公平战斗。出来干一架，我就知道你的斤两了。

## `ZagreusField_1766`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Besmirch what honor? You're just complaining that you lost. Even though I was outnumbered!

**中文**

我玷污个屁。你只不过是不甘心自己输了而已。你们二打一都能输！

## `ZagreusField_1767`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Then you leave me with little choice but to make you shut up, already. And your bull-headed manservant there.

**中文**

那我现在除了让你闭嘴别无他法了。还包括你那个牛头人男仆。

## `ZagreusField_1768`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh no! Not the flame of righteousness! Are you finished? Let's get this started, I've come a long way.

**中文**

哦不！不是正义的火焰！说完没有？直接开始吧，我可是走了好长时间呢。

## `ZagreusField_1769`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, on quite the contrary I've been under no impression that avoiding conflict is an option here. So say your piece, and let's get on with it.

**中文**

不，完全没有，我觉得避免战斗是不可能的。真实诚，那来战斗吧。

## `ZagreusField_1770`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So all you do here in Elysium is train for martial competition? You're constantly awaiting your next fight to the death?

**中文**

所以你在至福乐土的工作就是训练打架？一直准备着下一次的生死搏斗？

## `ZagreusField_1771`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

God-hated? You'll have to be more specific. I have the gods on my side. Though I suppose you do, too. Why can't they make up their minds?

**中文**

众神记恨？你能说得再仔细点吗。众神可都站在我这边。不过我想你也跟我一样。为什么他们就不能专一些呢？

## `ZagreusField_1772`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And what's this lesson that I ought have learned? Is it to always slay you first since you're so damn insufferable all the time?

**中文**

那你倒是说说我有什么可学的？第一个要杀的永远是你这个屁话连天的东西？

## `ZagreusField_1773`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Shouldn't I  be the Champion of Elysium, now that I've beaten you? Isn't there someone else around to challenge me, say, Heracles? Someone less chatty?

**中文**

既然我打败过你，那我应该是极乐世界最强了吧？难道不是有其他人要来挑战我吗，比如说赫拉克勒斯？话少的人？

## `ZagreusField_1774`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I couldn't care less about your title. Though, shouldn't you share it with the Minotaur? He's more than half the reason you've been any trouble.

**中文**

好像谁在意你这破头衔似的。你不是应该跟弥诺陶分享这个头衔吗？你遇到的麻烦跟他可都脱不了干系。

## `ZagreusField_1775`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So we're locked in an endless cycle of violence, basically. I guess that's one way for you to pass the time here in this stodgy place.

**中文**

所以我们是被困在了这个暴力循环中。我想这就是你在这个枯燥无味的地方消磨时间的方式吧。

## `ZagreusField_1776`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, this? I just picked it out of a Well. Was supposed to help me get here. No offense intended, more than usual. What's it to you?

**中文**

你说这个？是在一口井外找到的。一路上帮了我不少忙。若有得罪，还请谅解。跟你有什么关系？

## `ZagreusField_1777`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Ran into trouble on the way with your combative friends here in Elysium. One more unfair advantage in your favor.

**中文**

我来的路上跟你那些好战的至福乐土朋友起了些争执。你又掌握了一个不公平的优势。

## `ZagreusField_1778`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

One down, one to go.

**中文**

一个倒下了，还有一个。

## `ZagreusField_1779`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You're next, Bull-man!

**中文**

你是下一个，牛头怪！

## `ZagreusField_1780`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See that, Bull-man?

**中文**

看到了吧，牛头怪？

## `ZagreusField_1781`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You're next, king!

**中文**

下一个就轮到你了，国王！

## `ZagreusField_1782`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You and me, king!

**中文**

来和我单挑，国王！

## `ZagreusField_1783`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Now to deal with you!

**中文**

现在就对付你！

## `ZagreusField_1784`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You're next!

**中文**

你是下一个！

## `ZagreusField_1785`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Now for the other one.

**中文**

现在轮到另一个。

## `ZagreusField_1786`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You and me!

**中文**

你和我！

## `ZagreusField_1787`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You and me now!

**中文**

现在轮到你和我！

## `ZagreusField_1788`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I beat the Bull of Minos.

**中文**

我打败了米诺斯的公牛。

## `ZagreusField_1789`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well fought, Asterius.

**中文**

表现不错，阿斯忒里俄斯。

## `ZagreusField_1790`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I guess I win this round.

**中文**

我猜，这局是我赢了。

## `ZagreusField_1791`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got him that time.

**中文**

那次把他搞定了。

## `ZagreusField_1792`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Who's the short one now, huh?!

**中文**

现在谁是小矮个呢？！

## `ZagreusField_1793`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got you, Asterius.

**中文**

打败你了，阿斯忒里俄斯。

## `ZagreusField_1794`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That'll teach him.

**中文**

他会从中吸取教训。

## `ZagreusField_1795`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That ought to shut him up.

**中文**

这应该可以让他闭嘴。

## `ZagreusField_1796`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Who's Champion now?

**中文**

现在谁是最强战士？

## `ZagreusField_1797`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What a loudmouth.

**中文**

好大的嗓门。

## `ZagreusField_1798`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

They say to never meet your heroes.

**中文**

人们都说千万不要去见你心目当中的英雄。

## `ZagreusField_1799`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's one Champion unseated.

**中文**

那名最强战士名誉扫地。

## `ZagreusField_1800`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That'll show them.

赫耳墨斯大人，你没有要求任何回报，所以，请接受这份谦卑的礼物，因为……这真的没有什么！

## `ZagreusField_1935`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hermes, my friend? May I call you my friend? I hope this modest offering will lift your spirits but won't slow you down.

**中文**

赫尔墨斯，我的朋友？我可以叫你我的朋友吗？我希望这份谦卑的礼物能让你振奋起来，但不会拖你的后腿。

## `ZagreusField_1936`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Ever since we met, Lord Hermes, life here in the Underworld has been much faster-paced, and I am ever grateful. This is for you.

**中文**

赫耳墨斯大人，自从我们相识以来，冥界的生活节奏已经快了很多，我非常感激。这是给你的。

## `ZagreusField_1937`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I never could have come this far this quickly had it not been for your tireless support, Lord Hermes, sir. I'm in your debt.

**中文**

赫耳墨斯大人，先生，如果不是你的不懈支持，我决不会这么快就走到这一步。我欠你一份人情。

## `ZagreusField_1938`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The haste of Hermes.

**中文**

赫尔墨斯的速度。

## `ZagreusField_1939`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hermes, send me on.

**中文**

赫尔墨斯，让我去。

## `ZagreusField_1940`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's move, Hermes.

**中文**

我们出发，赫尔墨斯。

## `ZagreusField_1941`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's godspeed all right.

**中文**

真是神速。

## `ZagreusField_1942`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Godspeed.

**中文**

神速。

## `ZagreusField_1943`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Faster than ever.

**中文**

比任何时候都快。

## `ZagreusField_1944`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to pick up the pace.

**中文**

是时候加快速度了。

## `ZagreusField_1945`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cheers, Hermes.

**中文**

再见，赫尔墨斯。

## `ZagreusField_1946`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whew, felt that!

**中文**

呼，感觉到了。

## `ZagreusField_1947`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's the stuff.

**中文**

就是那东西。

## `ZagreusField_1948`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to go.

**中文**

现在出发。

## `ZagreusField_1949`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

And here we go.

**中文**

我们走。

## `ZagreusField_1950`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lord Hermes, I am in your debt.

**中文**

赫尔墨斯大人，我欠你个人情。

## `ZagreusField_1951`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Perish the thought, Lord Hermes. It's been great hearing from you from time to time.

**中文**

赫尔墨斯大人，别瞎想了。能时不时收到你的来信真是太好了。

## `ZagreusField_1952`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Now that you mention it, Lord Hermes, yes, let's keep our interactions nice and quick.

**中文**

既然你提到了，赫尔墨斯大人，是的，希望我们能有事说事，节省大家的时间。

## `ZagreusField_1953`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I miss hearing from you, Hermes. Let's carry on like we did before.

**中文**

我想念你的来信，赫尔墨斯。让我们像以前那样保持联系。

## `ZagreusField_1954`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Appreciate you checking in, Lord Hermes. I think we should keep at it like we have been, nice and quiet.

**中文**

谢谢你的关心，赫尔墨斯。我觉得我们应该一直这样保持下去，美好又平静。

## `ZagreusField_1955`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, yes, let's go back to having witty conversation from you, Hermes. That sounds great.

**中文**

是的，你知道，谈天说地，赫尔墨斯。听起来不错。

## `ZagreusField_1956`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Straight to the Boons, Lord Hermes. We both appreciate cutting straight to the chase.

**中文**

直接给我恩赐，赫尔墨斯大人。我们都喜欢直接切入主题。

## `ZagreusField_1957`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hi Charon! I finally met Hermes, god of swiftness, travel, bringing souls of the departed to your boat. Calls you his professional associate. You and he, you've been working together for a while, haven't you?

**中文**

嗨卡戎！我终于遇到赫尔墨斯了，迅捷、旅行之神，将死者的灵魂带到你的船上。你真是请到了专业人士的帮忙呢。你跟他，你们合作有一段时间了吧，对吗？

## `ZagreusField_1958`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You don't say.

**中文**

可不是吗？

## `ZagreusField_1959`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Charon, I always thought Olympians never consorted with chthonic gods such as yourself. But it sounds like Hermes is a close colleague of yours. You wouldn't... happen to know why he's helping me, would you?

**中文**

卡戎，我总以为奥林匹斯众神从来不跟像你这种冥界的神打交道呢。不过，赫尔墨斯跟你好像是关系很好的同事一样。你该不会…碰巧知道他为什么要帮助我吧？

## `ZagreusField_1960`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Err I'm sorry mate, I meant you no offense.

**中文**

额，抱歉，朋友，我不是有意冒犯。

## `ZagreusField_1961`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Charon, Hermes told me that the other Olympians are unaware he's lending his support to me. I'm curious to know what's going on. Thinking of asking Nyx. Figured I'd check with you first.

**中文**

卡戎，赫尔墨斯说他是瞒着奥林匹斯众神在帮助我。我有点好奇到底是怎么一回事。我想问问倪克斯了。不过我得先问问你。

## `ZagreusField_1962`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll... keep it to myself for now, then. Thanks.

**中文**

我会...暂时保守这个秘密。谢谢。

## `ZagreusField_1963`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Charon mate, wanted to thank you once again for the fine hourglass you gave to me before! It's been very handy with those Wells of yours.

**中文**

喂，卡戎老伙计，我想再次感谢你，谢谢你之前给了我这个不错的沙漏！这在你们那些井里发挥了不小的作用呢。

## `ZagreusField_1964`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, cheers!

**中文**

好吧，再见！

## `ZagreusField_1965`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Say hi to Hermes for me!

**中文**

代我向赫尔墨斯问好！

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

替我跟赫尔墨斯打声招呼，好吗？

More Flame Wheels!

更多火焰战车！

**EN**

More Flame Wheels?

**中文**

更多火焰战车？

**EN**

**EN**

Well isn't that just great.

这样不是很好吗？

**EN**

All that for nothing.

**EN**

**EN**

**EN**

My Plume grew stronger.

**EN**

The Lambent Plume.

萤光羽毛。

**EN**

The power of Hermes.

赫尔墨斯的力量。

## `ZagreusField_1995`

**EN**

Swift as Hermes.

和赫尔墨斯一样快。

## `ZagreusField_1996`

**EN**

Hermes would approve.

赫尔墨斯也会同意。

**EN**

Short work.

## `ZagreusField_1998`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Detecting a pattern yet, Father?

**中文**

发现什么模式了吗，父亲？

## `ZagreusField_1999`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

They weren't that great.

**中文**

它们也没这么好。

## `ZagreusField_2000`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't forget Asterius!

**中文**

别忘了阿斯忒里俄斯！

## `ZagreusField_2001`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

He's all talk.

**中文**

他只会夸夸其谈。

## `ZagreusField_2002`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

So you keep saying.

**中文**

你继续扯。

## `ZagreusField_2003`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You know nothing of my struggles.

**中文**

你对于我的处境一无所知。

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I do.

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**EN**

Not making you do anything.

**EN**

**EN**

I'm sure I'll find something.

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_2012`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Looking forward to it!

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_2013`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See you soon, Father.

**中文**

再见，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_2014`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's more of a brisk jog.

**中文**

这不仅是随性的慢跑而已。

## `ZagreusField_2015`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Better off this way.

**中文**

这样做更好。

## `ZagreusField_2016`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Perhaps your expectations are too high.

## `ZagreusField_2017`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I pray his ego will recover soon.

**中文**

我希望他的自尊心很快就能恢复。

## `ZagreusField_2018`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No, but you're going to anyway.

**中文**

不，但你无论如何也要这样。

## `ZagreusField_2019`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You leave Cerberus out of this.

你不要把刻耳柏洛斯牵扯进来。

## `ZagreusField_2020`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tell him I said hi.

**中文**

替我向他问好。

## `ZagreusField_2021`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mind asking him to tone it down a bit?

**中文**

能不能让他降低声调？

## `ZagreusField_2022`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What would you know of it?

**中文**

你又知道些什么？

## `ZagreusField_2023`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What are you, the Fates?

**中文**

你们是谁，命运之神吗？

## `ZagreusField_2024`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I welcome you to try.

**中文**

欢迎你尝试。

## `ZagreusField_2025`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Then I'll deal with you same as all the rest.

**中文**

然后我会像对付其他人一样对付你。

## `ZagreusField_2026`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Can wash away my Boons.

**中文**

可以把我身上的祝福洗掉。

## `ZagreusField_2027`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Purge Boons for coin.

**中文**

把祝福换成钱。

## `ZagreusField_2028`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Any Boons worth giving up?

**中文**

要放弃哪些祝福呢？

## `ZagreusField_2029`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's think about this.

**中文**

我们来考虑一下。

## `ZagreusField_2030`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Do I still need these?

**中文**

我还需要这些吗？

## `ZagreusField_2031`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Should I take the trade.

**中文**

我应该接受交易吗。

## `ZagreusField_2032`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Should I take the trade?

**中文**

我应该接受交易吗？

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's one less Boon.

**中文**

少了个祝福。

## `ZagreusField_2034`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Feel cleaner already.

**中文**

感觉干净一些。

## `ZagreusField_2035`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There goes that one.

**中文**

然后是它。

## `ZagreusField_2036`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Won't be needing that.

**中文**

不再需要它。

## `ZagreusField_2037`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Could use the extra coin.

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_2038`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whoo, that's cold.

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_2039`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The gods won't mind.

**EN**

**EN**

Can't use this now.

**EN**

Looks like I'm clean.

我好像更干净了。

**EN**

Not much I can do with this now.

**EN**

**EN**

**EN**

**EN**

**EN**

## `ZagreusField_2063`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So it's really come to this, boy? After everything we've been through. Well then, if this is how it has to be, there's nothing left to say. Except for: On your guard!!

**中文**

这一天真的来了，乖乖。经过了这么多。好吧，如果非得这样的话，也没什么好说的了。除了：准备战斗！！

## `ZagreusField_2064`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hahaha, no, but seriously, boy. I know why you're here. There has to be a way to make you look the other way. What if I help you with the Satyr problem, would you let me go? Please. I have to find Persephone... my mother. You know what this means to me.

**中文**

哈哈哈，不，但是老实说，乖乖。我知道你为什么在这里。肯定有什么办法能让你放弃这样做。如果我帮你解决萨提尔的问题，你能放我走吗？求你了。我一定要找到珀尔塞福涅。你知道这对我意味着什么。

## `ZagreusField_2065`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good! Now I just need to head off the beaten path here and see what I can find.

**中文**

很好！我现在需要转移方向，去这条被踏平的小路看看能发现什么。

## `ZagreusField_2066`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

All right, boy, I got your favorite treat. But you have to promise to let me pass if I hand it over, OK?

**中文**

好了乖乖，这是你最喜欢的。给你可以，但是你得保证下次让我通过，怎么样？

## `ZagreusField_2067`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You know how many Satyrs I had to get through to bring you this, boy? So could you let me through?

**中文**

你知道要给你带着东西，我得对付多少萨提尔吗，乖狗狗？所以，你可以让我过去了吗？

## `ZagreusField_2068`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Break time, boy! Got you a veritable feast, here, in this lovely sack.

**中文**

休息时间，乖狗狗！给你带了名副其实的美味，就在这只麻袋里。

## `ZagreusField_2069`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

One Satyr Sack for one good boy.

**中文**

这一麻袋的萨提尔给乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusField_2070`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey boy, got you something you are going to eat right up!

**中文**

嘿，乖狗狗。给你带了点东西，猜你一定会狼吞虎咽地吃光！

## `ZagreusField_2071`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Special delivery, boy.

**中文**

给你的惊喜，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusField_2072`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got this delicious sack of meal for you, boy.

**中文**

这只装满美味的麻袋是给你的，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusField_2073`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Chow time, boy, you hungry?

**中文**

吃饭了，乖狗狗，你肚子饿吗？

## `ZagreusField_2074`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

One Satyr Sack coming right up!

**中文**

一麻袋的萨提尔马上就到！

## `ZagreusField_2075`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Please enjoy the finest dining, courtesy of our ongoing Satyr infestation here.

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_2076`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks, boy.

**中文**

谢谢你，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusField_2077`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bye for now, boy.

**中文**

再见了，乖狗狗。

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**中文**

**EN**

**中文**

**中文**

**EN**

**中文**

**中文**

**EN**

Should I go fight some Satyrs, or get out of here?

**中文**

我该去消灭一些萨提尔，还是离开这里呢？

**EN**

Maybe I should check the other passageways before I leave.

**EN**

Disgusting. Cerberus will love it!

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey boy.

嘿，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusField_2152`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Back on duty, huh.

## `ZagreusField_2153`

**EN**

## `ZagreusField_2154`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_2155`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey boy!

**中文**

嘿，乖狗狗！

## `ZagreusField_2156`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You must be hungry, huh.

**中文**

你肯定饿了吧？

## `ZagreusField_2157`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Just playing with you, boy.

**中文**

只是逗你玩呢，乖狗狗。

## `ZagreusField_2158`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There's a good boy.

**中文**

好样的，乖狗狗。

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No time for play, huh?

**中文**

**EN**

So serious on guard duty.

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_2161`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not going to budge, are you?

**中文**

不打算让步？

## `ZagreusField_2162`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cerberus puts up with so much.

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯一直逆来顺受。

## `ZagreusField_2163`

**EN**

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Father.

**中文**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nice night, no?

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_2169`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I guess there's only one thing left to do. Isn't that so, Father?

**中文**

我猜只剩下一件事没做了，对吗，父亲？

## `ZagreusField_2170`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I don't think I'll ever get accustomed to the breeze.

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_2171`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

## `ZagreusField_2172`

**EN**

One last bit of business to take care of, then.

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_2173`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No. No mournful speeches. Now get out of my way.

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not finished, then?

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh great.

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I told you that I would, Father. And I'm going to. Goodbye.

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

**中文**

什么？等下。我又不欠你什么干嘛帮你带话。不过，要是你回答我一个问题，我就考虑一下，怎么样？

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My mother. Persephone. Why did she leave? Why did she leave you? Why did she leave me?

**中文**

## `ZagreusField_2179`

**EN**

Good-bye, Father.

再见，父亲。

地狱传道者。

Spreaders!

地狱传道者！

**EN**

More Spreaders?

更多地狱传道者？

**EN**

Splitters.

**中文**

禁忌使者。

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Splitters!

禁忌使者！

## `ZagreusField_2214`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Persephone. My mother. Why did she leave?

**中文**

珀尔塞福涅。我的母亲。她为什么要离开？

## `ZagreusField_2215`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Snakestones.

**中文**

蛇石。

## `ZagreusField_2216`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Snakestones!

**中文**

蛇石！

## `ZagreusField_2217`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

More Snakestones!

**中文**

更多蛇石！

## `ZagreusField_2218`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

More Snakestones?

**中文**

更多蛇石？

## `ZagreusField_2219`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It did!

**中文**

是它！

## `ZagreusField_2220`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Shut up!

**中文**

住嘴！

## `ZagreusField_2221`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Father...!

**中文**

父亲...！

## `ZagreusField_2222`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You don't know me!

**中文**

你不了解我！

## `ZagreusField_2223`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Go back to your desk!

**中文**

去做正事！

## `ZagreusField_2224`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, you're right. The air up here seems pretty good so far. But I will reserve judgment till I've had my fill. I have to say, though, Father: The Helm of Darkness suits you. I'm touched you'd dust it off on my account.

**中文**

这倒没错。这里的空气目前来说还行。不过在我受够之前我还是持保留意见。我还是得说，父亲：黑暗之盔还蛮适合你的。你专门为我把它打理了一番，我很感动。

## `ZagreusField_2226`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No. You're going home, not me.

**中文**

不。是你要回家，不是我。

## `ZagreusField_2227`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, that can't possibly be true! What about Orpheus, Theseus, Heracles, Odysseus? Countless tales of brave men delving into the Underworld, then leaving whence they came!

**中文**

这不可能吧，那俄耳甫斯怎么解释？提修斯？赫拉克勒斯？奥德修斯？传说中的英雄们都是想来就来想走就走。

## `ZagreusField_2228`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

If you're quite finished teaching me about this snow phenomenon, can we get on with brutally trying to kill each other, here?

**中文**

如果你教完降雪现象这一课了，应该接着进行疯狂互殴环节了吧？

## `ZagreusField_2229`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And you succeeded. Chopped up some of them into many tiny bits, and sprinkled them throughout the pits of Tartarus so that they can't regenerate! Or so I hear. Inspiring, really!

**中文**

然后你们成功了。把其中的泰坦切成无数碎片，抛撒到塔耳塔罗斯的深渊之中，这样他们就再也不能复原！我是这样听说的。很精彩！

## `ZagreusField_2230`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I do. Besides, making these treks helps pass the time during the ongoing Underworld renovations. And I'll be in tiptop shape once they're complete!

**中文**

不放弃。另外，我做这样的事也方便在冥界修复的同时消磨自己的时间。等到修复完成，我就会变成一流的高手！

## `ZagreusField_2231`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's not exactly easy getting here, you know. But I'm not going back. Not yet.

**中文**

一路杀过来可不容易，你知道吗。但我绝不回去。现在不会。

## `ZagreusField_2232`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Olympus!

**中文**

奥林匹斯山！

## `ZagreusField_2233`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Olympus...!

**中文**

奥林匹斯山...！

## `ZagreusField_2234`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Zeus!!

**中文**

宙斯！！

## `ZagreusField_2235`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lord Zeus...!

**中文**

众神之王宙斯...！

## `ZagreusField_2236`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Poseidon!!

**中文**

波塞冬！！

## `ZagreusField_2237`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Poseidon...!

**中文**

波塞冬...！

## `ZagreusField_2238`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Athena!!

**中文**

雅典娜！！

## `ZagreusField_2239`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Athena...!

**中文**

雅典娜...！

## `ZagreusField_2240`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ares!!

**中文**

阿瑞斯！！

## `ZagreusField_2241`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ares...!

**中文**

阿瑞斯...！

## `ZagreusField_2242`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Artemis!!

**中文**

阿尔忒弥斯！！

## `ZagreusField_2243`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Artemis...!

**中文**

阿尔忒弥斯...！

## `ZagreusField_2244`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Aphrodite!!

**中文**

阿弗洛狄忒！！

## `ZagreusField_2245`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Aphrodite...!

**中文**

阿佛洛狄忒...！

## `ZagreusField_2246`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Dionysus!!

**中文**

迪奥尼索司！！

## `ZagreusField_2247`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Dionysus...!

**中文**

迪奥尼索司...！

## `ZagreusField_2248`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hermes!

**中文**

赫尔墨斯！

## `ZagreusField_2249`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hermes...!

**中文**

赫尔墨斯...！

## `ZagreusField_2250`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The wrath of Poseidon at my beck and call.

**中文**

波塞冬的怒火对我来说有求必应。

## `ZagreusField_2251`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We'll see about that.

**中文**

我们走着瞧。

## `ZagreusField_2252`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I've almost made it...

**中文**

我差一点就成功了...

## `ZagreusField_2253`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Generous selection of his wares.

**中文**

他的商品种类繁多。

## `ZagreusField_2254`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

He loves to eat. Can you blame him?

**中文**

他喜欢吃。这能怪他吗？

## `ZagreusField_2255`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Satyr markings...

**中文**

萨提尔的标记...

## `ZagreusField_2256`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cerberus just loves the stuff.

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯喜欢这玩意儿。

## `ZagreusField_2257`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ground floor...

**中文**

一楼...

## `ZagreusField_2258`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

One last stop.

**中文**

最后一站。

## `ZagreusField_2259`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

One more stop.

**中文**

还有一站。

## `ZagreusField_2260`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I can do this. I can do this.

**中文**

我可以做到。我可以做到。

## `ZagreusField_2261`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

So close.

**中文**

就差一点。

## `ZagreusField_2262`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

To the surface.

**中文**

前往地表。

## `ZagreusField_2263`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There.

**中文**

那边。

## `ZagreusField_2264`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Yes.

**中文**

太好了。

## `ZagreusField_2265`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bye.

**中文**

再见。

## `ZagreusField_2266`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tsch...

**中文**

哧…

## `ZagreusField_2267`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Say hi to Hypnos for me.

**中文**

代我向修普诺斯问好。

## `ZagreusField_2268`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This one I guess.

**中文**

我猜是这个。

## `ZagreusField_2269`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Here goes.

**中文**

看吧。

## `ZagreusField_2270`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Maybe it's in here.

**中文**

它也许在这里。

## `ZagreusField_2271`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Should check this way.

**中文**

应该去这边看看。

## `ZagreusField_2272`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I bet it's in here.

**中文**

我敢打赌它在这里。

## `ZagreusField_2273`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Try this way.

**中文**

来这边试试。

## `ZagreusField_2274`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Maybe this way.

**中文**

或许在这边。

## `ZagreusField_2275`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh shut up, and get your fork, and burn away that regal cape, and let's get to this, then. I'd no idea you had all these capes to spare! Must have a whole entire storage chamber full of them.

**中文**

别说了吧，把你叉子拿上，身上那高贵披风给我焚了，我们干一架。我就搞不懂你哪来那么多披风！一定有一个专门的披风室吧。

## `ZagreusField_2276`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It has been snowy nightfall every single time now that you mention it. I didn't know it snowed so much up here.

**中文**

既然你都提到了，现在每天黄昏都会下雪。上面从来不会下这样多的雪。

## `ZagreusField_2277`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Beats having to toil away under your overbearing supervision. And besides, as you may be aware, I've recently made up my mind to leave this place.

**中文**

这可比在你手下艰辛工作要好得多。另外，你应该也知道了，我已经决心要离开这个地方了。

## `ZagreusField_2278`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Need help with something, Father?!

**中文**

需要帮助吗，父亲？！

## `ZagreusField_2279`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I thought you planned to handle this yourself!

**中文**

我以为你打算自己处理这件事呢！

## `ZagreusField_2280`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Why don't you tell your loyal subjects to stay out of this!

**中文**

你为什么不让你忠实的下属别插手呢！

## `ZagreusField_2281`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Upon my name, I'll bury all you wretches here and now!

**中文**

以我的名义，我要让你们这些暗灵全部葬身于此！

## `ZagreusField_2282`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hope this can get me out of here.

**中文**

但愿这样做可以让我离开这个鬼地方。

## `ZagreusField_2283`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Should be able to get back through here.

**中文**

应该可以从这里回去。

## `ZagreusField_2284`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's just go down this Satyr hole.

**中文**

我们下到这个萨提尔洞穴里看看。

## `ZagreusField_2285`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Back I go.

**中文**

我回去了。

## `ZagreusField_2286`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Enough of this place.

**中文**

受够了这地方。

## `ZagreusField_2287`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Going back.

**中文**

回去。

## `ZagreusField_2288`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Heading back.

**中文**

往回走。

## `ZagreusField_2289`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Had quite enough here, thanks.

**中文**

有点受够这地方了，谢谢。

## `ZagreusField_2290`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Down the chute.

**中文**

跳下去吧。

## `ZagreusField_2291`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I do!

**中文**

是的。

## `ZagreusField_2292`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's right!

**中文**

这就对了！

## `ZagreusField_2293`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What do you think, Father?

**中文**

你是怎么想的，父亲？

## `ZagreusField_2294`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I guess so!

**中文**

我也这么觉得！

## `ZagreusField_2295`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh you'll think better of me soon!

**中文**

哦，很快你就会对我另眼相看！

## `ZagreusField_2296`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

And I am shocked to hear it!

**中文**

我听了感到很震惊！

## `ZagreusField_2297`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

And I think you're a miserable failure as a father!

**中文**

我觉得你作为父亲非常失败！

## `ZagreusField_2298`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's my time to do with as I please!

**中文**

现在我要按照自己的方式来！

## `ZagreusField_2299`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I don't care what you think!

**中文**

我不在乎你会怎么想！

## `ZagreusField_2300`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh this isn't a waste of time at all!

**中文**

哦，这完全不是浪费时间！

## `ZagreusField_2301`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It beats having to live within your House!

**中文**

总好过和你一起住在圣殿里！

## `ZagreusField_2302`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You know exactly why!

**中文**

你知道是什么原因！

## `ZagreusField_2303`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You'd like that, wouldn't you?

**中文**

你喜欢这样，不是吗？

## `ZagreusField_2304`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Would that you could shut up and fight!

**中文**

你可以闭嘴，好好去战斗吗！

## `ZagreusField_2305`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Liar! Why should I trust a single word you say?

**中文**

骗子！为什么我要相信你的话？

## `ZagreusField_2306`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No! She's out there. I know it!

**中文**

不！她还在某个地方。我知道的！

## `ZagreusField_2307`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, let's see. You've berated me repeatedly and often. You've lied to me. Mustered your wretched forces to kill me, over and over. But, sure, I'll grant you that you've always kept most of your anger bottled up. All that's about to change, then?

**中文**

那就来看看吧。你一直在指责我。你骗了我。一次又一次召集你的人马来杀我。但是没错，我承认你一直憋着自己的怒气。所有这些都将改变。

## `ZagreusField_2308`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wow...

**中文**

哇...

## `ZagreusField_2309`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh. Of course.

**中文**

哦，当然。

## `ZagreusField_2310`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Though you didn't expect that, one day, I would end up using everything I learned from him against you.

**中文**

但你不知道的是，有一天，我会用我从他那学到的每招每式来对付你。

## `ZagreusField_2311`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I can leave, or explore more tunnels.

**中文**

我可以离开，也可以再探索几条隧道。

## `ZagreusField_2312`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cerberus is back on duty. Got to find him something to eat.

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯又回到了岗位。必须给他找点吃的。

## `ZagreusField_2313`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to feed the beast.

**中文**

该给那头野兽喂点吃的了。

## `ZagreusField_2314`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There's Cerberus. Won't budge till I find him a treat.

**中文**

那是刻耳柏洛斯。他是不会让步的，除非给他带点吃的。

## `ZagreusField_2315`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to find old Cerberus a Satyr sack.

**中文**

应该给老刻耳柏洛斯找个萨提尔麻袋。

## `ZagreusField_2316`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Won't get out of here until I find something for Cerberus.

**中文**

在为刻耳柏洛斯找到点什么之前，我是不会离开这里的。

## `ZagreusField_2317`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cerberus guarding the gates as always.

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯总是看守着大门。

## `ZagreusField_2318`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to clear some Satyr tunnels and get out of here.

**中文**

现在清空几条萨提尔隧道，然后离开这里。

## `ZagreusField_2319`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Now to get Cerberus to step aside.

**中文**

现在让刻耳柏洛斯别挡住我们的去路。

## `ZagreusField_2320`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Want something to eat, boy? Huh?

**中文**

想要吃的吗，乖狗狗？

## `ZagreusField_2321`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You hungry, Cerberus? I bet you are!

**中文**

你饿了吧，刻耳柏洛斯？我打赌你肯定饿了！

## `ZagreusField_2322`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Shut.

**中文**

关着。

## `ZagreusField_2323`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's shut.

**中文**

它被关上了。

## `ZagreusField_2324`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Locked.

**中文**

被锁住了。

## `ZagreusField_2325`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's locked.

**中文**

它被锁上了。

## `ZagreusField_2326`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cleared it out.

**中文**

清出一条路来。

## `ZagreusField_2327`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nothing else back there.

**中文**

后面什么都没有。

## `ZagreusField_2328`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Done with that.

**中文**

受够了。

## `ZagreusField_2329`

## `ZagreusField_2454`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well let's see. You've berated me repeatedly and often. You've lied to me. Mustered a bunch of incompetent wretches to try to kill me. But, sure, I'll grant you that you've always kept most of your anger bottled up. All that's about to change, then?

**中文**

那就来看看吧。你一直在指责我。你骗了我。甚至召集自己的人马想杀死我。但是没错，我承认你一直憋着自己的怒气。所有这些都将改变。

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Aah!

啊！

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You!

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

## `ZagreusField_2463`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

## `ZagreusField_2465`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You mean once you'd chopped up your cruel and overbearing Titan parents, right? Shame you threw it out. It's quite useful.

**中文**

你是指你们曾经把自己的泰坦爹妈大卸八块那件事吗？真可惜你把它给扔了。还挺好用的。

## `ZagreusField_2466`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Must be hard, knowing that everybody's going to think you learned your spearwork from Poseidon, not the other way around. As for Stygius, I can assure you it's in good hands now.

**中文**

一定很难过吧，所有人都认为你是跟波塞冬学的，而事实恰恰相反。至于斯第格思，我向你保证它现在在正确的人手里。

## `ZagreusField_2467`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What? I haven't even started to insult you here yet, Father. Flustered by the old Shield of Chaos, then?

**中文**

干嘛？我还什么都没说呢，父亲。只是见到这块旧混沌之盾就受不了了吗？

## `ZagreusField_2468`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hi there! No need for formalities, I'm Zagreus. Just heading out on some official business. Past the menacing Bone Hydra. Don't mean to be rude, though I'm in a bit of a rush.

**中文**

你好！不用这样正式啦，我叫扎格列欧斯。只是出来例行公事。经过凶恶的骸骨九头蛇。不太好意思，但是我着实赶时间。

## `ZagreusField_2469`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hi there! No need for formalities, I'm Zagreus. Just heading out on some official business! Surveying the magma situation firsthand. Don't mean to be rude, though I'm in a bit of a rush.

**中文**

你好！不用这样正式啦，我叫扎格列欧斯。只是出来例行公事。调查一下岩浆问题。不太好意思，但是我着实赶时间。

## `ZagreusField_2470`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hi there! No need for formalities, I'm Zagreus. Just heading out on some official business. Say, if your name's Eurydice, I don't suppose you know an Orpheus, do you? Quite musically inclined, a little dour, impressive hair?

**中文**

你好！不用这样正式啦，我叫扎格列欧斯。只是出来例行公事。这么说，如果你是欧律狄克，你应该认识俄耳甫斯把？喜好音乐，有点严肃，引人注目的头发？

## `ZagreusField_2471`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well it's just, I think that you may know an Orpheus, don't you? Quite musically inclined, a little dour, impressive hair? He's court musician in my father's house. Calls you his muse. Says he misses you terribly. I... thought you should know.

**中文**

嗯就是，你认不认识俄耳甫斯？喜好音乐，有点严肃，引人注目的头发？他现在是我父亲圣殿里的宫廷乐师。他说你是她的缪斯。还说他非常想你。我…认为你应该要知道。

## `ZagreusField_2472`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well it's just, I think I wanted to tell you more of Orpheus, since... he's court musician in my father's house. Calls you his muse. Says he misses you terribly. I... thought you should know.

**中文**

就是，我认为我要多跟你说说俄耳甫斯的事，因为…他现在是我父亲圣殿里的宫廷乐师。他说你是她的缪斯。还说他非常想你。我…认为你应该要知道。

## `ZagreusField_2473`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm... yes, I do believe I can. Look, I didn't mean to upset you.

**中文**

呃…我想我知道了。我不是想让你感到不快。

## `ZagreusField_2474`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Of course I know Orpheus, we're mates! Wait, why do you ask?

**中文**

我当然认识俄耳甫斯了，我们可是朋友呢！等下，你问这个干什么？

## `ZagreusField_2475`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Certainly not! I'm merely here for your wonderful singing, cooking, or conversational ability. Though, since you mentioned it, you... seem to have a lot to say to him. Is there anything I can do?

**中文**

当然不是！我来这里只是为了欣赏你美妙的歌唱、烹饪以及交流技巧。不过，既然你提到了，你…好像有很多话要对他说。有什么我能帮忙的吗？

## `ZagreusField_2476`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's a beautiful song. Even though you're angry with him... Orpheus, I mean... you're always singing, still?

**中文**

真是首不错的歌啊。尽管你生他的气。俄耳甫斯，我是说…你现在还经常在唱歌吗？

## `ZagreusField_2477`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, not exactly in those words, but... Orpheus can be a little hard to parse.

**中文**

呃，只是说法有点不同…俄耳甫斯他有点复杂。

## `ZagreusField_2478`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Look, Eurydice, I promise not to bother you with this each time, but... you should know your husband's shattered by what happened with the two of you. He's genuinely sorry, and he loves you very deeply, still, I'm sure of it.

**中文**

听着，欧律狄克，我每次都保证不会拿这个烦扰你，但是…你俩的事情让你的丈夫很受打击。他真的非常内疚，而且他还是深爱着你，这点我敢肯定。

## `ZagreusField_2479`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I... I don't know yet. Though, you know what? You shouldn't take my word for it. You should take his.

**中文**

我…我不知道。我告诉你噢。你不应该只听我说的。你也要听他怎么说。

## `ZagreusField_2480`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Curls? Erm, not exactly, no. But he's doing all right! For him. He's started singing again, lately, which was a big step. He never used to, before. Would go on about how he'd lost his muse.

**中文**

卷发？呃，算不上吧，不过他现在过得还不错，最近又开始唱歌了！这是个相当不错的进步，因为他以前从来没唱过歌，他总是不断地说他是如何失去了他的缪斯。

## `ZagreusField_2481`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Eurydice, can I ask you for a favor here? Not that you owe me anything, but, would you hear me out?

**中文**

欧律狄克，我能请你帮个忙吗？不是说你欠我什么，不过，你能听我说吗？

## `ZagreusField_2482`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's not your recipes, though they are wonderful. It's your song. I'm sure it'd be a hit around the House! And, I know someone who really needs to hear it. Don't suppose you've got a parchment-copy I could have?

**中文**

不是食谱的事，尽管真的非常美味。是你的歌。这首歌一定能在圣殿里爆红的！而且，我知道有谁要去听一听这歌。你应该有羊皮纸副本吧，能给我吗？

## `ZagreusField_2483`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No need to wonder any longer. He sings it beautifully, around the House sometimes. It... has a sadder sound to it than when you sing, but... you've inspired him again. As you've done many times before, from what I know.

**中文**

不用再想了。他唱歌非常动听，有时候他会绕着圣殿唱。听起来比你唱的时候要多一点哀伤，但是…你又给了他灵感。就像你以前做的那样，就我所知。

## `ZagreusField_2484`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Even if you can't be together right now, I hope it feels better knowing how much he cares for you. And I will try to pull some strings, like you said. I can't make promises where my father's concerned, but... I'll do everything I can.

**中文**

即使你们现在无法在一起，我希望在知道他很关心你以后，你可以好过些。而且我会私底下想想办法，就像你说的那样。我没办法打包票说我的父亲关心什么，但是……我会竭尽所能。

## `ZagreusField_2485`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, you two, how's everything lately? On speaking terms again? Or singing terms?

**中文**

喂，你们两个，最近怎么样？是说出来还是唱出来？

## `ZagreusField_2486`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I've never heard anything like this. As good as you two sound each on your own, you're positively wonderful together.

**中文**

我从来没听过这种的歌曲。你们两个本身都非常出色，现在合唱的这首歌简直是棒极了。

## `ZagreusField_2487`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

By all means, carry on!

**中文**

继续努力！

## `ZagreusField_2488`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Eurydice, you're more than welcome, really. You've helped me through the flames of hell, time after time. Least I could do is coax old Orpheus out of his private pit of despair so you and he could set things right between the two of you.

**中文**

真的，欧律狄刻，你真是太客气了。你一次又一次助我闯过了这地狱之火。帮老俄耳甫斯摆脱内心的绝望，让他能够处理好你们之间的问题，这是我应该做的。

## `ZagreusField_2489`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Eurydice, wanted to thank you once again for this enchanted acorn that you gave me. So this is what plants and things grow from, up on the surface?

**中文**

欧律狄克，再次谢谢你送我这颗令人着迷的橡子。这是凡界的植物长出来的？

## `ZagreusField_2490`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, this is sort of a weird question, but... this Evergreen Acorn, it's not... related to you, is it? Since, you know, you're... partly oak, aren't you?

**中文**

嘿，我有个奇怪的问题……这颗常绿橡子，应该和你……没什么关系吧？因为，一半的你也是……橡树，对吗？

## `ZagreusField_2491`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Eurydice, how long have you been down here, anyway, if you don't mind my asking? Seems like you're pretty settled in. It's quite a lovely place!

**中文**

欧律狄克，你来这里多久了，不介意我问的话？你好像很自在的样子，这地方真的挺不错的！

## `ZagreusField_2492`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Am I ever. You're always so generous. And you're always cooking! And singing. Do you have many guests come by? Your place seems... somewhat off the beaten path.

**中文**

非常饿。你总是如此慷慨。你总是在烹饪！在唱歌！经常有客人来吗？你的这个地方…有点偏僻。

## `ZagreusField_2493`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Eurydice, how can you cook so much with all the searing heat around these parts? Has it been magma-flooded like this all the time?

**中文**

欧律狄克，你是怎么在这种炙热环境下煮出这些东西的？这里一直都是这样烈焰滚滚吗？

## `ZagreusField_2494`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, I've never met a nymph before. You're closer to the gods than mortals, right?

**中文**

你知道，我从来没有遇见过仙女呢。你比凡人更接近神，对吧？

## `ZagreusField_2495`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I... know the feeling better than you might expect.

**中文**

我…对这种感觉理解颇深。

## `ZagreusField_2496`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's... look, the truth is, I am trying to get out of here. I've hit some stumbling blocks along the way, but, I think I will make it there, eventually.

**中文**

就是…我还是说实话吧，我打算逃离这个地方。我在前进的路上遇到了一些障碍，不过我觉得自己一定能够成功的。

## `ZagreusField_2497`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't know that a map would help me much since things keep shifting out here every single time. Though it is an interesting coincidence how I keep running into you.

**中文**

外头的东西时刻都在变化，我想地图应该没什么用处吧。但我不停地碰见你真是有趣的巧合啊。

## `ZagreusField_2498`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Eurydice, thank you for making me feel welcome in your pleasant home. Can't say the same for most places I've been lately. I've a small gift I wanted you to have.

**中文**

欧律狄克，谢谢你让我在你温馨的家中受到如此款待。我最近去过的大多数地方都不如这里。我这里有个小礼物希望你可以收下。

## `ZagreusField_2499`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, don't worry, we're still getting to know each other! Plenty of time for me to live up to my notorious reputation. Though I'm surprised you've heard of me at all.

**中文**

噢，没事，我们还在互相了解当中呢！我有足够的时间来不辜负我的恶名。不过我很惊讶你竟然听说过我。

## `ZagreusField_2500`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, of all the souls I've ever met, I feel like you put this stuff to the best possible use.

**中文**

你知道吗，在我认识的所有灵魂当中，只有你知道这东西怎么用最好。

## `ZagreusField_2501`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And I, in turn, shall go through your incredible cooking awful fast, given the opportunity.

**中文**

如果有机会的话，我也会想尝试一下你美味的料理。

## `ZagreusField_2502`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

My pleasure! I'm always pleased to see you during my otherwise generally unfortunate travels in Asphodel.

**中文**

很荣幸！我能在水仙花平原的悲惨旅程中遇到你，真的非常欣慰。

## `ZagreusField_2503`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hah, no! I don't think I can get into more trouble at the moment. May this make your dishes that much more delectable!

**中文**

当然不会了！我是想不出有什么比我现在的处境还麻烦的事了。希望这东西能让你的菜肴更加美味！

## `ZagreusField_2504`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No, no, no, of course, and, thanks for your directness about this. Look I know you've been through a lot with Orpheus, but... it's clear to me how much you love each other, still. I'm just glad we met.

**中文**

不会，不会，当然不会，感谢你在这方面的坦率。我知道你和俄耳甫斯经历了很多，但是……我知道你们彼此还是相爱的。很高兴能认识你。

## `ZagreusField_2505`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I think your culinary practice would be every bit as extraordinary, though I'm pleased to have done small service to you in this great pursuit.

**中文**

我认为您的烹饪手艺还是会非常出色，尽管我很荣幸在这一伟大的追求中为您提供了一点帮助。

## `ZagreusField_2506`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What... singing... here?

**中文**

什么……歌声……在这？

## `ZagreusField_2507`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Must be Eurydice.

**中文**

应该是欧律狄刻。

## `ZagreusField_2508`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

She's always singing.

**中文**

她一直都在唱歌。

## `ZagreusField_2509`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good to be back here.

**中文**

回到这真好。

## `ZagreusField_2510`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Eurydice...

**中文**

欧律狄刻……

## `ZagreusField_2511`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wait... she's not singing...

**中文**

等等……她不在唱歌……

## `ZagreusField_2512`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hope Eurydice's all right...

**中文**

希望欧律狄刻没事……

## `ZagreusField_2513`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Why thank you!

**中文**

感谢款待！

## `ZagreusField_2514`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks so much!

**中文**

非常感谢！

## `ZagreusField_2515`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ooh thank you!

**中文**

噢！谢谢你！

## `ZagreusField_2516`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mm really hit the spot.

**中文**

正合心意。

## `ZagreusField_2517`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Feeling better already!

**中文**

感觉已经舒服点了！

## `ZagreusField_2518`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whew, that's good!

**中文**

呼，真不错！

## `ZagreusField_2519`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mmph, cheers!

**中文**

嗯，真不错！

## `ZagreusField_2520`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ooh that's good.

**中文**

噢，真不错。

## `ZagreusField_2521`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wow that's good.

**中文**

哇，真不错。

## `ZagreusField_2522`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

So good.

**中文**

真好。

## `ZagreusField_2523`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bye now!

**中文**

再见了！

## `ZagreusField_2524`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Till next time!

**中文**

下次见！

## `ZagreusField_2525`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See you!

**中文**

再见！

## `ZagreusField_2526`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks again!

**中文**

再次感谢！

## `ZagreusField_2527`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Take care!

**中文**

保重！

## `ZagreusField_2528`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bye!

**中文**

拜！

## `ZagreusField_2529`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See you again!

**中文**

回头见！

## `ZagreusField_2530`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's test that theory!

**中文**

来试试那理论！

## `ZagreusField_2531`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh I think it shall!

**中文**

噢！我觉得挺好！

## `ZagreusField_2532`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The other sister left...

**中文**

另一位姐妹离开了……

## `ZagreusField_2533`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Where'd the other one go?

**中文**

另一个去哪了？

## `ZagreusField_2534`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The other two took off...

**中文**

另外两个人离开了……

## `ZagreusField_2535`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The others flew off...

**中文**

其他人都飞走了……

## `ZagreusField_2536`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The other sisters left...

**中文**

剩下的姐妹都走了……

## `ZagreusField_2537`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Where'd the others go?

**中文**

其他人去哪了？

## `ZagreusField_2538`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Alecto took off...

**中文**

阿勒克图走了……

## `ZagreusField_2539`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Alecto flew off.

**中文**

阿勒克图飞走了。

## `ZagreusField_2540`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tisiphone took off...

**中文**

提西福涅走了……

## `ZagreusField_2541`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tisiphone flew off...

**中文**

提西福涅飞走了……

## `ZagreusField_2542`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Meg took off...

**中文**

墨纪走了……

## `ZagreusField_2543`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Meg flew off...

**中文**

墨纪飞走了……

## `ZagreusField_2544`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sorry!

**中文**

对不起！

## `ZagreusField_2545`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm sorry!

**中文**

对不起！

## `ZagreusField_2546`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sorry?

**中文**

抱歉？

## `ZagreusField_2547`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This is awkward!

**中文**

这好尴尬！

## `ZagreusField_2548`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Olympus?

**中文**

奥林匹斯？

## `ZagreusField_2549`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Gemstones.

**中文**

宝石。

## `ZagreusField_2550`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Assorted gems.

**中文**

各种宝石.

## `ZagreusField_2551`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There they are.

**中文**

他们在这儿。

## `ZagreusField_2552`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

For my trouble.

**中文**

有收获。

## `ZagreusField_2553`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Never enough of these back at the House.

**中文**

这些东西拿多少回去都不嫌多。

## `ZagreusField_2554`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Colorful.

**中文**

五颜六色。

## `ZagreusField_2555`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll take 'em.

**中文**

我全要了。

## `ZagreusField_2556`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mine now.

**中文**

我的了。

## `ZagreusField_2557`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll hang onto these.

**中文**

都是我的了。

## `ZagreusField_2558`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

For safe keeping.

**中文**

我来保管这些。

## `ZagreusField_2559`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

For the House.

**中文**

拿回去用。

## `ZagreusField_2560`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Claiming these.

**中文**

全是我的了。

## `ZagreusField_2561`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A diamond...

**中文**

一块钻石……

## `ZagreusField_2562`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ooh.

**中文**

噢。

## `ZagreusField_2563`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Aw, for me?

**中文**

噢，是给我的吗？

## `ZagreusField_2564`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Beautiful.

**中文**

真美。

## `ZagreusField_2565`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Should fetch me something good back home.

**中文**

回去后应该能拿它买些好东西。

## `ZagreusField_2566`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got some heft.

**中文**

真有分量。

## `ZagreusField_2567`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's brilliant.

**中文**

真棒啊。

## `ZagreusField_2568`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's beautiful.

**中文**

真美啊。

## `ZagreusField_2569`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll just tuck that away.

**中文**

我直接拿走好了。

## `ZagreusField_2570`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's no Skeleton Key.

**中文**

这不是万能钥匙。

## `ZagreusField_2571`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A Chthonic Key...

**中文**

一把冥府钥匙……

## `ZagreusField_2572`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A key of power.

**中文**

一把力量的钥匙。

## `ZagreusField_2573`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Found you...

**中文**

找到了……

## `ZagreusField_2574`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Stygius shuddered as I picked it up...

**中文**

我捡起它时科罗纳赫特颤抖了……

## `ZagreusField_2575`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I felt Aegis shudder...

**中文**
