## `ZagreusField_2718`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See that, Varatha?

**中文**

看到了吗，瓦拉塔？

## `ZagreusField_2719`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See that, Exagryph?

**中文**

看到了吗，伊格瑞菲？

## `ZagreusField_2720`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There it is.

**中文**

就是这样。

## `ZagreusField_2721`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There we go.

**中文**

我们走。

## `ZagreusField_2722`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My blade grows stronger.

**中文**

我的剑变得更强了。

## `ZagreusField_2723`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My bow grows stronger.

**中文**

我的弓变得更强了。

## `ZagreusField_2724`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My shield grows stronger.

**中文**

我的盾变得更强了。

## `ZagreusField_2725`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My spear grows stronger.

**中文**

我的矛变得更强了。

## `ZagreusField_2726`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The rail grows stronger.

**中文**

我的弩炮变得更强了。

## `ZagreusField_2727`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Power.

**中文**

力量。

## `ZagreusField_2728`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Deadlier than ever.

**中文**

比以前更锋利了。

## `ZagreusField_2729`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Perfect.

**中文**

完美。

## `ZagreusField_2730`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's go, Stygius.

**中文**

我们走吧，斯第格思。

## `ZagreusField_2731`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's go, Coronacht.

**中文**

我们走吧，科罗纳赫特。

## `ZagreusField_2732`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's go, Aegis.

**中文**

我们走吧，埃癸斯。

## `ZagreusField_2733`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's go, Varatha.

**中文**

我们走吧，瓦拉塔。

## `ZagreusField_2734`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's go, Exagryph.

**中文**

我们走吧，伊格瑞菲。

## `ZagreusField_2735`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I wasn't. But then you gave me a good idea to try. I'm not as weak as I once was. With or without the Mirror's help.

**中文**

我本来是不会。但现在，你让我有了一种要试一试的想法。我已经不再像过去那么软弱。不管有没有圣镜的帮助。

## `ZagreusField_2736`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, it's just... there's something that I wanted to discuss with you, at some point, Meg, but after more consideration, I am thinking maybe now is not the time.

**中文**

是这样，额……我本来想找个时间和你说点事，但经过慎重考虑，我觉得现在可能还不是时候。

## `ZagreusField_2737`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, you mean you aren't lonelier without Alecto's or Tisiphone's companionship? They seem so wonderful to have around.

**中文**

什么，你是说，没了阿勒克图或提西福涅的陪伴，你并不觉得孤单？有他们在身边不是很好吗？

## `ZagreusField_2738`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, because I've been distracting you from administering lashings like before? I hate to think you prefer to whip with no fear of reprisal whatsoever, Meg.

**中文**

什么意思，就因为我一直在分散注意力，不让你拿鞭子抽人家吗？我可不认为你是个喜欢拿鞭子抽人，从来不怕别人报复的家伙啊，墨纪。

## `ZagreusField_2739`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, Stygius and I have been getting much more closely acquainted lately, Meg. Would you care to see the fruits of our healthy symbiotic relationship?

**中文**

哦，斯第格斯和我最近的关系更紧密了，墨纪。你要见识一下我们之间这种健康的共生关系带来的成果吗？

## `ZagreusField_2740`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Now that you mention it, he hasn't, no. Though I'm prepared to say I don't need special augmentations to get past you.

**中文**

既然你都提到了，那他确实没有。不过我得说，想打败你，我还用不着这些额外的恩惠。

## `ZagreusField_2741`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, this old thing? It's just a listless butterfly Than gave me, which happens to fill me with even more supernatural strength than usual. Don't pay it any mind.

**中文**

噢，这个旧东西啊？是塔纳给我的，一只萎靡不振的蝴蝶，这东西可以给我拥有更加强大的超自然力量。别太在意就是了。

## `ZagreusField_2742`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, not just yet, though thanks for asking, Meg. If you intend to force me, know that I am going to resist, with all my might.

**中文**

没，还没准备好呢。不过谢谢你这么问，墨纪。如果你强行要送我回家，那我只能全力反抗了。

## `ZagreusField_2743`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, it wasn't I who stole them, if that's any reassurance to you, Meg!

**中文**

呃，偷这东西的人可不是我，不知这么说能不能让你安心一点，墨纪！

## `ZagreusField_2744`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I do. We wouldn't both be consummate professionals if it were any other way. You won't be going easy on me, will you?

**中文**

我知道。如果不是这样，我们两个都不会成为如此资深的专家。你非得对我这么苛刻吗？

## `ZagreusField_2745`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, don't take it so hard, Meg! Once you die several dozen times, you start to get accustomed to the emotional pain, as well as the physical! Up for some more of either?

**中文**

噢，别太在意这个，墨纪！一旦你死得多了，你就会慢慢适应心理和生理上的痛苦了！那么，咱们继续吧？

## `ZagreusField_2746`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's called a smile, Meg. I'm simply pleased to see you, nothing more. It could have been one of your sisters, no offense.

**中文**

这叫笑容，墨纪。我只是单纯地见到你很开心而已。无意冒犯，不过换做是你的姐妹，我也一样会笑。

## `ZagreusField_2747`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh she's been positively perfect, thank you, Meg. Especially in how she puts us into contact when I need a hand. I hope it's not too much hassle, helping out from time to time?

**中文**

噢，她好得很呢，谢谢关心，墨纪。而且每当我需要帮助的时候，她都能让我们之间产生联结。时不时让她帮忙，我只希望这不会给她添太多麻烦。

## `ZagreusField_2748`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Appreciate all the concern, but I just thought I'd live a little, Meg. You ought to try it for yourself, sometime!

**中文**

感谢关心，不过我还打算活一阵子呢，墨纪。你什么时候也应该体验体验努力求生的滋味！

## `ZagreusField_2749`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...This has to be some kind of bad dream.

**中文**

……这肯定是在做噩梦吧。

## `ZagreusField_2750`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, this is unfair... that's a bronze-plated Macedonian Tau-Lambda! What's an obnoxious bellyacher like you doing with the most durable chariot created, to date?

**中文**

哦，不公平……那是马其顿塔乌-拉姆达镀铜战车！像你这样讨厌的牢骚鬼要这辆迄今为止最伟大的战车做什么？

## `ZagreusField_2751`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, indeed, nice work! Though must have cost a fortune in gemstones and diamonds, restoring an old Macedonian. I thought it was totaled after last time.

**中文**

哦，确实，干得不错！不过，要修复一辆老旧的马其顿战车肯定用了不少宝石和钻石。我以为，经历过上次一战，它早就报废了呢。

## `ZagreusField_2752`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You only have that chariot because of me, you know. It's all because of a condition of the Pact of Punishment! Nothing you did or earned. So drop the act!

**中文**

你能得到那辆战车，都亏了我。都要拜惩罚契约的条款所赐！你根本不配。别装腔作势了！

## `ZagreusField_2753`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, my opinion of the Macedonian Tau-Lambda has fallen precipitously of late. Though perhaps it's not the chariot but the driver.

**中文**

你知道吗，我最近对马其顿塔乌-拉姆达战车的看法急转直下。但这可能和战车本身无关，主要是驾驶者的关系。

## `ZagreusField_2754`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Your strategy might work a little better if you didn't shout it loud enough for me to hear. Though I have to say I'm impressed you can drive, fight, and shout all at the same time.

**中文**

如果你别这么大喊大叫的，说不定这套策略能更有效。不得不承认，我对你的印象深刻，又能驾驶战车，又能打，嗓门还特别大。

## `ZagreusField_2755`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, this old thing? How nice of you to notice. It once belonged to the hero, Achilles. A little past your time, but maybe you've heard of him? Seeing as he's much more famous than you ever were.

**中文**

哦，你说的是这件老古董吗？很高兴你注意到了。它曾经属于英雄阿喀琉斯。和你不是一个时代的，不过你可能听说过，对吧？他好像比你出名多了。

## `ZagreusField_2756`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You seem pretty put off by a random bit of twine, there, Former King of Athens. Care to share your innermost thoughts on the subject, or best keep them bottled up?

**中文**

看起来几根线就缚住了你的手脚，雅典的老国王。要和我们分享你对这件事最真实的想法吗，还是将它将它憋在心里？

## `ZagreusField_2757`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Just happened to have it on me, Theseus. But it seems to keep upsetting you. Maybe you should seek this Ariadne in my father's realm, and make amends?

**中文**

我只是碰巧拿到它了，忒修斯。但它好像还是会让你心烦意乱。或许你应该到我父亲的领地里找找这位阿里阿德涅，做点补偿？

## `ZagreusField_2758`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Theseus? I would appreciate it if you could be more considerate of the substantial effort necessary just to reach this point where I can make you shut your mouth.

**中文**

忒修斯，如果你能体谅我们这么努力地想让你闭嘴，我会对你充满感激。

## `ZagreusField_2759`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thanks for your concern, there, Theseus. But I trust I have plenty of life left in me with which to destroy you, if not your companion as well.

**中文**

谢谢你的关心，忒修斯。但我相信自己还有足够的力量打败你，至于你的同伴就不好说了。

## `ZagreusField_2760`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh! No thanks. Appreciate the offer, though. How about a fight, instead?

**中文**

噢！不了，谢谢。不过还是要谢谢你对我这么大方。要不来打一场？

## `ZagreusField_2761`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Theseus, before you flap your ghostly gums at me again, I'd like to hear from good Asterius this time! He almost never gets a word in, thanks to you.

**中文**

忒修斯，我知道你狗嘴里吐不出象牙，这次我想听听阿斯忒里俄斯的意见！你说的太多，他几乎插不上话。

## `ZagreusField_2762`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Megaera!

**中文**

墨纪拉！

## `ZagreusField_2763`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Megaera?

**中文**

墨纪拉？

## `ZagreusField_2764`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Meg, help!

**中文**

墨纪，帮帮忙！

## `ZagreusField_2765`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Meg!!

**中文**

墨纪！！

## `ZagreusField_2766`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Meg!!

**中文**

墨纪！！

## `ZagreusField_2767`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey Meg?

**中文**

哎，墨纪？

## `ZagreusField_2768`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Skelly!

**中文**

骨头！

## `ZagreusField_2769`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Skelly?

**中文**

骨头？

## `ZagreusField_2770`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Come forth, Skelly!

**中文**

来啊，骨头！

## `ZagreusField_2771`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh Skelly?!

**中文**

噢，骨头？！

## `ZagreusField_2772`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Skelly, help!

**中文**

骨头，帮帮我！

## `ZagreusField_2773`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

To me, Skelly!

**中文**

来我这里，骨头！

## `ZagreusField_2774`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cheers!

**中文**

谢谢！

## `ZagreusField_2775`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bye now!

**中文**

再见了！

## `ZagreusField_2776`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See you!

**中文**

回见！

## `ZagreusField_2777`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See you at home?

**中文**

家里见吧？

## `ZagreusField_2778`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks!

**中文**

谢谢！

## `ZagreusField_2779`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not here.

**中文**

这里不行。

## `ZagreusField_2780`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not right now.

**中文**

现在不行。

## `ZagreusField_2781`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I wish.

**中文**

我也想。

## `ZagreusField_2782`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's spent.

**中文**

还没好。

## `ZagreusField_2783`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Out of charges.

**中文**

还没恢复呢。

## `ZagreusField_2784`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm on my own.

**中文**

我只能靠自己了。

## `ZagreusField_2785`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Bouldy...? I'm Zagreus, good to make your acquaintance. I know that friends are difficult to come by here in Tartarus, and so, I'm grateful that you seem to be a trusty one.

**中文**

额，石头……？我是扎格列欧斯，很高兴认识你。我知道在塔耳塔罗斯很难交到朋友，但你是值得深交的，我对此心怀感恩。

## `ZagreusField_2786`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Bouldy, while on one hand I know that making offerings to you won't really help get me out of here, on the other hand... maybe it will?

**中文**

石头，我知道送礼物给你对于从这里逃脱没有帮助，但另一方面……没准可以呢？

## `ZagreusField_2787`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Bouldy, how do you handle being trapped in Tartarus for all eternity, doomed to be pushed up and down a hill repeatedly for no good reason?

**中文**

石头，你对于永世被困在塔耳塔罗斯有什么想法，难道你命中注定要被反复机械地推上、滚下？

## `ZagreusField_2788`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So, erm, there something you like to do for entertainment out here, Bouldy? Or just, I don't know, take it all in, I guess?

**中文**

那么，呃，你这里有什么娱乐消遣吗？或者只是，怎么说呢，就这么坦然接受？

## `ZagreusField_2789`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What's the latest, Bouldy? Had any good tumbles down that hill of late?

**中文**

最近还好吗，石头？最近仍然要从那座山上滚下来吗？

## `ZagreusField_2790`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, I think I'm growing more accustomed to relating to a giant stone.

**中文**

你知道吗，我渐渐对有一个巨石朋友习以为常了。

## `ZagreusField_2791`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't know why I keep doing this, Bouldy, yet here we are.

**中文**

你知道我为什么一直要这样做吗，像现在这样，石头。

## `ZagreusField_2792`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You take good care of Sisyphus, there, won't you, Bouldy?

**中文**

你一直在细心地照顾西西弗斯，不是吗，石头？

## `ZagreusField_2793`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sometimes a conversation with a friend is its own reward, isn't that right, Bouldy?

**中文**

有时候和朋友聊聊天本身就会令人感到非常愉快，对吗，石头？

## `ZagreusField_2794`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're looking as stately as ever, there, Bouldy.

**中文**

你看起来一直这么威严，石头。

## `ZagreusField_2795`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

A tribute to the greatest boulder of all time.

**中文**

向有史以来最伟大的石头致敬。

## `ZagreusField_2796`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

To our most famous boulder!

**中文**

致我们最负盛名的石头！

## `ZagreusField_2797`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

May you roll smoothly for eternity.

**中文**

祝你在滚动时永远不会受到阻碍。

## `ZagreusField_2798`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thank you for keeping my friend company.

**中文**

感谢你一直以来的陪伴，我的朋友。

## `ZagreusField_2799`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This is for you, Bouldy.

**中文**

这是送给你的，石头。

## `ZagreusField_2800`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Bouldy, please accept this humble offering.

**中文**

石头，请收下这份微薄的礼物。

## `ZagreusField_2801`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Bouldy, lend your favor to me, won't you?

**中文**

石头，可以请你帮个忙吗？

## `ZagreusField_2802`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

An offering to the finest boulder in all of Tartarus.

**中文**

给整个塔耳塔罗斯最伟大的石头的礼物。

## `ZagreusField_2803`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Cheers, Bouldy! Thank you for brightening my day or night.

**中文**

开心点，石头！感谢你驱散我生命中的阴霾。

## `ZagreusField_2804`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Bouldy, ever get the feeling that we've had this conversation in the past?

**中文**

石头，你是不是感觉我们的对话好像似曾相识？

## `ZagreusField_2805`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Let's be sure to do this again sometime, Bouldy my friend.

**中文**

我们下次找个时间再来一次，我的朋友，就这么说定了。

## `ZagreusField_2806`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Any advice for me on this occasion, Bouldy?

**中文**

对于此时此刻的我，你有什么建议吗，石头？

## `ZagreusField_2807`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Bouldy, you can speak up if there's something on your mind, you know.

**中文**

石头，你心里想什么可以尽管说。

## `ZagreusField_2808`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I, uh... OK!

**中文**

我，呃……好吧！

## `ZagreusField_2809`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well then!

**中文**

那好吧！

## `ZagreusField_2810`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I see!

**中文**

我明白了！

## `ZagreusField_2811`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, cheers!

**中文**

好吧，再会！

## `ZagreusField_2812`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Enlightening!

**中文**

很有启发性！

## `ZagreusField_2813`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right!

**中文**

好嘞！

## `ZagreusField_2814`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Fair enough!

**中文**

很好！

## `ZagreusField_2815`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The ancient blood...

**中文**

古老的血……

## `ZagreusField_2816`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Blood of the Titans...

**中文**

泰坦的血……

## `ZagreusField_2817`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Blood...

**中文**

血……

## `ZagreusField_2818`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Titan Blood...

**中文**

泰坦之血……

## `ZagreusField_2819`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...Asterius, you look absolutely smashing!

**中文**

……阿斯忒里俄斯，你看起来真的太猛了！

## `ZagreusField_2820`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanatos!

**中文**

塔纳托斯！

## `ZagreusField_2821`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanatos?

**中文**

塔纳托斯？

## `ZagreusField_2822`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Than, help!

**中文**

塔纳，帮帮忙！

## `ZagreusField_2823`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Than!!

**中文**

塔纳！！

## `ZagreusField_2824`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Than!!

**中文**

塔纳！！

## `ZagreusField_2825`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey Than?

**中文**

嗨，塔纳？

## `ZagreusField_2826`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sisyphus!

**中文**

西西弗斯！

## `ZagreusField_2827`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sisyphus?

**中文**

西西弗斯？

## `ZagreusField_2828`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bouldy!

**中文**

石头！

## `ZagreusField_2829`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bouldy?

**中文**

石头？

## `ZagreusField_2830`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Go Sisyphus!

**中文**

上啊！西西弗斯！

## `ZagreusField_2831`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey Bouldy!

**中文**

嘿！石头！

## `ZagreusField_2832`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sisyphus, did I just hear correctly that Megaera has vowed to leave you be for now? Both you and Bouldy there?

**中文**

西西弗斯，我没听错吧？刚才墨纪拉发誓说暂时不管你了，也不管石头了？

## `ZagreusField_2833`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thanatos really isn't such a bad sort once you get to know him, sir. Though, I suppose you'll have to take my word for that.

**中文**

如果了解塔纳托斯的话，你会发现他并不是什么坏人，先生。你应该相信我的话。

## `ZagreusField_2834`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Made some arrangements to ensure your chamber here got some more visibility is all. Made for a good excuse to visit you again, sir!

**中文**

我做了些修整，能让你的房间变得更漂亮。用这个理由来再次拜访你还不错吧，先生！

## `ZagreusField_2835`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know I was just thinking, sir... sometimes I am a little envious of you and Bouldy there. You seem to have things pretty figured out. I still grow frustrated with myself quite often and don't always know whom to turn to.

**中文**

我一直在想，大人……有时候甚至有点羡慕你和那块巨石。你们好像都很清楚自己想要什么。但我还是不得志，我不知道应该向谁求助。

## `ZagreusField_2836`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So I can just... walk up and talk to him, and chat? That's all it takes?

**中文**

我可以……直接走过去和它说话、聊天？就这么简单吗？

## `ZagreusField_2837`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have to thank you, Sisyphus, not just for all the help you've given me time after time, but for encouraging me to have a chat with Bouldy there. I daresay I feel a little more at peace of late, and maybe it's thanks to him.

**中文**

我要好好感谢你，西西弗斯，你不仅三番五次地帮助我，而且鼓励我和石头聊天。我觉得最近平静多了，这可能都是你的功劳。

## `ZagreusField_2838`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You ever meet Alecto, Sisyphus? The Fury Megaera's nastier sister. We recently became acquainted, she and I.

**中文**

你有见到阿勒克图吗，西西弗斯？复仇女神墨纪拉的姐姐，比墨纪拉还难缠。我们最近刚刚认识。

## `ZagreusField_2839`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sisyphus, have you had run-ins with Tisiphone the Fury here before? She isn't much for conversation, I don't think.

**中文**

西西弗斯，来到这里之前你见过复仇女神提西福涅没？她好像不怎么太爱说话的样子。

## `ZagreusField_2840`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

She is. Although, I think she's back to deal with me. I'll have to keep her plenty busy just in case.

**中文**

没错。而且，我觉得她是专门回来找我的。我得让她好好忙一阵了。

## `ZagreusField_2841`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sisyphus, I've meant to ask, where are you getting all this stuff you give to me? You must be pretty well connected around here.

**中文**

西西弗斯，我想问你，你给我的那些东西都是从哪儿来的？你在这边的人脉肯定很广吧。

## `ZagreusField_2842`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey sir, certainly it's been most welcome having something from you with me at all times. Though has it always held this power? And... how did you remove it to begin with?

**中文**

噢，先生，我一直都非常荣幸能够继承您的“衣钵”。不过，它一直都有这种力量吗？还有……您究竟是怎么把它拿下来的呢？

## `ZagreusField_2843`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Thanks, sir. It's been of help through my repeated treks across this realm, all thanks to you. You sure you don't want it back?

**中文**

谢谢夸奖。在这个领地一次又一次的历险中，它帮了我很大的忙，这都要感谢您呢。您确定不想收回它吗？

## `ZagreusField_2844`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sisyphus, sir? You've helped me selflessly time after time without so much as asking anything in return. Wanted to say I'm very grateful, and thought you might like to try this. Here, for a special occasion!

**中文**

西西弗斯大人？你多次无私地帮助了我，从没有要求回报。我要告诉你我对此充满感激，而且想着你在特殊的场合可能会用到这个，就在这里！

## `ZagreusField_2845`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Unless...? Oh no, you're not making me take something in return, you know this was a gift not an exchange, sir, right? Wait, what is that?

**中文**

除非……？噢……不，这可不是什么物品交换，这是一件礼物啊，先生。等下，这是什么？

## `ZagreusField_2846`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Whoever it was you used to be, sir, I believe you've changed. And I'm proud to say that you're my friend. And Bouldy there as well. It's odd for me to say, but I am glad you're here. I think I've learned a lot from you, about all this.

**中文**

无论过去是怎样的人，大人，我相信你早已脱胎换骨了。作为你的朋友，还有巨石的朋友，我感到非常自豪。这么说听起来有点矫情，但我真的很庆幸有你在。我从你那里学到很多东西，关于这一切纷纷扰扰。

## `ZagreusField_2847`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You guys are great.

**中文**

你们真棒。

## `ZagreusField_2848`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He is, sir, and I'm grateful once again to have adopted him. You sure you don't want him back, though? Always seems like you could use a friend.

**中文**

是的，大人，我真的很高兴领养了他。你不会想把他要回去吧？你好像很需要朋友。

## `ZagreusField_2849`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sisyphus, I know you tend to be in remarkably good spirits, all things considered, but there must be something I can do to help with your predicament, isn't there?

**中文**

西西弗斯，我知道因为种种原因，你总是可以处于精神饱满的状态，但我肯定能做点什么，帮助你摆脱现在的困境吧？

## `ZagreusField_2850`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

But, what of the Furies and their torments and all that? They might be back and make all this more difficult for you.

**中文**

但复仇女神和她们对你的折磨呢？她们随时可能回来，让你更不好过。

## `ZagreusField_2851`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have good news, sir! I located the contract binding you, and bought it out. It's null and void. You're free to go!

**中文**

我有个好消息要告诉你，大人！我找到了制约你的契约，还把它给买下来了。现在它已经失去了效力。你自由了！

## `ZagreusField_2852`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Not just that, Sisyphus! You can make your way someplace else, where other decent shades reside! You know, somewhere with fewer chains and torture devices and such?

**中文**

不仅如此，西西弗斯！你现在可以去别的地方，去其他体面的暗灵居住的地方！比如，那些没有镣铐和刑具的地方？

## `ZagreusField_2853`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You mean you're really going to stay? Even though you don't have to?

**中文**

你是说你真的打算留下来？即使你没必要这样做？

## `ZagreusField_2854`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sisyphus, I have to be up front with you. The Fury Megaera and Thanatos... I know you're not exactly on the best of terms with either one of them. But they're both close to me. You won't hold it against me, sir, will you?

**中文**

西西弗斯，我必须和你实话实说。复仇女神墨纪拉和塔纳托斯……我知道你和他们都合不来。但他们对我来说很重要。你不会因此记恨我吧，大人？

## `ZagreusField_2855`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't even say that, sir. I think Meg and Than know me better than that by now. Well, thanks for understanding, as always.

**中文**

别这么说，大人。我相信以墨纪和塔纳现在对我的了解不至于出现这种情况。不过，还是要谢谢你这么善解人意。

## `ZagreusField_2856`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sisyphus, sir? Have you any idea how Shady works? I'm able to just whisk both you and Bouldy out of here, and to my aid? It must be awfully disorienting.

**中文**

西西弗斯大人，你知道沙迪是怎么回事吗？我可以把你和石头变到其他地方去来帮助我？你们明明留在这里，真让人费解。

## `ZagreusField_2857`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I thought you'd like it, sir. Almost a shame we seldom get such imports from Olympus around here. I hope I can appreciate it for myself sometime. Once all this is over with, I think.

**中文**

我想着你会喜欢的，先生。遗憾的是，我们这里很少能拿到这种来自奥林匹斯山的好东西。我希望有机会我也能亲自品尝一番。等这一切都尘埃落定吧。

## `ZagreusField_2858`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I made it out of here, indeed, sir. I always said that I was leaving, but... turns out there was good reason for me to come back. Not the least of which is visiting my friends from time to time.

**中文**

我确实去了外面的世界，先生。我以前总是说我要离开这个地方，可是……现在我又有理由回到这里了。不过，这样至少可以时不时地见见我的朋友们了。

## `ZagreusField_2859`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're right. I'm back because... I'm leaving once again. Something I have to do if I get up there, though I should be back before long either way, I guess.

**中文**

你说得没错，我回来是因为……我要再次离开这里。我在地面世界还有必须要做的事情，不过不管怎样，我猜我很快就能回来了。

## `ZagreusField_2860`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It isn't us she's hiding from up there. Father let her go. He never wanted to go after her. So I appreciate your concern, but I'll find her again.

**中文**

她想避而不见的人不是我们。是父亲把她放走了，他根本就没想过要去追她回来。所以，虽然我很感谢你的关心，但我会再次找到她的。

## `ZagreusField_2861`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That I'm her son...? It's true, sir. And she's back with us for now, indeed. I think we're all still getting used to it, but... it feels good, having her in my life.

**中文**

是说我是她的儿子吗……？是真的，先生，而且她确实回来了。我觉得所有人都还在适应这件事情，不过……能和她在一起，这种感觉很好。

## `ZagreusField_2862`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I seem to have this whole easy-to-underestimate thing about me, based on a decent sum of my relationships. In any case, I always welcome your appearances out here. I know it's not been easy for you, Than.

**中文**

根据以往的人际经验，我好像很容易被低估。不管怎样，欢迎你随时来这里。我知道这对你来说不容易，塔纳。

## `ZagreusField_2863`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...What, you're telling me this is it? You're just going to leave me to my own devices, then, I've proved myself to you, something like that?

**中文**

……什么？你是说就这样了吗？我不是已经向你证明自己了吗，你怎么还是要离我而去，你什么意思？

## `ZagreusField_2864`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It is all right. Thanks, Than.

**中文**

没事，谢谢了，塔纳。

## `ZagreusField_2865`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He's been great to have around, for sure. Still can't believe you found him after all this time! You used to always carry him around, I was so sad for you when he got lost.

**中文**

毫无疑问，他的确是个不错的同伴。很难想象在经历这么多事情之后，你竟然能够找到他！以前你总是把他带在身边，当时听说你把他弄丢了，我也很难过。

## `ZagreusField_2866`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Accurate.

**中文**

真准。

## `ZagreusField_2867`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You don't attack me indiscriminately, I give you Nectar. I don't think I'm being over-generous!

**中文**

你不要无缘无故攻击我，我就会送你这蜜露。我可不认为我这是慷慨过头！

## `ZagreusField_2868`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That is an ominous-looking portal on the ground right there.

**中文**

那边的传送门看上去很诡异。

## `ZagreusField_2869`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What is that portal thing there on the ground?

**中文**

那边那个传送门一样的东西是干什么的？

## `ZagreusField_2870`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

So all I need to do is sacrifice some of my life essence to go in here?

**中文**

所以，我必须牺牲一部分生命精华才能进去吗？

## `ZagreusField_2871`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Aaugh, don't see why not!

**中文**

啊啊，试试看吧！

## `ZagreusField_2872`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That looks like a Daedalus Hammer!

**中文**

看上去像是狄德勒斯之锤！

## `ZagreusField_2873`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Enchantments for my weapon...

**中文**

是武器附魔啊……

## `ZagreusField_2874`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Daedalus, wherever you may be right now, thank you.

**中文**

狄德勒斯，虽然不知道你现在在哪，但我得谢谢你。

## `ZagreusField_2875`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's one big Satyr...

**中文**

这萨提尔可不小啊……

## `ZagreusField_2876`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Another one of those gigantic Satyrs?

**中文**

又是一只巨型萨提尔？

## `ZagreusField_2877`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Go clop your hooves in hell.

**中文**

滚回地狱里蹬蹄子去吧。

## `ZagreusField_2878`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Get out of here!

**中文**

给我滚出这里！

## `ZagreusField_2879`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's the most gigantic vermin I have ever seen.

**中文**

这是我见过的最大的鼠兽了。

## `ZagreusField_2880`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Another one of those especially gigantic vermin!

**中文**

又是那种大型鼠兽！

## `ZagreusField_2881`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Yeugh, those things...

**中文**

呃呕，那群家伙……

## `ZagreusField_2882`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Where are they coming from...

**中文**

它们都是从哪里来的……

## `ZagreusField_2883`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What sort of terrible Snakestone is that?

**中文**

这都是什么鬼蛇石啊？

## `ZagreusField_2884`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's that giant Snakestone once again!

**中文**

又是那个巨型蛇石！

## `ZagreusField_2885`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Satyrs must be fashioning those things...

**中文**

萨提尔应该很崇拜那些东西……

## `ZagreusField_2886`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's quiet, finally...

**中文**

终于安静下来了……

## `ZagreusField_2887`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't like the look of that big Bother over there.

**中文**

我可不太喜欢那边那个大恶灵妖。

## `ZagreusField_2888`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That blasted giant Bother has come back.

**中文**

那只该死的大恶灵妖又回来了。

## `ZagreusField_2889`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whew, looks like I'm clear.

**中文**

呼，看来敌人已经清理干净了。

## `ZagreusField_2890`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What even are those things...

**中文**

那些怪物到底是什么玩意儿……

## `ZagreusField_2891`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This blasted place again.

**中文**

又是这个鬼地方。

## `ZagreusField_2892`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's one of their lairs.

**中文**

是它们的巢穴。

## `ZagreusField_2893`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to deal with Father's pest problem I guess.

**中文**

该整治一下父亲说的虫害问题了。

## `ZagreusField_2894`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

One of their disgusting lairs, huh.

**中文**

又是一个恶心的巢穴，呃。

## `ZagreusField_2895`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Can't quite get acclimated to this smell.

**中文**

我真是适应不了这里的味道。

## `ZagreusField_2896`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

And Father thinks my chambers are a mess!

**中文**

父亲竟然还觉得我的房间乱！

## `ZagreusField_2897`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

My father is to thank for that, not me. I've proven difficult for him to contain, so now I'm beneficiary of his latest Pact of Punishment. You're welcome!

**中文**

是我的父亲，不是我。对他来说，我是个不好管理的孩子，所以现在，我成了他最新的惩罚契约的受益者。不要客气！

## `ZagreusField_2898`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't suppose you have some good acquaintances with whom to train, until such time as you see fit to face me once again?

**中文**

我觉得，我们再见面之前，你找不到合适的熟人来训练你？

## `ZagreusField_2899`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, my father keeps the dress code very strict down there in Tartarus. No armor in the House! Never tried the stuff.

**中文**

你也知道，我的父亲在塔耳塔罗斯执行着一套非常严格的着装要求。在圣殿里不许穿护甲！这东西，永远都没想过。

## `ZagreusField_2900`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Fine change of pace, Asterius! Who needs that old, spectacular, custom-fitting suit of legendary armor, anyway?

**中文**

真是不错的改变，阿斯忒里俄斯！谁会需要那身老旧，但却很华丽合身的传奇护甲呢？

## `ZagreusField_2901`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Great! Thanks for the encouragement, Asterius. Don't let me keep you waiting on that, then.

**中文**

好！谢谢你的鼓励，阿斯忒里俄斯。那么，别让我再耽搁你了。

## `ZagreusField_2902`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

They took a lot out of me, yes. But you have no idea just how long and arduous a trip it is from Tartarus to here!

**中文**

它们让我损失惨重，没错。但你不知道从塔耳塔罗斯到这里的道路有多么漫长而艰难！

## `ZagreusField_2903`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We have this!

**中文**

成了！

## `ZagreusField_2904`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You definitely ought!

**中文**

你应得的！

## `ZagreusField_2905`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Or I'll slay you instead if you prefer!

**中文**

如果你想的话，我把你杀了也行！

## `ZagreusField_2906`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not me.

**中文**

别是我。

## `ZagreusField_2907`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lesson one: Don't mess with either one of us!

**中文**

第一课：别惹我们中的任何一个！

## `ZagreusField_2908`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I know, right?

**中文**

没错吧？

## `ZagreusField_2909`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's see, indeed!

**中文**

没错，让我们来看看吧！

## `ZagreusField_2910`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Race you, Than!

**中文**

来比一场吧，塔纳！

## `ZagreusField_2911`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No doubt at all from me.

**中文**

我一点也没怀疑过。

## `ZagreusField_2912`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

And here they fall!

**中文**

它们完蛋了！

## `ZagreusField_2913`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This gigantic vermin head deserves a lot more credit if you ask me.

**中文**

要说我，这个巨型鼠兽的头已经很能说明问题了。

## `ZagreusField_2914`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Must be the Fountain Chamber I commissioned back at home...

**中文**

这肯定是我在家里委托建造的泉水房间了……

## `ZagreusField_2915`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ah good, this has to be the chamber renovation I put in.

**中文**

啊，真不错，这一定是我下令建造的房间。

## `ZagreusField_2916`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Glad I put in the order for a Fountain Chamber in this area.

**中文**

我在这片地方建了个泉水房间，我真是太明智了。

## `ZagreusField_2917`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That must be one of those Infernal Troves!

**中文**

那肯定就是地狱宝藏了！

## `ZagreusField_2918`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My Keepsake Collection! Just what the House Contractor ordered.

**中文**

是我的信物收集品！正是圣殿承包商要的。

## `ZagreusField_2919`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That Darkness there felt different than before.

**中文**

那块黑暗宝石感觉跟之前不太一样。

## `ZagreusField_2920`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That key, it granted me Fated Authority.

**中文**

那把钥匙可以让我逆天改命。

## `ZagreusField_2921`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Gemstones, and a little extra for Charon.

**中文**

是宝石，可以带一点给卡戎。

## `ZagreusField_2922`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That Nectar also amplified one of my Boons.

**中文**

蜜露可以增强我的一项祝福。

## `ZagreusField_2923`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's him.

**中文**

是他。

## `ZagreusField_2924`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's him...!

**中文**

是他……！

## `ZagreusField_2925`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Than...!

**中文**

塔纳……！

## `ZagreusField_2926`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Here he comes.

**中文**

他来了。

## `ZagreusField_2927`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Death approaches?

**中文**

死亡靠近？

## `ZagreusField_2928`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I know that toll.

**中文**

我知道价钱。

## `ZagreusField_2929`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Come on in, Than.

**中文**

进来，塔纳。

## `ZagreusField_2930`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got to go!

**中文**

该出发了！

## `ZagreusField_2931`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll just be going!

**中文**

我要出发了！

## `ZagreusField_2932`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, see you!

**中文**

好吧，再见！

## `ZagreusField_2933`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oops I got to run!

**中文**

哦哦！我得走了！

## `ZagreusField_2934`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bye-bye!

**中文**

拜拜！

## `ZagreusField_2935`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Begging your pardon!

**中文**

不好意思啦！

## `ZagreusField_2936`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't think that's going to work!

**中文**

这样估计不行！

## `ZagreusField_2937`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Can't do that here!

**中文**

在这里不行！

## `ZagreusField_2938`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You sure you won't assist me here, Meg?

**中文**

你真的不打算帮我吗，墨纪？

## `ZagreusField_2939`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That doesn't work right now!

**中文**

那个现在还没用！

## `ZagreusField_2940`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Than can't help me here!

**中文**

塔纳在这里帮不了我！

## `ZagreusField_2941`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Meg can't help me here!

**中文**

墨纪在这里帮不了我！

## `ZagreusField_2942`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I can't do that right now!

**中文**

我现在还不能那么做！

## `ZagreusField_2943`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Achilles might know what to do with this.

**中文**

阿喀琉斯或许会知道这个怎么用。

## `ZagreusField_2944`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This holds some sort of power, but for what?

**中文**

这东西蕴含着力量，但这力量是做什么用的？

## `ZagreusField_2945`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That a genuine Ambrosia bottle there?

**中文**

是一瓶货真价实的仙馔密酒吗？

## `ZagreusField_2946`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Huh, I can't seem to open it...

**中文**

呃，好像打不开的样子……

## `ZagreusField_2947`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Must be something I can do with this.

**中文**

我应该能对它做点什么才对。

## `ZagreusField_2948`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Charon mate, now hypothetically, if I provided you with, say, a thousand coins, would you be willing to give me a ride in your beautiful boat? Upriver, I should say, specifically.

**中文**

卡戎老兄，现在我们假设一下，如果我给你，嗯，一千个硬币，你能用你那漂亮的船送我一程吗？咱们往上游走，如果可以的话。

## `ZagreusField_2949`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I had to ask...

**中文**

我得问问了……

## `ZagreusField_2950`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I never! Look, I'm simply pleased to see you, Meg. Hence the insuppressible smile. Could have been one of your lovely sisters here instead...

**中文**

从来没有！我只是单纯的见到你很开心，墨纪，于是就情不自禁笑了起来。要知道，当时来的也可能会是你的姐妹啊……

## `ZagreusField_2951`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

OK!

**中文**

好了！

## `ZagreusField_2952`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

OK?

**中文**

确定？

## `ZagreusField_2953`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Indeed.

**中文**

没错。

## `ZagreusField_2954`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well...?

**中文**

那么……?

## `ZagreusField_2955`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good chat!

**中文**

聊得很尽兴！

## `ZagreusField_2956`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Pleasure!

**中文**

真愉快！

## `ZagreusField_2957`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Huh.

**中文**

嗯。

## `ZagreusField_2958`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks?

**中文**

谢谢？

## `ZagreusField_2959`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks!

**中文**

谢谢！

## `ZagreusField_2960`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cheers?

**中文**

多谢了？

## `ZagreusField_2961`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cheers!

**中文**

多谢了！

## `ZagreusField_2962`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Brr, that's cold...

**中文**

呃，好冷啊……

## `ZagreusField_2963`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Last time I felt a cold like this was... on the surface, but, who...? Well, one way to find out, then, in the name of Hades! Olympus! I accept this message!

**中文**

上次我感觉这么冷的时候还是在……地表，但是，会是谁呢……？好吧，以哈迪斯之名，看来只有一种方法能弄清楚了！奥林匹斯，我收到这条消息了！

## `ZagreusField_2964`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Are all the seasons on the surface freezing cold?

**中文**

地面的季节都这么刺骨的寒冷吗？

## `ZagreusField_2965`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Brr, that is cold...

**中文**

呃，好冷啊……

## `ZagreusField_2966`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Last time I felt a chill like this was... on the surface, but, who...? Well, one way to find out. Then, in the name of Hades! Olympus, I accept this message!

**中文**

上次我感觉这么冷的时候还是在……地表，但是，会是谁呢……？好吧，以哈迪斯之名，看来只有一种方法能弄清楚了！奥林匹斯，我收到这条消息了！

## `ZagreusField_2967`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I've the power of the freezing surface, now.

**中文**

现在，我有冰封的地表之力量了。

## `ZagreusField_2968`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What? I don't... wait, this everlasting winter, it's her doing, isn't it? Why? Don't mortals die when it's cold? Sounds like a lot of extra work for you.

**中文**

什么？我没有……等等，这场阴魂不散的寒冬都是她搞出来的吗？这是怎么回事？凡人不是会冻死的吗？听起来给你增加了不少工作量啊。

## `ZagreusField_2969`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

She lost her daughter... who's my mother. But, Demeter still thinks I'm Nyx's son, and... what have you done, Father? This everlasting winter, it's her doing, isn't it? Freezing mortals to death. Why don't you stop this?

**中文**

她失去了自己的女儿……也就是我的母亲。但是，得墨忒尔仍然认为我是倪克斯的孩子，而且……你到底做了什么啊，父亲？这场无尽的寒冬是她弄出来的，是吧？凡人都冻死了，你为什么你不去阻止她？

## `ZagreusField_2970`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, just took a splash of Titan Blood at the right time was all. There's more to your ancient weapon than you gave it credit for, I guess. Though let's not keep it waiting, no?

**中文**

噢，只是适时地吸收了一点泰坦之血罢了。看来你也不是很了解你口中的远古武器啊，不过咱们闲话少说，开打吧？

## `ZagreusField_2971`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lady Demeter...

**中文**

得墨忒尔女士……

## `ZagreusField_2972`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Goddess Demeter...

**中文**

得墨忒尔女神……

## `ZagreusField_2973`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's Demeter...

**中文**

是得墨忒尔……

## `ZagreusField_2974`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's Lady Demeter...

**中文**

是得墨忒尔女士……

## `ZagreusField_2975`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Grandmother Demeter...

**中文**

得墨忒尔姥姥……

## `ZagreusField_2976`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My Grandmother...

**中文**

我的毑母……

## `ZagreusField_2977`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Brr, that's brisk!!

**中文**

呃，凉飕飕的！！

## `ZagreusField_2978`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mm, that's cold.

**中文**

嗯，好冷。

## `ZagreusField_2979`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whoo, that woke me up!

**中文**

呼，这下清醒了！

## `ZagreusField_2980`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Brr, perks you right up!

**中文**

噢，马上就清醒了！

## `ZagreusField_2981`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The winter's chill.

**中文**

冬天的寒冷。

## `ZagreusField_2982`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ice cold.

**中文**

冰冷。

## `ZagreusField_2983`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That beats the heat.

**中文**

这样就不会热了。

## `ZagreusField_2984`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Icy.

**中文**

冰。

## `ZagreusField_2985`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cool, cool.

**中文**

很凉爽，很凉爽。

## `ZagreusField_2986`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Very cool.

**中文**

非常凉爽。

## `ZagreusField_2987`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm in your debt, great Goddess Demeter.

**中文**

我欠你一份人情，伟大的得墨忒尔女神。

## `ZagreusField_2988`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm sorry, Demeter.

**中文**

对不起，得墨忒尔。

## `ZagreusField_2989`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Forgive me, Demeter.

**中文**

原谅我，得墨忒尔。

## `ZagreusField_2990`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm sorry, Grandmother.

**中文**

对不起，姥姥。

## `ZagreusField_2991`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm sure that Demeter won't mind.

**中文**

得墨忒尔肯定不会介意。

## `ZagreusField_2992`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hope Demeter won't mind.

**中文**

希望得墨忒尔不会介意。

## `ZagreusField_2993`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lady Demeter won't mind, will she?

**中文**

得墨忒尔女士应该不会介意的吧？

## `ZagreusField_2994`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Appreciate the offer, Lady Demeter.

**中文**

感谢您的馈赠，得墨忒尔女士。

## `ZagreusField_2995`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I can't refuse this, Lady Demeter.

**中文**

这我无法拒绝，得墨忒尔女士。

## `ZagreusField_2996`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good Lady Demeter, I must accept.

**中文**

得墨忒尔女士，我会接受的。

## `ZagreusField_2997`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Demeter!!

**中文**

得墨忒尔！！

## `ZagreusField_2998`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Demeter...!

**中文**

得墨忒尔……！

## `ZagreusField_2999`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Good Lady Demeter, you've shown me warmth and generosity, and I am very grateful for it. Please, accept this as a small token of my appreciation.

**中文**

得墨忒尔女士，您带给了我温暖与慷慨，我非常感激。请务必接受这件代表感谢之情的小小薄礼。

## `ZagreusField_3000`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

A little offering for you, good Lady Demeter. I'm deeply grateful for your willingness to aid me in all this.

**中文**

这是给您的小礼物，得墨忒尔女士。对于你给予的帮助，我深表谢意。

## `ZagreusField_3001`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

May this small offering bring you some joy there on Olympus, Lady Demeter. Thank you for all your help.

**中文**

希望这件不起眼的礼物能为您在奥林匹斯山上的生活带来愉悦，得墨忒尔女士。感谢您的帮助。

## `ZagreusField_3002`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Your power and your wisdom has been of such help, good Lady Demeter. I'm honored, and this is the least I can do in exchange.

**中文**

您的神力和智慧对起了很大帮助，我的好女士得墨忒尔。我很荣幸，这是我最起码该做的。

## `ZagreusField_3003`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wait... Persephone... Mother... is Demeter's daughter?

**中文**

等等……珀尔塞福涅……就是说我母亲……是得墨忒尔的女儿？

## `ZagreusField_3004`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Winter... that's when it's cold, or something, right?

**中文**

冬天……就是当天气很冷，或什么的，是吧？

## `ZagreusField_3005`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Could dash clear through that wall I think...

**中文**

我应该可以直接撞破那堵墙……

## `ZagreusField_3006`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Dusa!

**中文**

杜莎！

## `ZagreusField_3007`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Dusa?

**中文**

杜莎？

## `ZagreusField_3008`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Need you, Dusa!

**中文**

需要你，杜莎！

## `ZagreusField_3009`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You hear me, Dusa?

**中文**

你听得到我吗，杜莎？

## `ZagreusField_3010`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Dusa, you there?

**中文**

杜莎，你在吗？

## `ZagreusField_3011`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Dusa, help!

**中文**

杜莎，帮帮我！

## `ZagreusField_3012`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thank you!!

**中文**

谢谢！！

## `ZagreusField_3013`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I owe you one!

**中文**

我欠你个人情！

## `ZagreusField_3014`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

OK good-bye!

**中文**

好的，再见！

## `ZagreusField_3015`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We'll chat later OK?

**中文**

晚点再聊好吗？

## `ZagreusField_3016`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thank you for that!

**中文**

谢谢你来帮忙！

## `ZagreusField_3017`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wasn't this whole expanse once lush and green?

**中文**

我记得这里应该是一片郁郁葱葱的广阔土地啊？

## `ZagreusField_3018`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Bloodless lie in wait out there I guess.

**中文**

那些无血僵尸应该在外等待着吧。

## `ZagreusField_3019`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's nice and warm out here, I'll give it that.

**中文**

我得承认，这里很暖和。

## `ZagreusField_3020`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Just a few rides across the Phlegethon, and up we go.

**中文**

再坚持一会就穿过火焰河了，然后我们要沿河而上。

## `ZagreusField_3021`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That blasted Hydra must be back by now.

**中文**

那个该死的九头蛇肯定已经回去了。

## `ZagreusField_3022`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This place has definitely seen better days.

**中文**

这个地方以前的光景应该会好得多吧。

## `ZagreusField_3023`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This place has definitely seen better days. Or nights?

**中文**

这个地方以前的光景应该会好得多吧，或者说，夜景？

## `ZagreusField_3024`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks for believing in me, my good Shade!

**中文**

感谢你能信任我，我的好暗灵！

## `ZagreusField_3025`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I dedicate this victory to you, good Shade!

**中文**

这场胜利应该属于你，我的好暗灵！

## `ZagreusField_3026`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That shade there, have I got a fan?

**中文**

那边有个暗灵，难道我有粉丝了？

## `ZagreusField_3027`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hello, supportive Shade!

**中文**

你好啊，帮助我的暗灵！

## `ZagreusField_3028`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I won again, Shade, thanks to your support!

**中文**

我又赢了，谢谢你的帮忙，暗灵！

## `ZagreusField_3142`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bone Hydra, you're back! How wonderful.

**中文**

骸骨九头蛇，你回来了，真好！

## `ZagreusField_3143`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hydra, I feel like we're just not getting through to one another, here.

**中文**

九头蛇，我觉得咱俩之间就是没办法沟通。

## `ZagreusField_3144`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bone Hydra, how come we never talk?

**中文**

骸骨九头蛇，咱俩怎么从来都没聊过呢？

## `ZagreusField_3145`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bone Hydra, if you understand me, menacingly hiss.

**中文**

骸骨九头蛇，要是你听懂我的话，就发出嘶嘶的声音吧。

## `ZagreusField_3146`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bone Hydra, I hope you know this isn't personal, don't you?

**中文**

骸骨九头蛇，我和你之间没什么私人恩怨，希望你能明白，好吗？

## `ZagreusField_3147`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bone Hydra... can I call you Bone Hydra? Lernie, maybe? Lernie, I like that.

**中文**

骸骨九头蛇……叫你骸骨九头蛇怎么样？或者叫你勒拿？勒拿，嗯，这名字挺不错的。

## `ZagreusField_3148`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You don't mind if I call you Lernie from now on, right?

**中文**

那我以后都叫你勒拿好了，可以吧？

## `ZagreusField_3149`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll take that as a 'No, not at all, thank you for asking, friend.'

**中文**

那我就当你是在说：”没问题，我一点都不介意。感谢你征求我的意见，朋友。“

## `ZagreusField_3150`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Why, hello, Lernie, what have you been up to since I last destroyed your multitude of heads?

**中文**

你好啊，勒拿。我把你的头砍掉之后，你过得还好吗？

## `ZagreusField_3151`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good day, Lernie, and now? Prepare to die.

**中文**

你好啊，勒拿，准备去死吧。

## `ZagreusField_3152`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How goes it lately, Lernie, everything just fine?

**中文**

最近怎么样啊，勒拿，一切还好吗？

## `ZagreusField_3153`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm back, Lernie, to kill you once again!

**中文**

我回来了，勒拿，我要再杀你一次！

## `ZagreusField_3154`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

If only you knew just how I feel, Lernie.

**中文**

要是你能理解我的感受就好了，勒拿。

## `ZagreusField_3155`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I know you missed me, Lernie, and I missed you, too!

**中文**

你一定想死我了吧，勒拿，我也想你！

## `ZagreusField_3156`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lernie, wonderful to see you once again!

**中文**

勒拿，真高兴能再见到你呀！

## `ZagreusField_3157`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What's the matter, Lernie? You seem quite upset!

**中文**

怎么了，勒拿？你看起来挺不高兴的！

## `ZagreusField_3158`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How is the magma temperature lately, Lernie?

**中文**

最近岩浆的温度还舒适吗，勒拿？

## `ZagreusField_3159`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lernie, if you'd like for me to kill you, hiss.

**中文**

勒拿，如果你想让我弄死你，就嘶嘶叫两声。

## `ZagreusField_3160`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lernie, hiss once if you think you're going to lose.

**中文**

勒拿，如果你觉得自己快不行了，就嘶嘶叫两声。

## `ZagreusField_3161`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time for more heads?

**中文**

该砍头了吗？

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**中文**

该砍头啦。

**EN**

**中文**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**中文**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's bash some heads.

**中文**

**EN**

**EN**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Done bashing heads for now.

**中文**

暂时没别的头要敲烂了。

## `ZagreusField_3169`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Heads down.

**中文**

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's all the heads.

**中文**

就这些头了。

## `ZagreusField_3171`

**EN**

No more heads.

**中文**

Why, thank you.

**中文**

谢谢你啊。

## `ZagreusField_3321`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There.

**中文**

好了。

## `ZagreusField_3322`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Just what I wanted.

**中文**

正合我的心意。

## `ZagreusField_3323`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thank the Fates.

**中文**

感谢命运。

## `ZagreusField_3324`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good, good.

**中文**

不错，不错。

## `ZagreusField_3325`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ah, yes.

**中文**

啊，不错。

## `ZagreusField_3326`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My reward.

**中文**

我的奖励。

## `ZagreusField_3327`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

For me?

**中文**

是给我的吗？

## `ZagreusField_3328`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm sure you'll think of something soon enough.

**中文**

我敢肯定，你很快就会想起什么的。

## `ZagreusField_3329`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You stay away from me.

**中文**

你离我远点。

## `ZagreusField_3330`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Headed over to Elysium right now...

**中文**

现在就朝至福乐土前进吧……

## `ZagreusField_3331`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Who needs your rivers.

**中文**

谁想要你的河水？

## `ZagreusField_3332`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...Ceaseless chatter.

**中文**

……你可真是絮絮叨叨。

## `ZagreusField_3333`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I've anger enough.

**中文**

我已经生气了。

## `ZagreusField_3334`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not afraid of you or your damn spear.

**中文**

我可不怕你，也不怕你那根破矛。

## `ZagreusField_3335`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Fear is for the weak...

**中文**

恐惧是弱者的专利……

## `ZagreusField_3336`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's find some gemstones then.

**中文**

我们看看能不能找到宝石吧。

## `ZagreusField_3337`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Guess I'll see what I can do about it, Father.

**中文**

我得看看能不能做点什么了，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_3338`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No one will worship you.

**中文**

没人会崇拜你的。

## `ZagreusField_3339`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What is this...

**中文**

这是什么玩意儿……

## `ZagreusField_3340`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right, let's catch some fish.

**中文**

好啦，该钓几条鱼了。

## `ZagreusField_3341`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You are my biggest fan!

**中文**

你可真是我的铁粉啊！

## `ZagreusField_3342`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

To my greatest fan!

**中文**

敬我最铁的粉！

## `ZagreusField_3343`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There's my Shade!

**中文**

我的幽灵来啦！

## `ZagreusField_3344`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That was for you, good Shade!

**中文**

这一下是为你打的，我的好幽灵！

## `ZagreusField_3345`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hope you enjoyed the show, my good Shade!

**中文**

希望你还喜欢这场表演，我的好暗灵！

## `ZagreusField_3346`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thank you for your support, my Shade!

**中文**

感谢你的帮忙，我的暗灵！

## `ZagreusField_3347`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We got them, my good Shade!

**中文**

我们打败他们了，我的好暗灵！

## `ZagreusField_3348`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Showed them who's boss, didn't we, Shade?

**中文**

他们这下明白谁才是老大了，是吧，暗灵？

## `ZagreusField_3349`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got them again, my Shade!

**中文**

又打败他们了，我的暗灵！

## `ZagreusField_3350`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Was not about to let you down, my Shade!

**中文**

我可没打算让你失望啊，我的暗灵！

## `ZagreusField_3351`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Makes up for last time I hope, my good Shade?

**中文**

希望能弥补上回的遗憾，我的好暗灵。

## `ZagreusField_3352`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Until next time, good Shade!

**中文**

下次再见，我的好暗灵！

## `ZagreusField_3353`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Uh....

**中文**

呃……

## `ZagreusField_3354`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, haha! It's just a tiny vermin!

**中文**

噢，哈哈！不过是只小鼠兽！

## `ZagreusField_3355`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wha, aah!

**中文**

哇，啊啊！

## `ZagreusField_3356`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh gods!

**中文**

我的天啊！

## `ZagreusField_3357`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That... what... I...

**中文**

那……什么……我……

## `ZagreusField_3358`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Is it dead...?

**中文**

它死了吗……？

## `ZagreusField_3359`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Eugh, that thing...

**中文**

呃，那玩意……

## `ZagreusField_3360`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That vicious little...

**中文**

那只凶狠的小东西……

## `ZagreusField_3361`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I much prefer gigantic vermin, thanks...

**中文**

相比之下，我还是更喜欢大鼠兽，多谢了……

## `ZagreusField_3362`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I got you, tiny vermin.

**中文**

逮着你了，小鼠兽。

## `ZagreusField_3363`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You don't scare me, tiny vermin!

**中文**

你可吓不到我，小鼠兽！

## `ZagreusField_3364`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's dead...

**中文**

它死了……

## `ZagreusField_3365`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's dead... I think...?

**中文**

它死了……大概吧……？

## `ZagreusField_3366`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh no.

**中文**

哦，不。

## `ZagreusField_3367`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wait, it's back?

**中文**

等等，它又回来了？

## `ZagreusField_3368`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No, not the tiny vermin.

**中文**

不，别来这些小鼠兽啊。

## `ZagreusField_3369`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No, anything but the tiny vermin...!

**中文**

不，别来这些小鼠兽啊……！

## `ZagreusField_3370`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The tiny vermin!

**中文**

是小鼠兽！

## `ZagreusField_3371`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Come forth, you tiny vermin!

**中文**

放马过来吧，你们这些小鼠兽！

## `ZagreusField_3372`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh no!

**中文**

噢！不！

## `ZagreusField_3373`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

So you're the ringleader!

**中文**

看来，你就是老大了吧！

## `ZagreusField_3374`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Where'd you go?

**中文**

你要去哪？

## `ZagreusField_3375`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Is it gone...?

**中文**

它不见了……？

## `ZagreusField_3376`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Where'd it go?

**中文**

它去哪里了？

## `ZagreusField_3377`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's underground...

**中文**

它在地下……

## `ZagreusField_3378`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Where is it...

**中文**

它在哪里..……

## `ZagreusField_3379`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ooh.

**中文**

噢。

## `ZagreusField_3380`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Heyy...

**中文**

嘿……

## `ZagreusField_3381`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hoh...

**中文**

嚯……

## `ZagreusField_3382`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mm, hmm.

**中文**

嗯，嗯。

## `ZagreusField_3383`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mmm!

**中文**

嗯！

## `ZagreusField_3384`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right...

**中文**

好吧……

## `ZagreusField_3385`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nice...

**中文**

不错……

## `ZagreusField_3386`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The goods.

**中文**

很不错。

## `ZagreusField_3387`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ooh, good.

**中文**

哦哦，不错。

## `ZagreusField_3388`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ah, yes.

**中文**

啊，很好。

## `ZagreusField_3389`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Yes...

**中文**

很好……

## `ZagreusField_3390`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whoo...

**中文**

呼噢……

## `ZagreusField_3391`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Interesting...

**中文**

有意思……

## `ZagreusField_3392`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hmm...

**中文**

嗯……

## `ZagreusField_3393`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I see you drawn in the arms of the seventh avatar...

**中文**

我看到第七化身的手将你拉开……

## `ZagreusField_3394`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

A waking-phrase... and an all-seeing artifact, you must mean Aegis, right? So I'm to say to it, I see you stand your ground against the serpent's flame, and it'll wake right up. Though, can you tell me what it means, or who?

**中文**

唤醒咒语……还有全视圣器，你指的是埃癸斯，是吧？只要我对它说：“我看到你傲然屹立在巨蛇的烈焰之中，”它就会被唤醒。不过，你可以告诉我这句话是什么意思吗？是谁说的？

## `ZagreusField_3395`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Master Chaos, it's little wonder all existence sprang from you, because I see the noble qualities in so many I've met embodied in you. Please, accept this gift.

**中文**

卡俄斯君主，一切生命都起源于您，这一点毫无疑问，因为我在您身上看到了我所认识的人所具有的全部优点。请收下这份礼物吧。

## `ZagreusField_3396`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This is the greatest delicacy of Olympus, Master Chaos. It's for you. I think normally it would be difficult for the Olympians to get it to you here.

**中文**

这是给您的礼物，卡俄斯君主，是来自奥林匹斯最为精贵的物品。通常来说，想把它从奥林匹斯山运到您这里来，还是很困难的。

## `ZagreusField_3397`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I just think the act of giving isn't quite the same as an exchange. I know I won't be getting any sort of other talisman or something in return. But, through these offerings, I can express my thanks. That's worth it by itself.

**中文**

……我觉得给予跟交换是两码事。我知道不会再得到护身符之类的东西。但是通过这种给予，我可以表达自己的谢意，这样就足够了。

## `ZagreusField_3398`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

If you'll forgive me, Master Chaos, this Ambrosia is intended to be given to those whom I care about, and I can think of few recipients I'd like to have it more. Please.

**中文**

抱歉打扰，卡俄斯君主。这瓶仙馔密酒是送给我所关心的人的礼物，而我想不到有谁能比您更配得上它了。请吧。

## `ZagreusField_3399`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't even mention it, please, Master Chaos. All of this is new for me, as well. I try never to overanalyze... my father does enough of that for all of us. I just know, when I encounter the primordial creator, I am going to give thanks. For everything.

**中文**

没什么大不了的，卡俄斯君主。这一切对我来说也是全新的。我试着不去过度解读大家……父亲对我们的过度解读已经太多太多。我只知道，如果我遇到了原初的造物主，我一定要对其表示感谢，感谢这所有一切。

## `ZagreusField_3400`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I... do not know how to respond to that, exactly, Master Chaos, but, Nyx, yes, she has been aiding me, in my attempts to flee my father's realm.

**中文**

我……我不知道该怎么答复，卡俄斯君主。不过，确实是倪克斯，她一直在帮助我，帮助我逃离我父亲的国度。

## `ZagreusField_3401`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, Master Chaos, she can be a little difficult to read at times, but... I got the impression she was quite surprised, even excited to have heard from you again. She said that she would think about how to respond. Or, whether to respond.

**中文**

嗯，卡俄斯君主，她最近可能没什么空闲，不过……又收到了你的信，她感到很惊讶，甚至可以说是激动。她说她会考虑怎么答复您的，或者说，要不要答复您。

## `ZagreusField_3402`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, it's just... I sometimes wonder, Master Chaos... if you created all of this, then... who created you? What was this place before you arrived?

**中文**

噢，就是……卡俄斯君主，有时候我会想……如果是您创造了这一切，那么……是谁创造了您呢？在您诞生之前，这地方是什么样子？

## `ZagreusField_3403`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I do. She's somewhere on the surface. That's why I keep on finding you like this. I'm trying to find her.

**中文**

是的，她就在地面世界，这就是为什么我一直在找你的原因。我想要找到她。

## `ZagreusField_3404`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh! I... Master Chaos, I don't know what to expect from there. But, to be direct with you, if I could leave the Underworld forever... I think I would. Though, I would miss you. Many others here.

**中文**

噢！我……卡俄斯君主，我也不知道接下来会发生什么，但我可以对你实话实说。如果我能永远离开冥界的话……我想我会这么做的。不过我会想念你，也会想念很多这边的人。

## `ZagreusField_3405`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's see what else.

**中文**

看看还有什么别的。

## `ZagreusField_3406`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What else is there?

**中文**

那儿还有什么？

## `ZagreusField_3407`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'd like to see more options.

**中文**

要是还有其它选择就好了。

## `ZagreusField_3408`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What about these?

**中文**

这些如何？

## `ZagreusField_3409`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What else?

**中文**

还有什么？

## `ZagreusField_3410`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That a fishing point?

**中文**

那里能钓鱼吗？

## `ZagreusField_3411`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A fishing point, here?

**中文**

这里可以钓鱼？

## `ZagreusField_3412`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Fates aren't having it.

**中文**

命运之神是不会允许的。

## `ZagreusField_3413`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Fates won't budge.

**中文**

命运之神寸步不让。

## `ZagreusField_3414`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Fates won't let me.

**中文**

命运之神不会允许我那么做。

## `ZagreusField_3415`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Fates aren't letting me.

**中文**

命运之神不许我这么做。

## `ZagreusField_3416`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Fates say no.

**中文**

命运之神说不行。

## `ZagreusField_3417`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Fates say no I guess.

**中文**

我猜命运之神是不会同意的。

## `ZagreusField_3418`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Fates won't have it.

**中文**

命运之神是不会允许的。

## `ZagreusField_3419`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ah, come on.

**中文**

啊，拜托。

## `ZagreusField_3420`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I guess the Fates won't let me try that here.

**中文**

我猜，命运之神不会允许我那么做。

## `ZagreusField_3421`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Malphon hungers.

**中文**

玛尔封饿了。

## `ZagreusField_3422`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I think we've grown quite close to one another, yes. Enough to where I've come to know a different side of this fine blade right here. This is the aspect of some Arthur fellow! Maybe the two of you could get acquainted properly?

**中文**

我觉得我们之间已经有了足够的羁绊，足以让我发现这把利刃的另一面。这就是某个叫亚瑟的形态！或许你们两个可以好好认识一下？

## `ZagreusField_3423`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...That's Orpheus!

**中文**

……是俄耳甫斯！

## `ZagreusField_3424`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...They're both here...

**中文**

……他俩都在……

## `ZagreusField_3425`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bye, Than.

**中文**

再见，塔纳。

## `ZagreusField_3426`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See you, Than...

**中文**

回见，塔纳……

## `ZagreusField_3427`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There he goes.

**中文**

他来了。

## `ZagreusField_3428`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

And he's off.

**中文**

他又走了。

## `ZagreusField_3429`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wish I could do that.

**中文**

要是我也会这招就好了。

## `ZagreusField_3430`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks for the help.

**中文**

感谢帮忙。

## `ZagreusField_3431`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

At the risk of incurring your wrath, Lady Artemis, I hope you will accept this from me, and see that my gratitude for all you have done for me is genuine.

**中文**

希望这不会冒犯到您，阿尔忒弥斯女士。请务必收下这礼物，我对你一直以来的帮助表示由衷的感谢。

## `ZagreusField_3432`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

In your honor, Lady Artemis! Your mighty blessings have helped me through so many trials now, and I always welcome your level-headed presence here.

**中文**

我尊敬的阿尔忒弥斯女士！您强大的恩赐帮我度过了如此多的艰难险阻，我一直都很欢迎您这样冷静的人大驾光临。

## `ZagreusField_3433`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I know you have no shortage of Ambrosia on Olympus, Lady Artemis, but I imagine it's less often that the stuff go all the way from there to here, and back. What can I say? You make me proud to be a member of this family.

**中文**

我知道你们奥林匹斯从来不缺仙馔密酒，但是我想，从冥界送过来的仙馔密酒可能不太多吧。我能说什么呢？能成为您的大家族的一份子，我感到很骄傲。

## `ZagreusField_3434`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Please, Lord Uncle Zeus, accept this gift of Nectar in your honor, though it is such a pittance in comparison to all the generosity you've shown.

**中文**

拜托了，宙斯叔叔。尽管这件礼物与您展现出的慷慨相比，实在是微不足道，但也还是请您收下这蜜露吧。

## `ZagreusField_3435`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

If you can hear me, mighty Uncle Zeus, I wish to make to you this simple gift, and with it send my humble wish that someday you and Father settle all your differences, and be at peace.

**中文**

如果您可以听见我的声音，强大的宙斯叔叔啊，我希望赠与您这份薄礼，并且衷心祝愿您和我的父亲终有一天可以化解矛盾，重归于好。

## `ZagreusField_3436`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

King of the Olympians, Lord Zeus! Forgive this offer of Ambrosia, for I know you have no limit of it there. But here, it is the finest gift that can be given, and even still it isn't anything compared to all you've done for me. Thank you, Uncle.

**中文**

众神之王，宙斯大人！请接受这不起眼的仙馔密酒。我知道这东西对您来说再平常不过了，但在这里，这已经是最上等的礼物了。即便如此，这仍然比不上您为我所作的一切。谢谢您，叔叔。

## `ZagreusField_3437`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wouldn't say the Twin Fists of Malphon counts as empty-handed, sir, though it surprises me to hear you could be beaten in such a way.

**中文**

双子之拳玛尔封可算不上空手啊，先生。不过你居然会被人空手击败，我真挺惊讶的。

## `ZagreusField_3438`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, I was going to say, I'd much prefer we settle this more amicably, couldn't we? Perhaps I could interest you in a fine bottle of Nectar? Ambrosia, even?

**中文**

哎呀，我刚想说我更希望我们能和睦相处呢，你觉得怎么样？不如我请你喝一杯蜜露，或者仙馔密酒？

## `ZagreusField_3439`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Damn it...!

**中文**

可恶……！

## `ZagreusField_3440`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Damn.

**中文**

可恶。

## `ZagreusField_3441`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ah.

**中文**

啊。

## `ZagreusField_3442`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Blast.

**中文**

见鬼。

## `ZagreusField_3443`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Blood and...

**中文**

鲜血与……

## `ZagreusField_3444`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sigh

**中文**

叹气

## `ZagreusField_3445`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tsk

**中文**

啧

## `ZagreusField_3446`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Scoff

**中文**

嘲笑

## `ZagreusField_3447`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not quite...

**中文**

还没有……

## `ZagreusField_3448`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Butterfly...

**中文**

是蝴蝶……

## `ZagreusField_3449`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sorry, Than...

**中文**

对不住了，塔纳……

## `ZagreusField_3450`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Plume...

**中文**

是羽毛……

## `ZagreusField_3451`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sorry, Hermes...

**中文**

不好意思，赫尔墨斯……

## `ZagreusField_3452`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Where do you think you're going?

**中文**

你这是要去哪里？

## `ZagreusField_3453`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wait, where's it going...

**中文**

等等，它要去哪里……

## `ZagreusField_3454`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No! Bad vermin! Bad!

**中文**

不！坏鼠兽！你这个坏东西！

## `ZagreusField_3455`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

So vicious, yet so tiny.

**中文**

体型虽小，却凶狠异常。

## `ZagreusField_3456`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Come back when you're bigger!

**中文**

等你长大了再来吧！

## `ZagreusField_3457`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Pick on somebody your own size next time!

**中文**

挑一个适合您的对手啊，王子殿下！

## `ZagreusField_3458`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Merely listen, so it's just like when I get those messages from the Olympians. Wait, you did just prompt me, Master Chaos, didn't you? Or should I have remained silent just now?

**中文**

……只听不说，这和我从奥林匹斯众神那里接收信息也没什么不一样。等等，卡俄斯君主，你刚刚提示过我了，对吧？还是说，我应该保持沉默才对？

## `ZagreusField_3459`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nyx, you're here...!

**中文**

倪克斯，你来了……！

## `ZagreusField_3460`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nyx...!

**中文**

倪克斯……！

## `ZagreusField_3461`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sorry to interrupt.

**中文**

抱歉打扰了。

## `ZagreusField_3462`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nyx, Master Chaos.

**中文**

倪克斯，卡俄斯君主。

## `ZagreusField_3463`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hello, Nyx...!

**中文**

你好，倪克斯……！

## `ZagreusField_3464`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey, you two!

**中文**

嘿，你们两个！

## `ZagreusField_3465`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hello, you two.

**中文**

你们两个好呀。

## `ZagreusField_3466`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You sound terrific.

**中文**

你听起来真是棒极了。

## `ZagreusField_3467`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey mate! Eurydice!

**中文**

嘿！欧律狄刻！

## `ZagreusField_3468`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hope Nyx is all right...

**中文**

希望倪克斯一切安好……

## `ZagreusField_3469`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dear Aphrodite, the incomparable beauty of Olympus is less difficult to fathom, having already met you. I am so grateful to have earned your favor.

**中文**

亲爱的阿弗洛狄忒，在见面之后，我便发觉，您当之无愧奥林匹斯山上无与伦比的最美的神。能够得到您的青睐是我莫大的荣幸。

## `ZagreusField_3470`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Aphrodite, you must have more worshipers and suitors than all your kin upon Olympus combined. I may not fall exactly into either category, though I hope you know my gratitude for all you've done for me is true.

**中文**

阿弗洛狄忒，您的追随者和爱慕者应该比所有奥林匹斯山的亲人们加起来还要多吧。也许我既不是爱慕者，也不是追随者，但希望你明白，我对你的感激发自真心。

## `ZagreusField_3471`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

My lady Aphrodite, once more you've graced me with your presence and your favor, which inspire the best from me. Let this modest gift of Ambrosia signify I'm forever in your debt.

**中文**

阿弗洛狄忒女士，您再次眷顾了我，出现在我面前，这对我来说是极大的鼓舞。谨以这微不足道的仙馔密酒，来表达我对您无尽的感激之情。

## `ZagreusField_3472`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This is his bracer, yes. You recognize it, then. Were you a myrmidon, yourself? I heard they were unstoppable.

**中文**

没错，你认出来了，这就是他的护腕。你以前也是位密尔弥冬吗？我听说你们无人能敌。

## `ZagreusField_3473`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's... a bit more complicated than that, sir, though yes. This is the very same as he once wielded. I am honored to carry it now.

**中文**

事情……实际上要更加复杂。不过你说得没错，这就是他以前用过的那把长矛，我感到很自豪。

## `ZagreusField_3474`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't think anybody owns this spear, sir. I'm hanging onto it for the time being. Though I don't think I'll be the last to do so.

**中文**

我认为这把长矛不属于任何人，先生。我只是它暂时的使用者，不过我认为，我肯定不会是最后一个挥舞它的人。

## `ZagreusField_3475`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Achilles hasn't been particularly forthcoming about his mortal days, no. So he wouldn't fight? I've heard that mortals sometimes frown on that, but... why should he have fought if he didn't believe in the cause?

**中文**

关于他活着的时候，他并没怎么和我说过。所以说，阿喀琉斯那个时候不肯参战，是吗？我知道，凡人有时候会对不肯参加战斗的士兵大为光火，可……如果连他自己都不相信战争的动机，他为什么还要参战呢？

## `ZagreusField_3476`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He blames himself for what happened to you, you know. I figured he failed to defend you or something, not that... he didn't fight at all. Although, he said something about how, once you died, he shortly followed in a rage...

**中文**

你也知道，他一直为你的惨死而自责。我觉得，他没能保护好你，而不是……根本没有参加战斗。不过，他也确实说过一些事情。他说你死了之后，他马上就气得发疯了，之类的吧……

## `ZagreusField_3477`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, from what I gathered, sir... after you fell, he was overcome with grief. And anger. He joined the war, just like you urged. And, single-handedly, he almost ended it.

**中文**

据我所知，先生……你一死，他就被悲痛击垮了，陷入了狂怒之中。他像你希望的那样，上了战场，而且几乎靠一己之力就结束了这场战争。

## `ZagreusField_3478`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You should know, sir. You were buried together. Your ashes, that is. It was his final request. As for how he came to be at my father's house, that's something I think he can best explain. And I will do everything I can to make sure that he has the opportunity.

**中文**

你应该明白，先生，你们的骨灰被葬在了一起，那是他最后的遗愿。至于他怎么跑到了我父亲的圣殿，我想他肯定能解释清楚。而我要做的，就是确保他有这个解释的机会。

## `ZagreusField_3479`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No need to even ask forgiveness, sir. Achilles is one of my closest friends, but.. he doesn't care to talk about himself. About those times. It must be very painful. For the both of you.

**中文**

不用客气，先生。阿喀琉斯差不多是我最亲密的朋友，可是……他从来不说他自己的事情，也不说曾经的事情。这对你们两个而言，一定非常痛苦吧。

## `ZagreusField_3480`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, of course, sir, what is it? What's on your mind?

**中文**

没问题，先生，是什么事情？你有什么打算？

## `ZagreusField_3481`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Risk it all? Risk what all? He wants what's best for you. He wouldn't want anything to happen.

**中文**

放手一搏？搏什么？他希望你能一切都好，他不希望你发生任何意外。

## `ZagreusField_3482`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nonsense, sir. You're my friend. And Achilles... I don't know where I would even be, if not for you.

**中文**

这就不对了，先生。你是我的朋友，阿喀琉斯也是……如果没了你们，我都不知道自己现在会在什么地方。

## `ZagreusField_3483`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

How about you just keep offering me those premium goods you have no interest in when I pass through, and we can call it even, sir?

**中文**

这样吧，等我路过这里的时候，你们继续向我提供那些你们不感兴趣的好东西吧。这样我们就扯平了，你们觉得呢？

## `ZagreusField_3484`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I, too, would vastly prefer having to contend with Theseus rather than yourself, Achilles. Although, that's not to say I disagree that almost anybody would be better in the role than him.

**中文**

我也是。比起和你交手，阿喀琉斯，我更愿意去和忒修斯打一场。你说随便挑个什么人都比忒修斯能打，我也没什么异议。

## `ZagreusField_3485`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm happy for the both of you. So, is everything all right, then? The circumstances under which you were separated in life... it's unimaginable, really. How does one move on from a thing like that?

**中文**

我为你们两个感到开心。所以，一切都很顺利，是吗……？说真的，我实在是无法想象你们活着的时候生离死别的情形，那到底要怎么才能熬过来啊？

## `ZagreusField_3486`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't mind at all, Patroclus, sir. Coming from you I consider it a term of endearment.

**中文**

别介意，普特洛克勒斯，你这么叫我让我觉得很亲切。

## `ZagreusField_3487`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Here, sir. The drink of the gods, themselves. Thought maybe you would like to try it! Just a small token of my appreciation for your selflessness with me, time after time.

**中文**

这是给你的礼物，先生。是众神的蜜酒，我觉得你可能会喜欢！你一次又一次毫无私心地帮助了我，这是我给你的小小回礼。

## `ZagreusField_3488`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, you're not. You're someone I look forward to seeing here in Elysium, which is more than I can say for just about anybody else around here, so... please, enjoy it. Perhaps with Achilles.

**中文**

才不是呢，你就是我在至福乐土一直想遇到的人。我在这里见过了这么多人，几乎没人能比得上你。所以请好好享受蜜酒吧，如果能和阿喀琉斯一起的话，就再好不过了。

## `ZagreusField_3489`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't see that last bottle of Ambrosia anywhere, which I can only take to mean... you need another. Here, sir. Thanks for seeing me through all of this, time after time.

**中文**

之前那瓶仙馔密酒已经不见了，我觉得这意味着……是时候再给你拿一瓶过来了。收下吧，先生，感谢你一次又一次地帮助我渡过难关。

## `ZagreusField_3490`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's just a bottle of Ambrosia, sir, it's really no big deal. OK, fine, it's rather difficult to come by, but still!

**中文**

不过是瓶仙馔密酒罢了，算不上什么大不了的事情。没错，这瓶酒确实不太容易弄到，但我还是弄到了！

## `ZagreusField_3491`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I suppose I'm somewhat infamous around here now. Though, you can call me Zagreus, or stranger, whatever suits you.

**中文**

看样子我在这里名声不太好啊。不过，你可以叫我扎格列欧斯，或者陌生人，你高兴怎么叫都行。

## `ZagreusField_3492`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You've got the rest of your life ahead of you, as far as I'm concerned. Here's to starting anew, right? You can call me Zagreus, or stranger, whatever suits you.

**中文**

在我看来，你的余生才刚刚开始。你打算让一切重新开始，是吧？你可以叫我扎格列欧斯，或者叫我陌生人，只要你叫着顺口就行。

## `ZagreusField_3493`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'll have you know some of us take matters of fishing very seriously, sir! Besides... helps take my mind off having to fight your Elysian brethren nonstop.

**中文**

我得和你说清楚，我们之中有些人非常认真对待钓鱼这件事情，先生！另外……我得和你在至福乐土的同胞们不停地战斗，钓鱼有助于让我转移注意力。

## `ZagreusField_3494`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Whatever happened with that war you mentioned, sir? If you don't mind my asking.

**中文**

你提到的那场战争，后来怎么样了，先生？希望你不介意我这么问。

## `ZagreusField_3495`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I always wondered, sir. What was Achilles like...? During his youth, I mean. If you can say.

**中文**

我很好奇，先生。阿喀琉斯以前是什么样子……？我是说，他年轻的时候，可以告诉我吗？

## `ZagreusField_3496`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, you know him? Though, yes, he does seem rather set in his ways. Convinced himself that I'm some sort of demon from the lowest depths. Which... well, it's partly true, I guess.

**中文**

等等，你认识他吗？不过你说得没错，他有些方面确实一成不变。他坚信我是来自地狱最深处的恶魔，不过……某种程度上来说，倒也没错吧。

## `ZagreusField_3497`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Must be Theseus and his fancy Macedonian. So he's been joyriding about in that thing? Showing it off?

**中文**

那肯定是忒修斯和他花哨的马其顿号吧。所以他一直都乘着那架战车，到处耀武扬威吗？

## `ZagreusField_3498`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You heard I battled with Asterius, here, sir? I thought you kept more to your own devices. Weren't much for conversation with the other shades.

**中文**

你已经听说我和阿斯忒里俄斯打了一仗，是吗？我还以为你对外面的事情都不感兴趣呢，你好像不怎么和其他的幽灵来往。

## `ZagreusField_3499`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This spearpoint that you gave me, sir. It's been quite useful, though I have to ask you... is there any particular significance to it? Most spearpoints aren't imbued with power such as this.

**中文**

你送给我的矛头非常有用，不过我想问一句……这个矛头有什么特别的地方吗？绝大部分的矛头都没有这么强大的力量。

## `ZagreusField_3500`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Did you once wield a spear yourself, sir? In your fighting days?

**中文**

你以前也是用矛的吗，先生？在你还活着的时候？

## `ZagreusField_3501`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You sure you don't need any of this stuff, sir? I really appreciate it, but... I don't want to take it for granted.

**中文**

你真的不想要这些东西了吗，先生？我是很喜欢啦，不过……我不想把这当成理所当然的礼物。

## `ZagreusField_3502`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have to tell you, sir, it was a real sight to see you fighting alongside Achilles. You two were amazing!

**中文**

我不得不说，先生，看你和阿喀琉斯一起战斗真是场视觉盛宴，你们两个真是太了不起了！

## `ZagreusField_3503`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't sell yourself so short! Though, I thought you laid down your spear forever. Why choose to fight again?

**中文**

别这么低估自己嘛！不过，我还以为你再也不打算挥舞长矛了呢，为什么又打算战斗了？

## `ZagreusField_3504`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, isn't that the Lethe right nearby? You've been through so much, don't you ever... you could just drink deeply of the River of Forgetfulness, and be done with, no?

**中文**

我说，这条河不就是遗忘之河吗？你肯定经历过很多事情吧，你就没想过要……你可以直接喝上一口河水，之后把一切都忘掉，这不好吗？

## `ZagreusField_3505`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You must have met so many great heroes in your day, having fought alongside Achilles... do you still keep in touch with any of them, here?

**中文**

你活着的时候与阿喀琉斯并肩作战，肯定遇到过很多伟大的英雄吧……来这里之后，你们还保持联系吗？

## `ZagreusField_3506`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, for such a loudmouth obnoxious braggart, Theseus does seem to have formed a strong kinship with the Bull of Minos, hasn't he. Are they truly as inseparable as they seem? I feel bad for the Bull.

**中文**

说真的，那个臭名昭著的牛皮大王忒修斯，似乎和那头米诺斯的公牛结成了牢固的盟友关系。他们的感情真的像看起来那么亲密无间吗？我真为那头牛感到难过。

## `ZagreusField_3507`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What do you mean by that, sir? Though, yes, my father being who he is, it means I have a lot of uncles, cousins, and the like, up on the surface, there.

**中文**

你这么说是什么意思，先生？不过确实，我的父亲是冥界之王，我也有很多叔叔和表亲之类的亲戚住在凡界。

## `ZagreusField_3508`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have been, sir. Or, I should say, I am.

**中文**

我爱过，先生。或者说，我现在爱着某个人。

## `ZagreusField_3509`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Not sure that I have been, sir. I understand it's one of those things where, you'll know it when it happens.

**中文**

我不太清楚有没有爱过，先生。我知道，当爱来了的时候，你就会明白了。

## `ZagreusField_3510`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You ever want to travel past this glade, sir? Elysium in all its splendors... there's a lot to see. Sure you're all right?

**中文**

你打算穿过这片林地吗，先生？至福乐土这么美妙的地方……这里还有好多怡人的景色，你真的确定吗？

## `ZagreusField_3511`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You haven't ever made anything worse, Than. But as for me, I realized what you meant. I was so eager to do something for Orpheus when I first met Eurydice, I... never asked them if it was OK.

**中文**

你从来都没搞砸过什么事情，塔纳。可至于我嘛，我明白你是什么意思了。第一次见到欧律狄刻的时候，我实在是太想为俄耳甫斯做些什么了，我……我都没问过他们想不想让我这么做。

## `ZagreusField_3512`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm glad you did, though, Than.

**中文**

不过，我很高兴你那么做了，塔纳。

## `ZagreusField_3513`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, Than, are you OK? I mean, with everything, lately?

**中文**

嘿，塔纳，你最近还好吗？一切都好？

## `ZagreusField_3514`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right.

**中文**

好吧。

## `ZagreusField_3515`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

About when we last spoke. I care about you, Thanatos. A lot. Though, you asked what you are to me... how am I supposed to even answer that?

**中文**

关于我们之前聊过的事情，我很在乎你，塔纳托斯，非常在乎。不过你问我，对我来说，你究竟是什么呢……我该怎么回答你这个问题呢？

## `ZagreusField_3516`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

To think discussing feelings would be even harder than annihilating Father's forces. But... I've been asking myself the same thing, Than. If it's any reassurance.

**中文**

想想看把，讨论感情问题比对抗我父亲的力量还要困难。不过……我也一直在问自己同样的问题，塔纳，不知这么说能不能让你宽慰点。

## `ZagreusField_3517`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You too.

**中文**

你也是。

## `ZagreusField_3518`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, you mean that Coronacht has taken on the aspect of a divine preserver person of some sort, of whom I've seen mere glimpses but seems definitely very capable, and really rather brave?

**中文**
