什么，您指的是科罗纳赫特继承了某个圣人的形态吗？虽然我可能只见过那个人几面，但那个人看起来无人能敌，而且非常勇敢，你是这个意思吗？

## `ZagreusField_3519`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Why, Lernie, you are looking positively fabulous, I have to say.

**中文**

不得不说，勒拿，你看起来真的是很漂亮。

## `ZagreusField_3520`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lernie, you really didn't have to reconstitute yourself just for my sake.

**中文**

勒拿，你真的不用为了我重新构建自己的身体。

## `ZagreusField_3521`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Stopped anybody from attempting to leave Asphodel of late, Lernie?

**中文**

你不打算让任何人离开水仙花平原，是吗，勒拿？

## `ZagreusField_3522`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We'll have to catch up some other time, Lernie, as I'm in a bit of a rush.

**中文**

我们下次有机会再谈谈吧，勒拿，我现在有点急事要办。

## `ZagreusField_3523`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not really in a talking mood this time, Lernie.

**中文**

我现在没心情聊天，勒拿。

## `ZagreusField_3524`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right, Lernie, let's have it out between us, then.

**中文**

好了，勒拿，咱们把这件事解决了吧。

## `ZagreusField_3525`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Why must we always fight like this, Lernie?

**中文**

为什么咱们总是得像这样互相残杀，勒拿？

## `ZagreusField_3526`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I have longed to hear the sound of your hiss, Lernie.

**中文**

我想念你的“咝咝”声了，勒拿。

## `ZagreusField_3527`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

More Darkness.

**中文**

更多黑暗宝石。

## `ZagreusField_3528`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Some Darkness.

**中文**

一些黑暗宝石。

## `ZagreusField_3529`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's dark all right.

**中文**

已经可以了。

## `ZagreusField_3530`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got it.

**中文**

拿到了。

## `ZagreusField_3531`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's mine.

**中文**

是我的了。

## `ZagreusField_3532`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mine now.

**中文**

是我的了。

## `ZagreusField_3533`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mine.

**中文**

是我的了。

## `ZagreusField_3534`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thank you.

**中文**

谢谢你。

## `ZagreusField_3535`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nyx's power.

**中文**

倪克斯的力量。

## `ZagreusField_3536`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Life.

**中文**

生命。

## `ZagreusField_3537`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Life force.

**中文**

生命的力量。

## `ZagreusField_3538`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

More life.

**中文**

更多生命值。

## `ZagreusField_3539`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Living strength.

**中文**

脉动的力量。

## `ZagreusField_3540`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Feeling good.

**中文**

感觉很好。

## `ZagreusField_3541`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right.

**中文**

很好。

## `ZagreusField_3542`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Centaur toughness.

**中文**

半人马的耐力。

## `ZagreusField_3543`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tougher now.

**中文**

我变得更耐打了。

## `ZagreusField_3544`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Getting tougher.

**中文**

我的身体越来越结实了。

## `ZagreusField_3545`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Can't bring me down.

**中文**

没什么能打败我。

## `ZagreusField_3546`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Be right with you, Father.

**中文**

我来找你了，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_3547`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Get you for last time, Father.

**中文**

这是我最后一次和你交战，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_3548`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Made it again.

**中文**

我又成功了。

## `ZagreusField_3549`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to go.

**中文**

该走了。

## `ZagreusField_3550`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cold out there.

**中文**

外面真冷。

## `ZagreusField_3551`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm leaving, Father. Try and stop me.

**中文**

我要走了，父亲，有本事来阻止我吧。

## `ZagreusField_3552`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

To hell with this place.

**中文**

去死吧，这个破地方。

## `ZagreusField_3553`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll find you, Mother.

**中文**

我会找到你的，母亲。

## `ZagreusField_3554`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey, that's Achilles, isn't it!

**中文**

嘿，那不是阿喀琉斯嘛！

## `ZagreusField_3555`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No sign of Dusa!

**中文**

找不到杜莎的身影了！

## `ZagreusField_3556`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Is Dusa really gone?

**中文**

杜莎真的走了吗？

## `ZagreusField_3557`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Where's Dusa?

**中文**

杜莎在哪？

## `ZagreusField_3558`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Can't locate Dusa.

**中文**

我找不到杜莎了。

## `ZagreusField_3559`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Think I missed my chance here.

**中文**

我觉得我是错过了。

## `ZagreusField_3560`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hmm, no bites I guess...

**中文**

嗯，好像没有上钩……

## `ZagreusField_3561`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't think I'll get a bite at this rate.

**中文**

这样下去的话，我恐怕是钓不到鱼的。

## `ZagreusField_3562`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...I think I've waited long enough.

**中文**

……我觉得我等得已经够久了。

## `ZagreusField_3563`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No river denizens about I think.

**中文**

我猜已经没有鱼了。

## `ZagreusField_3564`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I should probably go...

**中文**

或许我该走了……

## `ZagreusField_3565`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Have to take care of that Butterfly Ball!

**中文**

我得先解决那个蝴蝶球才行！

## `ZagreusField_3566`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Need to deal with the blasted Butterfly Ball!

**中文**

我得先干掉那个该死的蝴蝶球！

## `ZagreusField_3567`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

They'll just keep coming 'cause of that Butterfly Ball!

**中文**

不把那个蝴蝶球干掉，这些家伙就没完没了！

## `ZagreusField_3568`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I should deal with that Butterfly Ball!

**中文**

我得把那个蝴蝶球解决掉！

## `ZagreusField_3569`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Better handle that Butterfly Ball!

**中文**

最好先把那个蝴蝶球解决掉！

## `ZagreusField_3570`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There's no end to these guys!

**中文**

这些家伙简直没完没了！

## `ZagreusField_3571`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll get right on it, Than!

**中文**

交给我吧，塔纳！

## `ZagreusField_3572`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What do you think, Than?

**中文**

你是怎么想的，塔纳？

## `ZagreusField_3573`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I say: Sure, Than, sounds good to me.

**中文**

我觉得没问题，塔纳，听起来不错。

## `ZagreusField_3574`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, absolutely terrified.

**中文**

哦，我真是吓坏了。

## `ZagreusField_3575`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hope you don't mean me.

**中文**

希望你不是在说我。

## `ZagreusField_3576`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, then, have at it!

**中文**

好吧，那就动手吧！

## `ZagreusField_3577`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll be as reckless as I want!

**中文**

我想怎么鲁莽，就怎么鲁莽！

## `ZagreusField_3578`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

They really don't.

**中文**

他们确实没什么长进。

## `ZagreusField_3579`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I see you stand your ground against the serpent's flame...

**中文**

我看到你傲然屹立在巨蛇的烈焰之中……

## `ZagreusField_3580`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I see your prideful fall down from the heavens to the flames...

**中文**

我看到高傲的你从天堂跌落下来，落入了地狱的烈焰之中……

## `ZagreusField_3581`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Not much, really, besides awakening the aspect of a supposedly unstoppable, dragon-slaying warrior called Beowulf. If it has the power to protect him against a giant lizard, I figure it may help me against you.

**中文**

老实说没做什么，我只不过是唤醒了某个勇猛无双的屠龙英雄，某个叫贝奥武夫的战士的形态。如果这面盾牌的力量足以帮助他对付大蜥蜴的话，我猜，它应该也能帮助我对付你才是。

## `ZagreusField_3582`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, someone called Lucifer apparently thought differently, and took a crack at his own father with it, once. This one's for him, then.

**中文**

不过，某个叫路西法的家伙却很喜欢这把武器。他拿着这把武器，朝他的父亲开了火。我猜，这把武器应该就是给他用的吧。

## `ZagreusField_3583`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Achilles!

**中文**

阿喀琉斯！

## `ZagreusField_3584`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Achilles!!

**中文**

阿喀琉斯！！

## `ZagreusField_3585`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Achilles?

**中文**

阿喀琉斯？

## `ZagreusField_3586`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Achilles, help!

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，帮帮我！

## `ZagreusField_3587`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Achilles, sir!

**中文**

阿喀琉斯，先生！

## `ZagreusField_3588`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I made it...

**中文**

我成功了……

## `ZagreusField_3589`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Beyond the frozen overlook... await the first glimpse of the sun to your left... then onward through the cold.

**中文**

远眺冰冻的大地……等待左手边的第一缕阳光……然后穿过严寒。

## `ZagreusField_3590`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sure hope this works, Nyx.

**中文**

希望这样有用，倪克斯。

## `ZagreusField_3591`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Beyond the frozen overlook... await the first glimpse of the sun to your left... then onward through the cold.

**中文**

远眺冰冻的大地……等待左手边的第一缕阳光……然后穿过严寒。

## `ZagreusField_3592`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wha, that thing, that's... the sun...

**中文**

哇啊，那，那是……是太阳……

## `ZagreusField_3593`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

So much water... Uncle Poseidon's realm.

**中文**

好多水……是波塞冬叔叔的国度。

## `ZagreusField_3594`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mother isn't far...

**中文**

就快见到母亲了……

## `ZagreusField_3595`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

So... bright... ugh...

**中文**

好……刺眼……呃……

## `ZagreusField_3596`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Is this... it can't...

**中文**

这是……不可能……

## `ZagreusField_3597`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Gasp

**中文**

气喘吁吁

## `ZagreusField_3598`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm here...

**中文**

我来了……

## `ZagreusField_3599`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Warmer, finally...

**中文**

终于暖和了……

## `ZagreusField_3600`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Erm... hello...?

**中文**

呃...你好？

## `ZagreusField_3601`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wait, can, can we just...?

**中文**

等，等一下，我们先……？

## `ZagreusField_3602`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, you must be... Persephone. My name is Zagreus, and... I think I might be your son.

**中文**

呃，你一定是……珀尔塞福涅了。我的名字是扎格列欧斯，而且……我想我应该是你的儿子。

## `ZagreusField_3603`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No!! So then you are her. I, I don't care if you hate me, but I'm not leaving until I get some answers! I never even knew you existed. Why did you leave me?

**中文**

不！！所以说你就是她。我、我不在乎你讨不讨厌我，但我在得到答案之前是不会走的！我根本不知道你还活着，你为什么要离开我？

## `ZagreusField_3604`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I died? I died... what, you mean permanently died, but, I'm alive! Oh, gods... you never even knew I existed, either.

**中文**

我死了？我死了……等等，你说的是彻底死了，可我还活着啊！哦，天啊……你也不知道我还活着。

## `ZagreusField_3605`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mother...!

**中文**

母亲……！

## `ZagreusField_3606`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...In short, I beat him, finally, though he did everything he could to prevent our meeting here like this. But there's something he wanted me to tell you, in the end. He said to tell you Cerberus is doing very well.

**中文**

……简单地说，虽然他竭尽全力阻止我们像这样见面，但我还是把他打败了。不过有些事情他想让我告诉你，他想让我告诉你，刻耳柏洛斯一切都相当好。

## `ZagreusField_3607`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Ugh... Oh, it's... guess I'm just a little tired, that's all. Not accustomed to the weather, as I think you call it.

**中文**

呃啊……哦，我猜……我可能有点累了，没什么的。我还不怎么适应这里的“天气”，是这么说的吧。

## `ZagreusField_3608`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Really, you're... heh... kicking me out? Why?

**中文**

真的吗，你真的……呵……要赶走我吗？为什么？

## `ZagreusField_3609`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No... I can... come back... I can come back, now that I found you, he... maybe he'll just let me come back.

**中文**

不……我还会……回来的……既然我找到你了，那我就还能回来。他……或许他也会允许我回来的。

## `ZagreusField_3610`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'll tell him... I'll tell him, I... feel awful. I... I have to go.

**中文**

我会告诉他……我会告诉他的，我……感觉很难受，我……我得走了。

## `ZagreusField_3611`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

However... long... it takes...

**中文**

不管……要……多久……

## `ZagreusField_3612`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mother!

**中文**

母亲！

## `ZagreusField_3613`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's me.

**中文**

是我啊。

## `ZagreusField_3614`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm back.

**中文**

我回来了。

## `ZagreusField_3615`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm back, Mother!

**中文**

我回来了，母亲！

## `ZagreusField_3616`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's only me.

**中文**

只有我。

## `ZagreusField_3617`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I made it, Mother.

**中文**

我成功了，母亲。

## `ZagreusField_3618`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hello, Mother!

**中文**

你好，母亲！

## `ZagreusField_3619`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mother...?

**中文**

母亲……？

## `ZagreusField_3620`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm here, Mother.

**中文**

我来了，母亲。

## `ZagreusField_3621`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mother!

**中文**

母亲！

## `ZagreusField_3622`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father didn't make it any easier for me. Nor would he tell me why he never sent for you, after you left. So, I don't know, what's going on, still, but... I wanted to see you, again.

**中文**

父亲并没有对我手下留情，也没告诉过我你离开之后，他为什么没来找你。所以，我也不知道发生了什么，不过……我希望能再见到你。

## `ZagreusField_3623`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I just... I need to know what happened, between you and Father. Why are you here, and not with us?

**中文**

我只想……我想知道你和父亲之间发生了什么事情。你为什么在这里，而不是和我们在一起呢？

## `ZagreusField_3624`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I know the feeling, I think. But you stayed. Long enough to have me. Those whom I've spoken to about you, it's as you said, they have fond memories of you. And then, you even had a child. Did Father... did he mistreat you?

**中文**

我觉得我明白这种感觉，但你还是留在了那里，一直留到生下我为止。我和其他人提起过你的事情，就像你说的，他们都很喜欢和你在一起。而且，你甚至还生了个孩子。父亲他……他有没有虐待过你？

## `ZagreusField_3625`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Could not live down there, according to whom, exactly? The Fates?

**中文**

没办法在冥界生存，这到底是谁说的？是命运三女神吗？

## `ZagreusField_3626`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Urgh... but... why would Father lie to you? If he never wanted you... or never wanted me... surely he had the means.

**中文**

呃……可是……父亲为什么要对你撒谎？如果他根本不想你……或者说，根本不想我诞生……他肯定应该有办法才对。

## `ZagreusField_3627`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...I'm trying... but I can't... stay that much longer... I don't think.

**中文**

……我也想……但我已经……没办法呆太久了……我恐怕不行了。

## `ZagreusField_3628`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Until we... ungh.

**中文**

下次我们再……呃。

## `ZagreusField_3629`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mother, I... discovered how come Father led you to believe that I could not survive past birth. He didn't lie to you, exactly. So he says.

**中文**

母亲，我……我弄清楚父亲是怎么让你相信，我一生出来就会死了。确切地说，他并没有对你撒谎，他是这么说的。

## `ZagreusField_3630`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Because... he thought it was the truth. The Fates appointed him dominion over the entire Underworld. But he would never have an heir, so he was led to understand. He never expected me to be born, much less survive.

**中文**

因为……他以为这是真的。命运女神让他统治整个冥界，但他没办法拥有子嗣，于是他就是这么理解的。他根本不认为我会出生，更不认为我会活下来。

## `ZagreusField_3631`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx saved my life. Or gave me life, I guess. Then raised me in your absence. The Fates are her daughters, right? Maybe she persuaded them to go easier on me. She cannot say more. In any case, apparently it took her quite a while to revive me, and... by then, you were gone.

**中文**

是倪克斯救了我一命，或者说，是她赐予了我生命。你不在的时候，是她养大了我。命运三女神都是她的女儿，是吧？说不定是她劝她们别对我这么刻薄。她不能透露更多内情了。总之，她为了救我，花了相当大的力气……等我活过来的时候，你已经走了。

## `ZagreusField_3632`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yes. Although, we still don't know how come Father and Nyx never sent for you. Though, ungh, let me ask you something, Mother, if you please. If you had found out... that I was born, and very much alive... would you have come back?

**中文**

是啊，不过我们还是不清楚，为什么父亲和倪克斯从来没找过你。不过，呃，如果可以的话，有件事我想问你，母亲。如果你发现……我还活着，而且活得好好的……你愿意回来吗？

## `ZagreusField_3633`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well then... what if... urgh... what if Father... didn't want you... doing that?

**中文**

可如果……如果……呃……如果父亲……不希望你……这么做呢？

## `ZagreusField_3634`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's just... a hunch... of mine...

**中文**

只是……我的……直觉……

## `ZagreusField_3635`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I just... don't think that can be. Though, let me ask you. How long did you know Father? And Nyx, for that matter? Did they seem trustworthy?

**中文**

我……不觉得他们是这样的人。不过我想问你，你和我的父亲认识了多久？和倪克斯认识了多久？他们看起来可信吗？

## `ZagreusField_3636`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Especially since neither of them is being forthcoming with me even now, right? And yet, I have this feeling something else is going on, Mother. The way that Father speaks of you, the way Nyx speaks of you... I think... they care for you.

**中文**

尤其是他们谁都没和我一起来，是这样吧？不过，我和你的感觉不太一样，母亲。父亲谈起你时的样子，还有倪克斯谈起你的样子……我觉得……他们很在乎你。

## `ZagreusField_3637`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I do. Mother, what if... what if Father thinks that he's protecting you? Have you... considered that?

**中文**

是啊，母亲。可如果……如果父亲觉得他这么做是在保护你呢？你有没有……这么想过？

## `ZagreusField_3638`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well... urgh... you're from Olympus, aren't you? But... the gods, none of them seem to know... you're here... so...

**中文**

这么说吧……呃……你来自奥林匹斯山，是吧？可……奥林匹斯众神好像都不知道……你在这里……所以……

## `ZagreusField_3639`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I... I won't be long...

**中文**

我……用不了多久……

## `ZagreusField_3640`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I think I know quite a bit by now. I've been in steady contact with Athena, Zeus, and other members of the family ever since I first left Father's House... they've been assisting me in getting here.

**中文**

我觉得我已经对他们相当了解了。自从我第一次离开父亲的圣殿起，我就一直与雅典娜、宙斯，还有很多其他神明一直保持联系……来这里的路上，他们一直在帮我。

## `ZagreusField_3641`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

A little. I've been in contact with Athena, Zeus, and other members of the family ever since I first left Father's House... they've been assisting me in getting here.

**中文**

不是很了解，自从我第一次离开父亲的圣殿起，我就一直与雅典娜、宙斯，还有很多其他神明一直保持联系……来这里的路上，他们一直在帮我。

## `ZagreusField_3642`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, no, they think... they think I'm trying to escape from Father so that I can join them on their mountaintop. At least I think that's what they think. Most of them, anyway.

**中文**

不，不，他们以为……他们以为我打算逃出父亲的王国，去奥林匹斯山与他们相聚。至少我觉得他们是这么想的，反正大部分是这么想的。

## `ZagreusField_3643`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

He does. Both he and Nyx. In fact, it's Nyx who put me and Olympus into contact. I could never have broken free of the Underworld if not for their help.

**中文**

知道，他和倪克斯都知道。老实说，是倪克斯让我和奥林匹斯众神取得联系的。要不是因为他们的帮助，我绝对没办法逃出冥界。

## `ZagreusField_3644`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's right. More or less. Nyx and Father seem to put up with each other. Don't seem to have much of a choice. Though, ever since I learned of you, Nyx has been helping me to find you... while Father's tried to stop me.

**中文**

差不多算是这样吧，倪克斯和父亲看起来只能忍着对方，他们似乎也别无选择。不过，自从我得知了你的下落之后，倪克斯一直在帮我找你……而父亲一直试图阻止我。

## `ZagreusField_3645`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I... urgh... I think maybe he does think so, indeed... because, now having met my relatives upon Olympus, I... can definitely see that they can be a bit... vindictive about things.

**中文**

我……呃……我觉得说不定他以为是在保护你……因为我已经见过奥林匹斯山的亲戚们了，我……很明显看得出来，他们对很多事情都有点……复仇心切。

## `ZagreusField_3646`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

When I... return...

**中文**

就会……回来……

## `ZagreusField_3647`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Except Father absconded with you, didn't he? Dragged you down with him into the Underworld. So if Olympus were to find that out... I shudder to think of the consequences.

**中文**

……可父亲带你私奔了，是吧？他带着你私奔到了冥界。所以，如果奥林匹斯众神发现了这件事情的话……我都不敢想象会发生什么后果。

## `ZagreusField_3648`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I dare not ask who did bring you to Father, then. So, essentially, you ran away from home, to live with him? I mean no disrespect, but... did you realize what you were getting yourself into?

**中文**

我还是不问是谁把你带到父亲那里的了。所以大体而言，你从家里私奔出来，要和他一起生活，是吗？我无意冒犯，可……你知不知道这样做会惹出什么麻烦？

## `ZagreusField_3649`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So, urgh... then you secretly left Mount Olympus to be with the god of the dead in the Underworld. And Olympus never found out. What do you suppose would happen... if they did?

**中文**

所以，呃……你偷偷离开了奥林匹斯山，是为了能和冥王一起生活，而且奥林匹斯众神并不知道这件事情。你觉得，如果他们知道了的话……会怎么样呢？

## `ZagreusField_3650`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

So it makes sense... if Father, he agrees with you, and thinks... that leaving you in hiding here, that it's the only way... he can prevent that war...

**中文**

这就说得通了……如果父亲也和你想得一样，觉得……只有让你藏在这里……才能阻止这场战争的话……

## `ZagreusField_3651`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No... what have I done...

**中文**

不……我做了什么……

## `ZagreusField_3652`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, what? Because of what would happen if the Olympians found out? So, after everything, you're taking Father's side in all of this?!

**中文**

等等，你说什么？你害怕奥林匹斯众神发现这件事情可能带来的后果吗？所以说，经历了这么多之后，你还是站在父亲那一边吗？！

## `ZagreusField_3653`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Your fault? So let's say you never left Olympus! Then it'd be just like you thought, until quite recently, I think. That I'd never existed! Besides, they haven't found out any of this. What makes you so sure they will, all the sudden?

**中文**

都是你的错？这么说吧，假设你根本没有离开过奥林匹斯山！那一切就都会和你预想的那样。那我根本就不会出生了！而且奥林匹斯众神一直都没发现这件事情。你为什么觉得他们现在就会突然发现呢？

## `ZagreusField_3654`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Go home with me, Mother! You came here under false pretenses. I haven't fought all this way, over and over again, only to turn back now. I need you in my life! How can you just... turn me away like this?

**中文**

和我一起回去吧，母亲！你是因为误会才会来这里的。我一遍又一遍地冲到这里，可不是为了这种时候扭头就走的。我的人生需要你！你怎么能……就这样赶我走呢？

## `ZagreusField_3655`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Then... ungh... you're just... going to stay here? Alone, in the middle of nowhere, in this little garden... forever? After we part ways, just like that?

**中文**

所以……呃……你就打算……留在这里吗？一个人孤苦伶仃，呆在这座小花园里……呆一辈子吗？就这样和我们分开吗？

## `ZagreusField_3656`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mother... I... blast...!

**中文**

母亲……我……可恶！

## `ZagreusField_3657`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You can't do this to me, Mother. You said Father is no fool, and neither are you. There has to be a way to fix this. Not just with you and Father, but with everyone. There has to be a way!

**中文**

你不能这么对我，母亲。你说过父亲并不傻，你也不傻，那肯定会有什么办法解决问题才对。不仅仅是解决你和父亲的问题，而是解决所有人的问题。肯定会有什么方法才对！

## `ZagreusField_3658`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What? Besides what? Tell me, please.

**中文**

什么？而且什么？拜托了，告诉我吧。

## `ZagreusField_3659`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No! I won't be understanding anything of the sort. Father still loves you. I'm sure of it! And, he owes you an apology, damn it! I cannot make amends with you on his behalf. It's something only you can do together.

**中文**

我不！我才不想理解这些事情呢。父亲还爱着你，我很肯定！而且他还欠你个道歉呢，该死！我可没办法替他来让你们破镜重圆。只有你们齐心协力，才能办得到。

## `ZagreusField_3660`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Because... ungh... I'm his blasted son, that's how I know! But trust me isn't ever good enough, I know that, too. I'll prove it to you, Mother! Just you wait and see.

**中文**

与某位……呃……我是他该死的儿子，所以我知道！不过只有一句“相信我”是不够的，我也明白。我会向你证明这一点的，母亲！你等着看吧。

## `ZagreusField_3661`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No... urgh... it's not... it's not enough, for me, because... this blasted family... it cannot just keep hiding from its problems! Running from them! We're stuck with one another, we... we have to do everything we can... for one another!

**中文**

不够……呃……不够，对我来说不够。因为……这个该死的家庭……不能总是对问题视而不见！不能总是逃避问题！我们都是家人，我们……我们必须尽我们所能……为了家人！

## `ZagreusField_3662`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We... have to...

**中文**

我们……必须……

## `ZagreusField_3663`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mother... in Father's bedchambers... mind you, there's almost nothing there, just neatly sorted rows of all his samey capes... but on his nightstand... is a portrait of you. Wearing a regal crown of red laurels. A Queen. His Queen.

**中文**

母亲……在父亲的卧室里……我想告诉你，那里几乎什么都没有，只整齐地摆着一大排一模一样的披肩……不过在他的床头柜上……却摆着你的肖像。你戴着红色的桂冠，是那里的皇后，他的皇后。

## `ZagreusField_3664`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, no, he's never let anybody in his bedchambers! I broke in. Don't you see? He didn't put your portrait back on display. It's always been there! Ever since you left. So, Mother, why do you suppose he would do that?

**中文**

不，不，他根本不让任何人进入他的卧室！是我闯进去的。你还看不出来吗？他才不是把它摆回去做样子。你走了之后，那副画像一直就摆在那里。所以说，母亲，你觉得他为什么要这么做？

## `ZagreusField_3665`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Have you ever loved him, yourself?

**中文**

你有没有爱过他？

## `ZagreusField_3666`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Won't you... urgh... won't you please talk to him? Or visit Cerberus again, or Nyx? As for Olympus... surely all of you... can figure something out...?

**中文**

你就不想……呃……就不想和他聊聊天吗？就不想再见见刻耳柏洛斯，或是倪克斯吗？至于奥林匹斯众神……你们齐心协力的话……总能想出什么办法吧……？

## `ZagreusField_3667`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

But... you are... the Queen...!

**中文**

但是……你可是……冥后……！

## `ZagreusField_3668`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Mother, wait, what is all this?

**中文**

母亲，等等，这是怎么回事？

## `ZagreusField_3669`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Make what a surprise?

**中文**

什么惊喜？

## `ZagreusField_3670`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're... really?! Mother, you, you're serious?

**中文**

你……真的吗？！母亲，你，你是认真的吗？

## `ZagreusField_3671`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Join you, but... wait, how are you going to get back? I... hadn't thought that far ahead.

**中文**

和你一起回去，可……你打算怎么回去呢？我……还没做过这么远的打算。

## `ZagreusField_3672`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Charon?!

**中文**

卡戎？！

## `ZagreusField_3673`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sigh ...I'm still alive... but, how?

**中文**

叹气……我还活着……但这怎么可能？

## `ZagreusField_3674`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I can't believe that this is happening.

**中文**

我真不敢相信，这一切居然成真了。

## `ZagreusField_3675`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Seriously. Though, even after all of this... it's like we only just met. I have so many questions for you still.

**中文**

是啊，不过就算经历了这么多……我感觉好像还是刚刚见到你一样，我还有那么多问题要问。

## `ZagreusField_3676`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wait, isn't this... Eurydice and Orpheus?

**中文**

等一等，这不是……欧律狄刻和俄耳甫斯吗？

## `ZagreusField_3677`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wait, isn't this Orpheus?

**中文**

等下，这不是俄耳甫斯吗？

## `ZagreusField_3678`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We're here!

**中文**

我们到了！

## `ZagreusField_3679`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This is embarrassing, but... I don't know the way to get back in. Except, you know, the customary way.

**中文**

这说起来有点尴尬，不过……我不知道回去的路，我是说，除了我平时回去的那条路之外。

## `ZagreusField_3680`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I found her, Father. And I'm going to find Mother again. And again, and again, until I have some answers, finally. She'd like some answers, too, beginning with why did you never tell her that I lived? You let her go, and left her to her grief!

**中文**

我找到她了，父亲。我打算继续去找我的母亲，一次又一次地去找她，直到我得到某些答案为止。她有些问题也想知道答案，比如说你为什么从来没告诉过她我还活着？是你放走了她，任由她沉湎在悲伤之中！

## `ZagreusField_3681`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yeah, about that. Father, did... did you let Mother go because you feared Olympus would find out? And... not respond well to the circumstances if they did?

**中文**

是啊。关于这一点，父亲，你……你是故意让母亲走的吗，因为你担心奥林匹斯众神会发现这一点？如果……他们发现的话，恐怕不会有什么好脸色给你看，是吗？

## `ZagreusField_3682`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, she seems quite lovely, so... knowing you, you probably tricked her into signing a pact to come join you, or something. Since you weren't allowed on Olympus, why not have Olympus come to you?

**中文**

这么说吧，她看起来是个很可爱的人，所以说……她认识了你，说不定你骗她和你签署了什么协议，让她来到冥界。既然奥林匹斯众神不允许你留在那里，那他们怎么从来没找过你呢？

## `ZagreusField_3683`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You didn't drag her down to hell, yourself, apparently. You're not going to tell me who did, though I can well imagine. Your brothers got to reign forever on Olympus, while you got sent down into the Underworld. So they gave you a consolation prize... didn't they?

**中文**

很显然，并不是你把她拐到冥界的。虽然你不会告诉我是谁把她弄过来的，不过我也猜得到。你的兄弟们打算永远统治奥林匹斯山，而你却被送到了冥界。所以他们打算给你个安慰……是这么回事吧？

## `ZagreusField_3684`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, what would you even know?! You claim so much respect for her, yet you didn't even respect her enough to tell her what happened after she left! But... what am I saying... I know you love her, still, Father. That best explains all the terrible choices you've made.

**中文**

哦，你现在知道尊重了？！你口口声声说尊重她，可她离开之后，你甚至连这里发生了什么事情都不告诉她，还敢说什么尊重！不过……话说回来……我知道你还爱着她，父亲。这最好能解释得通你怎么会做出那么多糟糕的抉择。

## `ZagreusField_3685`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Maybe they just think you're in the wrong. And that you're an obstinate, overbearing, miserable excuse for a husband and father. Don't you understand I'm trying to fix the problems you caused? Since you're so unwilling to do it yourself.

**中文**

或许她们只是觉得你做错了，觉得你是个顽固、霸道、悲惨的父亲与丈夫。我正在努力解决你造成的问题，你难道不明白吗？这都是因为你不愿意自己解决那些问题。

## `ZagreusField_3686`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What? You're letting me go, just like that? You're just afraid of getting struck down again. You're serious?

**中文**

什么？你就打算这么让我走了吗？你该不会只是害怕又败在我手上一次吧？你是认真的吗？

## `ZagreusField_3687`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good-bye, Father...

**中文**

再见，父亲……

## `ZagreusField_3688`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's the spirit, Father! As for me, I find the candor of our interactions here to be rather refreshing, I must say. Especially the interactions between my weapons and your physique. You're tough, but you're an easy target.

**中文**

就是这个态度，父亲！对我来说，我得承认，我们在这里的互动让我感觉耳目一新，特别是我的武器与你的身体之间的互动。你的身体很结实，但想击中你实在是太容易了。

## `ZagreusField_3689`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come on, Father, even you must have enjoyed that feast to some extent. We ought to do it more often! Good thing you trusted Mother's judgment on that one. So, then, to the death, once more?

**中文**

别这样，父亲。就算是你，有时候也得享受一下盛宴的欢乐，咱们以后应该多举办些盛宴才对！很高兴你在这件事情上相信了母亲的判断。那就再来一次，至死方休吧？

## `ZagreusField_3690`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Here's the surface, Exagryph. Maybe it's as rare a sight for that Lucifer as it is for me.

**中文**

这里就是地面了，伊格瑞菲。或许对路西法和我而言，地面的景象没那么常见吧。

## `ZagreusField_3691`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Here we are, then, Aegis. This Beowulf will be most fortunate to have your protection.

**中文**

我们到了，埃癸斯。能得到你的保护，这个叫贝奥武夫的人一定很幸运。

## `ZagreusField_3692`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wringers.

**中文**

是碾碎者。

## `ZagreusField_3693`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wringers!

**中文**

是碾碎者！

## `ZagreusField_3694`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

More Wringers!

**中文**

更多碾碎者来了！

## `ZagreusField_3695`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

More Wringers?

**中文**

更多碾碎者来了？

## `ZagreusField_3696`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bone-Rakers.

**中文**

是碎骨者。

## `ZagreusField_3697`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bone-Rakers!

**中文**

是碎骨者！

## `ZagreusField_3698`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

More Bone-Rakers!

**中文**

更多碎骨者来了！

## `ZagreusField_3699`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

More Bone-Rakers?

**中文**

更多碎骨者来了吗？

## `ZagreusField_3700`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Dracons.

**中文**

是毒蛇。

## `ZagreusField_3701`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Dracons!

**中文**

是毒蛇！

## `ZagreusField_3702`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

More Dracons!

**中文**

更多毒蛇来了！

## `ZagreusField_3703`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

More Dracons?

**中文**

更多毒蛇来了吗？

## `ZagreusField_3704`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Doomstone. Just great.

**中文**

末日巨岩，真是太棒了。

## `ZagreusField_3705`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's go, Doomstone.

**中文**

来打一架吧，末日巨岩。

## `ZagreusField_3706`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to break you to bits.

**中文**

是时候把你砸成碎块了。

## `ZagreusField_3707`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Come on, Doomstone.

**中文**

来吧，末日巨岩。

## `ZagreusField_3708`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I thought crystals were supposed to be pretty.

**中文**

我还以为水晶应该很漂亮才对呢。

## `ZagreusField_3709`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Broken to bits.

**中文**

四分五裂了。

## `ZagreusField_3710`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You'll be back, won't you.

**中文**

你们是会回去的，是吧？

## `ZagreusField_3711`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Gone all to pieces.

**中文**

已经支离破碎了。

## `ZagreusField_3712`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Shattered that.

**中文**

把它砸碎了。

## `ZagreusField_3713`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Um. Wrong chamber?

**中文**

呃，我走错地方了吗？

## `ZagreusField_3714`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's that damn Wretched Sneak.

**中文**

又是那种该死的邪恶伏击者。

## `ZagreusField_3715`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Come on, Sneak!

**中文**

来啊，伏击者！

## `ZagreusField_3716`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You've been waiting for this, haven't you?

**中文**

你一直在等这个机会，对不对？

## `ZagreusField_3717`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right, you Sneak.

**中文**

好了，你这个伏击者。

## `ZagreusField_3718`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That'll show you.

**中文**

这样你应该就尝到教训了。

## `ZagreusField_3719`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See you next time, I'm sure?

**中文**

下次再见，好吗？

## `ZagreusField_3720`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's what you get.

**中文**

这是你自找的。

## `ZagreusField_3721`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Back to the shadows.

**中文**

回到阴影里去吧。

## `ZagreusField_3722`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Witches Circle...

**中文**

是女巫们……

## `ZagreusField_3723`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

These Witches again.

**中文**

又是这群女巫。

## `ZagreusField_3724`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hit me if you can, Witches!

**中文**

能打得到我的话，尽管试试吧，女巫！

## `ZagreusField_3725`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey look, it's me, Witches!

**中文**

嘿，女巫们，快看，是我啊！

## `ZagreusField_3726`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hello, Witches Circle.

**中文**

你好啊，女巫们。

## `ZagreusField_3727`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Witchcraft. What can you do.

**中文**

女巫们，你们还有什么能耐？

## `ZagreusField_3728`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Farewell, ladies.

**中文**

再见了，女士们。

## `ZagreusField_3729`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No hard feelings.

**中文**

别太难过。

## `ZagreusField_3730`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That takes care of them.

**中文**

这样就把她们解决掉了。

## `ZagreusField_3736`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whew, that guy...

**中文**

哎哟，那个家伙……

## `ZagreusField_3739`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There's some glory for you!

**中文**

你打得还挺不错的！

## `ZagreusField_3740`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Be sure to tell your friends about me!

**中文**

记得和你的朋友们说说我的事情！

## `ZagreusField_3741`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey, free money! Whoops!

**中文**

嘿，是可以白拿的钱！哎呀呀！

## `ZagreusField_3742`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This must be here for me. Right?

**中文**

这肯定是给我的，是吧？

## `ZagreusField_3743`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Charon won't mind, will he?

**中文**

卡戎是不会介意的，是吧？

## `ZagreusField_3744`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey Charon, want to fight?

**中文**

嘿，卡戎，想和我打一架吗？

## `ZagreusField_3745`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey Charon, look at me!

**中文**

嘿，卡戎，看看我！

## `ZagreusField_3746`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wow, thank you, Charon!

**中文**

哇哦，谢谢你，卡戎！

## `ZagreusField_3747`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Charon, look, I can explain. It's just, I've grown very accustomed to taking stuff throughout my father's realm. I wasn't stealing from you, honestly!

**中文**

呃，你看，卡戎，我可以解释的。我只是，呃，只是习惯了随手拿走我父亲领土上的一切东西。我没打算偷你的东西，我是说真的！

## `ZagreusField_3748`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That was not an understanding sound. Wait, why are you brandishing your oar in such a menacing way?

**中文**

听起来你没明白我的意思。等等，你干嘛要用那么吓人的姿势挥你的船桨？

## `ZagreusField_3749`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh. It's because you're going to kill me.

**中文**

哦，这是因为你想杀我在先。

## `ZagreusField_3750`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's settle this like gentlemen, Charon!

**中文**

咱们用绅士的方式来解决这个问题吧，卡戎！

## `ZagreusField_3751`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You just might be my favorite sparring partner, mate!

**中文**

论打架的话，你可能是我最喜欢的对手了，朋友！

## `ZagreusField_3752`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Come on, let's have another go of it!

**中文**

来吧，我们再来打一场吧！

## `ZagreusField_3753`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Think you can beat me, Charon?

**中文**

你觉得你能打败我吗，卡戎？

## `ZagreusField_3754`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sure beats having to ferry souls about, right?

**中文**

这肯定比划船有意思多了，是吧？

## `ZagreusField_3755`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's go, Charon!

**中文**

动手吧，卡戎！

## `ZagreusField_3756`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You and me, Charon mate!

**中文**

来单挑吧，卡戎伙计！

## `ZagreusField_3757`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You and me, mate!

**中文**

就你和我单挑，伙计！

## `ZagreusField_3758`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wait, did I win?

**中文**

等等，我赢了吗？

## `ZagreusField_3759`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hah, got you there!

**中文**

哈，是我赢了！

## `ZagreusField_3760`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whew, mate, you are tough!

**中文**

哇哦，伙计，你可真厉害！

## `ZagreusField_3761`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Um, no hard feelings, right?

**中文**

呃，别太难过了，好吗？

## `ZagreusField_3762`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, I was just getting warmed up!

**中文**

啊啊，我才刚刚暖身呢！

## `ZagreusField_3763`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nice fighting to the death with you.

**中文**

能和你打得至死方休，我很开心。

## `ZagreusField_3764`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good fight, mate.

**中文**

打得不错，伙计。

## `ZagreusField_3765`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You had me worried, mate.

**中文**

你真是让我吓了一跳，伙计。

## `ZagreusField_3766`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No way, a membership card? For me?

**中文**

什么，会员卡？是给我的吗？

## `ZagreusField_3767`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I look forward to making use of this.

**中文**

我很期待它能派得上用场。

## `ZagreusField_3768`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Appreciate your generosity.

**中文**

谢谢你这么慷慨。

## `ZagreusField_3769`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Looks like I'm back in the club.

**中文**

看起来我又成为俱乐部会员了。

## `ZagreusField_3770`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Look at these savings!

**中文**

看看这些积蓄吧！

## `ZagreusField_3771`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What a deal, mate.

**中文**

真是笔好买卖，伙计。

## `ZagreusField_3772`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's a deal!

**中文**

成交了！

## `ZagreusField_3773`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Dusa's not around to help me now...

**中文**

杜莎不在，没人帮我了……

## `ZagreusField_3774`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Is Dusa really gone?

**中文**

杜莎真的走了吗？

## `ZagreusField_3775`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I miss Dusa...!

**中文**

我好想念杜莎……！

## `ZagreusField_3776`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No word from Dusa?

**中文**

还是没有杜莎的消息吗？

## `ZagreusField_3777`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Dusa, where are you?

**中文**

杜莎，你在哪呢？

## `ZagreusField_3778`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ow! Money.

**中文**

哦，是钱！

## `ZagreusField_3779`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Augh, I'm rich!

**中文**

啊，我是有钱人了！

## `ZagreusField_3780`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Aah, wealth!

**中文**

啊啊，是财富！

## `ZagreusField_3781`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oof, extra coin!

**中文**

哦哦，是额外的钱币！

## `ZagreusField_3782`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's mix things up, Daedalus.

**中文**

是时候强化一番了，狄德勒斯。

## `ZagreusField_3783`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What's it going to be?

**中文**

这次会怎么样呢？

## `ZagreusField_3784`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to mess with my weapon.

**中文**

是时候收拾收拾我的武器了。

## `ZagreusField_3785`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hit me, Daedalus.

**中文**

来锤炼我吧，狄德勒斯。

## `ZagreusField_3786`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Random enchantments.

**中文**

随机附魔。

## `ZagreusField_3787`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Surprise me, Daedalus.

**中文**

让我大吃一惊吧，狄德勒斯。

## `ZagreusField_3788`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's take our chances then.

**中文**

咱们来碰碰运气吧。

## `ZagreusField_3789`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The old Daedalus Surprise.

**中文**

老狄德勒斯给了我个惊喜。

## `ZagreusField_3790`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mm, got stronger there.

**中文**

嗯，变得更强了。

## `ZagreusField_3791`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tart and tasty!

**中文**

精妙绝伦！

## `ZagreusField_3792`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Boosted a Boon.

**中文**

祝福变强了。

## `ZagreusField_3793`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

One of my Boons got stronger.

**中文**

我的某个祝福变强了。

## `ZagreusField_3794`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Always hits the spot.

**中文**

总是这样恰到好处。

## `ZagreusField_3795`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That Boon got better, huh.

**中文**

那个祝福变得更强了，哈。

## `ZagreusField_3796`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Give me a better Boon.

**中文**

给我个更强的祝福吧。

## `ZagreusField_3797`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Which Boon's it going to be.

**中文**

会是哪个祝福呢？

## `ZagreusField_3798`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Who is it?

**中文**

是谁？

## `ZagreusField_3799`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's see who I get.

**中文**

让我看看是谁来了。

## `ZagreusField_3800`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Show yourself, Mystery God.

**中文**

现身吧，神秘的神明。

## `ZagreusField_3801`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Who's this, then?

**中文**

这是谁呢？

## `ZagreusField_3802`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Still in their packaging.

**中文**

他们还没露面呢。

## `ZagreusField_3803`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Who'd I get?

**中文**

会是谁呢？

## `ZagreusField_3804`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

That's right, Master Chaos. My father personally takes it upon himself to stop me in the event none of his underlings beat him to it. He... can be a handful.

**中文**

是这样的，卡俄斯君主。如果我父亲的手下没能阻止我的话，他就会亲自来带我回去。他……是个棘手的敌人。

## `ZagreusField_3805`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Erm... yes? Was I supposed to respond there?

**中文**

呃……是的？刚刚我应答了，这没问题吧？

## `ZagreusField_3806`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Considering I beat you last we met, I'd be much more concerned with your predictability than mine.

**中文**

上次我们打架的时候，赢的人可是我。我觉得和我相比，你更让人觉得一目了然。

## `ZagreusField_3807`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Erm, I don't know how to respond to what you said without directly disobeying your request, Alecto. But for future reference, fine, I'll bite.

**中文**

……呃，我可不知道怎么样才能既回答你，又不违抗你的意愿，阿勒克图。不过如果以后的话，好吧，我尽量吧。

## `ZagreusField_3808`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, as a matter of fact, I have. Though I don't see how that's any business of yours, Alecto.

**中文**

老实说，确实如此。不过我可不觉得这件事和你有什么关系，阿勒克图。

## `ZagreusField_3809`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm sensing a pattern that it really doesn't take much for you to have something to get me for, does it?

**中文**

我发现不管发生什么事情，你都能想办法赖到我的头上，跟我没完，对不对？

## `ZagreusField_3810`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wish you could join me, Alecto. Want me to put in a good word with Father next time, so you can come visit us at the House yourself?

**中文**

你要是能和我一起来就好了，阿勒克图。想让我下次在我父亲面前美言你几句吗？这样你就可以亲自来我们的圣殿里看看了。

## `ZagreusField_3811`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Alecto, I just like to see you menacingly smile. And, hey, having to deal with you now and again beats having to fight Meg all the time.

**中文**

我只是很喜欢看到你那充满恶意的笑容，阿勒克图。另外，和你没事打上几架，总比不得不和墨纪动手要好多了。

## `ZagreusField_3812`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, habits can be hard to break. As for how many times, I've really no idea. Though I guess there's one way to find out.

**中文**

这么说吧，积习难改。至于要多少次，老实说我自己也不知道。不过，我猜只有一个办法能弄清楚了。

## `ZagreusField_3813`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, don't fret. I was just trying to help her expand her somewhat limited vocabulary.

**中文**

哦，别担心，我只是帮助她扩充一下她贫乏的词汇量而已。

## `ZagreusField_3814`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Not interested in your nasty remarks about her, Alecto. Though if you keep them up, I'm warning you, I might get mad.

**中文**

你对她这些刻薄的评语我不感兴趣，阿勒克图。不要如果你打算继续说的话，我警告你，我可能会大发雷霆。

## `ZagreusField_3815`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Considering I beat you last we met, I'd be much more concerned with your predictability than mine.

**中文**

上次我们打架的时候，赢的人可是我。我觉得和我相比，你更让人觉得一目了然。

## `ZagreusField_3816`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...Erm, I don't know how to respond to what you said without directly disobeying your request, Alecto. But for future reference, fine, I'll bite.

**中文**

……呃，我可不知道怎么样才能既回答你，又不违抗你的意愿，阿勒克图。不过如果以后的话，好吧，我尽量吧。

## `ZagreusField_3817`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, as a matter of fact, I have. Though I don't see how that's any business of yours, Alecto.

**中文**

老实说，确实如此。不过我可不觉得这件事和你有什么关系，阿勒克图。

## `ZagreusField_3818`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm sensing a pattern that it really doesn't take much for you to have something to get me for, does it?

**中文**

我发现不管发生什么事情，你都能想办法赖到我的头上，跟我没完，对不对？

## `ZagreusField_3819`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Perish the thought, Alecto. What would you have done with yourself if not for all our fated encounters?

**中文**

还是别这么想了，阿勒克图。要不是因为我们命中注定要相遇，你现在已经干出什么事情了？

## `ZagreusField_3820`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Alecto!

**中文**

阿勒克图！

## `ZagreusField_3821`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Alecto...

**中文**

阿勒克图……

## `ZagreusField_3822`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You again.

**中文**

又是你。

## `ZagreusField_3823`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Lovely.

**中文**

很可爱。

## `ZagreusField_3824`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, murder. Zagreus. My name is Zagreus! Can you say that, Tisiphone? No murder. Zagreus!

**中文**

不是杀手，是扎格列欧斯，我叫扎格列欧斯！你能说出这个名字吗，提西福涅？不是杀手，是扎格列欧斯！

## `ZagreusField_3825`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Murderzer?!

**中文**

杀手扎格？！

## `ZagreusField_3826`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You really did say murderzer, didn't you! That's progress! Zagreus! I'm Zagreus, I'm not a murderer! Can you say Zagreus?

**中文**

你刚刚是不是说杀手扎格了！你有进步了！是扎格列欧斯！我是扎格列欧斯，不是杀手！你能说出“扎格列欧斯”这个词吗？

## `ZagreusField_3827`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Do you remember me, Tisiphone? My name is Zagreus. Prince of the Underworld? Pretty close with Megaera, I guess. Try saying Zagreus again for me?

**中文**

你还记得我吗，提西福涅？我是冥界王子扎格列欧斯，还记得吧？我猜，我和墨纪拉的关系还挺亲密的吧。可以再试试说“扎格列欧斯”这个词吗？

## `ZagreusField_3828`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Almost!

**中文**

就差一点了！

## `ZagreusField_3829`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, you're so close, Tisiphone! Remember, Zagreus? That's me. I'm not your enemy, you know, don't you know that? I'm only Zagreus!

**中文**

哦，你马上就成功了，提西福涅！还记得吗？我是扎格列欧斯。我不是你的敌人，你是明白的吧？我只是扎格列欧斯而已！

## `ZagreusField_3830`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's it!

**中文**

就是这样！

## `ZagreusField_3831`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No. Wait. Just the first part. No murderer. Just Zagreus. Or... let's see, how about... happy? Can you say happy?

**中文**

不对，等等。只要说前面那半句就行了，只要扎格列欧斯就好了。或者说……我们试试别的……幸福？你会说“幸福”这个词吗？

## `ZagreusField_3832`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's not happy...

**中文**

“幸福”可不是这么说的……

## `ZagreusField_3833`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, well... let's try again with happy, how about that? Happy? No murder, only happy. Come on, now!

**中文**

呃，好吧……我们再来试试幸福这个词吧，怎么样？幸福？不要说“杀手”，只说“幸福”。来，试试吧！

## `ZagreusField_3834`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Great.

**中文**

太棒了。

## `ZagreusField_3835`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Eugh, I guess the happy thing just isn't doing it for you. Though, maybe trying to kill me like this is as close as you can get. You learned my name at least, that's something, right?

**中文**

好吧，我猜幸福什么的你并不理解。不过，或许像这样想要杀我，对你而言已经最详实类似于幸福的东西了吧。至少你已经记住我的名字了，还是有进步的，是吧？

## `ZagreusField_3836`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

...Then we're back to square one. Look, I was just trying to get through to you with all the Zagreus and happy stuff. But, if you can understand any of this, Tisiphone, I think you're all right, just the way you are. Friends?

**中文**

……看来我们又回到原点了。你看，我只是想教会你说“扎格列欧斯”啊，“幸福”啊之类的词。不过如果你已经能理解这些词的话，提西福涅，那我觉得你就算是说不出来，也不算什么问题。我们算是朋友吗？

## `ZagreusField_3837`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Friends!

**中文**

我们是朋友！

## `ZagreusField_3838`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tisiphone!

**中文**

提西福涅！

## `ZagreusField_3839`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Erm... hello.

**中文**

呃……你好。

## `ZagreusField_3840`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Erm... hi.

**中文**

呃……嘿。

## `ZagreusField_3841`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Friends?

**中文**

我们是朋友吗？

## `ZagreusField_3842`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Friends? Maybe?

**中文**

我们是朋友吗？或许是吧？

## `ZagreusField_3843`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, look at you, Charon mate, you're looking well! Something's different about you, can't quite put my finger on it. Have you done something with your hair?

**中文**

哎呀，这不是我的好伙计卡戎吗，你看起来很不错！你好像有哪里不太一样了，但我又看不出来是哪里不一样了。你是换过发型了吗？

## `ZagreusField_3844`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hah, I knew it!

**中文**

哈，我就知道！

## `ZagreusField_3845`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Charon mate? As a longtime customer, I just wanted to take a moment to thank you again for your invaluable service as a part of my repeated attempts at getting out of here. What would I have done without your wares all these many times? Besides dying a lot faster.

**中文**

卡戎，我的好伙计？我一次又一次地想要逃出这个地方，作为你的忠实客户，我只想稍微花点时间感谢你出色的服务。在这么多次冒险之中，要是没了你的宝物，我会落得什么下场呢？恐怕只会死得更快吧。

## `ZagreusField_3846`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, I meant every word!

**中文**

我每个字都是认真的！

## `ZagreusField_3847`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're just testing me, here, right, Charon? I mean, when you place a huge sum of coin conspicuously within my reach, what do you expect me to do?

**中文**

你是在考验我，没错吧，卡戎？我是说，你把这么多亮闪闪的金币特意摆在我的面前，那你还指望我会怎么做？

## `ZagreusField_3848`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sounds good, mate, when I rob you from now on it's just my way of saying Charon, I would like to battle you to the death right now for a quick change of pace.

**中文**

听起来不错，伙计。我抢你的东西的时候，我其实在我用自己的方式对你说这句话：卡戎，我想和你打个至死方休，好调剂调剂心情。

## `ZagreusField_3849`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know, Charon, your associate Hermes apparently had a wager with you that I could beat you twice in a row at some point? I'd hate to disappoint him, so... no hard feelings?

**中文**

卡戎，你的同伴赫尔墨斯和你打了个赌，赌我有朝一日肯定能连续打败你两次，对不对？我可不想让他失望，所以……希望你别见怪，好吗？

## `ZagreusField_3850`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Erm, you're not angry with me, are you, Charon mate?

**中文**

呃，你没生我的气，是吧，卡戎老伙计？

## `ZagreusField_3851`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey Charon, you're not angry with me after we had it out last time, are you, I mean... I figured since you gave me one of your Loyalty Cards, must mean everything's fine, but... I'm... just making sure.

**中文**

嘿，卡戎，我们上次谈过之后，你已经不生我的气了吧？我是说……既然你已经把你的忠诚卡牌给我了，那我们肯定和好了。不过……我……只是想确认一下，没问题吧？

## `ZagreusField_3852`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well that is a relief!

**中文**

哈啊，这真是让人舒心！

## `ZagreusField_3853`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No hard feelings about that whole battle-to-the-death thing last time, right, Charon? Especially since you absolutely demolished me. I was just messing with you, you know that, right?

**中文**

我们上次恶战一场，那件事你别太往心里去了，好吗，卡戎？再说了，你已经把我狠狠地收拾了一顿。我当时只是在跟你开玩笑而已，你明白的吧？

## `ZagreusField_3854`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Always appreciate your sense of humor, mate.

**中文**

我喜欢你的幽默感，伙计。

## `ZagreusField_3855`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Charon, I realize you have a very busy schedule and certainly needn't keep going out of your way for me like this, so here. Just a small token of my appreciation for you. If you ever want to talk, know that I'm here.

**中文**

卡戎，我知道你一直都很忙，也知道你本不需要像这样为了我的事情分心。所以，请收下这个吧，是我送给你的一点点心意。如果你想找人聊聊的话，我随时愿意奉陪。

## `ZagreusField_3856`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Where's Skelly when you need him?

**中文**

需要骨头的时候，他跑哪里去了？

## `ZagreusField_3857`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Skelly, where are you?

**中文**

骨头，你去哪了？

## `ZagreusField_3858`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Blast, Skelly?!

**中文**

该死的，骨头？！

## `ZagreusField_3859`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

As I remain committed to my task, Goddess, I'm reminded none of this would have been possible without your aid. And so, I must give thanks, even if through a meager offering.

**中文**

女神，我在旅途的过程中意识到，如果不是因为您的帮助，我是没办法走到这里的。虽然我只能献给您一些微薄的礼物，但我仍然希望能表达我的谢意。

## `ZagreusField_3860`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lady Athena, I know there's more at stake for you in this than just my plight... you care so much about this family. That care and your determination spurs me on. I'm ever grateful.

**中文**

雅典娜女士，我明白比起帮助我，你其实还有很多重要的事情要做……你真的很在乎这个大家庭。你的指引与决心让我很受鼓舞，我对你非常感激。

## `ZagreusField_3861`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I know this Ambrosia originated on Olympus, Lady Athena. Though through its long voyage to these depths, I trust its savor is more worthy of your grace. No one's ever had a greater cousin. And I'll never forget what you've done for me.

**中文**

我知道这瓶仙馔密酒是在奥林匹斯山酿造的，雅典娜女士。虽然它一路辗转到了冥界，但我认为只有您的恩典才配得上它甘甜的味道。没有哪个兄弟姐妹能比您更优秀了，我永远都不会忘记您对我的恩情。

## `ZagreusField_3862`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I salute you, Uncle Poseidon! Your strength and confidence inspire me, and you accepted me as part of the family without question. It means a lot.

**中文**

我向您致敬，波塞冬叔叔！您的力量与信心鼓舞了我，而且您把我当做您真真正正的家人，这对我而言非常重要。

## `ZagreusField_3863`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Whenever I encounter you, Uncle Poseidon, my spirits lift, and I feel there must not be anything that can stop me. I only wish we met sooner, though I'm grateful to have met you at all.

**中文**

波塞冬叔叔，每次见到您的时候，我都觉得斗志昂扬，感觉没什么困难能阻止我前进。我很庆幸能遇到您，如果我能早点遇到您就好了。

## `ZagreusField_3864`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Uncle Poseidon, sometimes I wonder what my life would have been like had you been a part of it sooner. Your encouragement, your enthusiasm... it means a lot. To you! This Ambrosia's been to hell and back!

**中文**

波塞冬叔叔，有的时候我不禁会想，如果能早点遇到您的话，我的生命会变成什么样子呢。您的鼓舞，您乐观的态度……对我非常重要。这是敬你的！让冥界的仙馔密酒回到奥林匹斯山上吧！

## `ZagreusField_3865`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You have shown me nothing but respect and granted me your favor repeatedly, Lord Ares. This is the least that I can do, in kind.

**中文**

阿瑞斯大人，您对我非常尊重，一直赐予我您的礼物。这是我的一点薄利，还请您笑纳。

## `ZagreusField_3866`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lord Ares! As bloodshed has become somewhat of a necessity in my situation, I am very grateful that you're with me in this. I know never to take it for granted.

**中文**

阿瑞斯大人！我目前的处境逼得我不得不大开杀戒，我很感激您能与我并肩作战。我明白，我不应该理所当然地接受您的好意。

## `ZagreusField_3867`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I offer this Ambrosia in your name and honor, Lord Ares. To the god of war! How many foes have I vanquished with your aid and guidance? But more than that, I'm pleased to know you, and to be your cousin.

**中文**

这瓶仙馔密酒是献给您的礼物，敬伟大的战神！有了您的帮助，我都数不清自己消灭过多少敌人了。不仅仅如此，能认识你，成为你的表兄弟，我感到很开心。

## `ZagreusField_3868`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, on no occasion whatsoever, cheers to you again, Lord Dionysus mate! This place is so much less dreary under your influence!

**中文**

嘿，让我敬你一杯吧，迪奥尼索司大人，我的好伙计！幸亏有了你，这个地方才没那么让人觉得闷！

## `ZagreusField_3869`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Dionysus mate, sometimes things weigh heavily on me, but then I hear from you, and it's like I don't have a care in the world! To your health!

**中文**

迪奥尼索司，我的好伙计。有时候我感觉肩上的担子太重了，可只要一听到你的声音，我就觉得什么事情都不用担心了！为你的健康干杯！

## `ZagreusField_3870`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What does one offer to the god of wine? The very best, is what, and down here, this is it, mate! I'm grateful I've got some of your spirit in me. To have such a connection, with you despite all the distance between us...!

**中文**

什么礼物能配得上酒神呢？当然是世界上最好的酒啦，收下这个吧，伙计！我很感激能受到你的影响。尽管我们之间相隔这么远，可我们还是紧密地连接在一起……！

## `ZagreusField_3871`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Lady Demeter, I am forever in your debt for the affection that you've shown to me, and for the power you've bestowed.

**中文**

得墨忒尔女士，你给了我那么多爱，还赐予了我那么多力量，我永远欠你的情。

## `ZagreusField_3872`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I often remember that there's sweetness in this world only by your grace, Lady Demeter. I'm thankful that we've grown closer through all this.

**中文**

我经常会想起，只有有了你的恩赐，这个世上才会有甜蜜和美好，得墨忒尔女士。能与你变得越来越亲密，我感到很开心。

## `ZagreusField_3873`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This one's for you, swift and sure-footed messenger of the gods! You make what you do look easy. But I know you're going out of your way. Thank you so much, Hermes.

**中文**

这是给你的礼物，为众神送信的敏捷信使！你为我做的这些看起来只是举手之劳，但我明白，这并不是你分内的事情。真的很感谢你，赫尔墨斯。

## `ZagreusField_3874`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I salute you, Lord Hermes, with this rarest of delicacies! Pretty rare here, anyway. You never show it, but I can only imagine how hard you must be working to back me up. Cheers, mate!

**中文**

赫尔墨斯大人，让我用这最珍贵的蜜酒来敬你一杯吧，总之在我们这里是挺珍贵的。虽然你从来没说过，但我明白，你肯定在我身上花了相当多的心思。干杯吧，朋友！

## `ZagreusField_3875`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Message for you, Lord Hermes! It says, 'This offering is dedicated to the incomparable god of swiftness, from whom the news is always good, because he's bringing it. You are a real mate, you know that? I really owe you one.' Zagreus.

**中文**

这里有一封送给你的信，赫尔墨斯大人！信上说：“无与伦比的敏捷之神啊，这是送给你的礼物。只要能收到信，我就会很开心，因为你带来的总是好消息。你知道吗？你是我真正的好朋友，我真的欠你个人情。”寄信人：扎格列欧斯。

## `ZagreusField_3876`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't understand this place at all. How is it you're still champion after all this time? After losing repeatedly and often, I mean.

**中文**

我真搞不清楚这个地方是怎么回事，你怎么还能是这里的最强战士呢？我是说，我明明都吊打过你这么多次了。

## `ZagreusField_3877`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's all right, Theseus. We have a splendid painting of you back at the House, for those moments when I invariably forget you exist. You were quite dashing with long hair, you know!

**中文**

别介意这个，忒修斯。我们在圣殿里挂了一副你的肖像画，万一我忘了你长什么样，看一眼就能想起来了。不过说真的，你留长发的样子还真是挺时髦的！

## `ZagreusField_3878`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You do know I'm related to them, right? The god of the dead is my father? I'm sure word's gotten around. Not that I want deferential treatment from the likes of you.

**中文**

你知道我和那些神有血缘关系，是吧？你也知道冥王是我父亲吧？我很确定这些消息早就传开了。不过，我这么说，可不是为了能得到你这种人的优待。

## `ZagreusField_3879`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What? You think I'm here to ruin your friendship? Look, to each his own in that regard, though since you bring it up, I really don't see why Asterius puts up with you.

**中文**

你想什么呢？你以为我是为了毁了你们的友情，才会来这里的吗？听着，我明白每个人都有自己的择友标准，但既然你把话挑明了，我也就实话实说了。我真不明白阿斯忒里俄斯到底是看上你哪里了。

## `ZagreusField_3880`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Asterius, about that, please consider it a standing offer, if you like. I'd rather have you as a friend than as a foe. Not sure that I can say the same for Theseus, of course.

**中文**

关于这一点，阿斯忒里俄斯，如果你愿意的话，我的提案长期有效。比起与你刀剑相向，我更希望能和你成为朋友。不过，同样的话我恐怕是不会对忒修斯说的。

## `ZagreusField_3881`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, glad that's settled, then!

**中文**

那就好，很高兴这件事解决了！

## `ZagreusField_3882`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Prepared for me, really? I've dismantled your ridiculous chariot before. Even Poseidon knows you're just a blowhard with a fancy ride.

**中文**

已经准备好了？你确定吗？你那辆滑稽的战车我早就拆过一次了，就连波塞冬都知道，你不过是个花架子罢了。

## `ZagreusField_3883`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Theseus, you do know you look even more ridiculous than normal wearing that mask? I get the chariot and the unsporting weaponry, but why wear that thing?

**中文**

忒修斯，你知道吗？你戴上那个面具之后，看起来比平时更滑稽了。我理解你喜欢乘着战车，喜欢挥舞那把华而不实的武器，但那个面具是怎么回事？

## `ZagreusField_3884`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, this? I wouldn't say I stole it, so much as naturally wooed the Fates into bestowing it upon me. You could say I'm kind of testing it out.

**中文**

哦，这个吗？我可不觉得这是偷来的，更像是命运女神把它赐给我的。我算是在试试它到底有多厉害吧。

## `ZagreusField_3885`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What are you even talking about? I barely get a word in edgewise, much less Asterius over there. You know what? Fine. I've had it with you, anyway.

**中文**

你到底在说什么傻话呢？我根本不知道你在说什么，阿斯忒里俄斯就更听不懂了。不过算了，你爱怎么样就怎么样吧，反正我也已经受够你了。

## `ZagreusField_3886`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I've no such fear, myself, Theseus. Though, I appreciate you noticing. You're quite an inspiration, you know? I wouldn't come all this way to destroy just anyone.

**中文**

我从来都没担心过这种事情，忒修斯，不过，很高兴你能注意到这一点。说真的，你其实是个很能鼓舞人心的家伙。我大老远地来到这里，可不是为了吊打某个无名小卒。

## `ZagreusField_3887`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Asterius. Theseus.

**中文**

阿斯忒里俄斯，忒修斯。

## `ZagreusField_3888`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Asterius. Other guy.

**中文**

阿斯忒里俄斯，还有另一个家伙。

## `ZagreusField_3889`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Rematch?

**中文**

要再打一场吗？

## `ZagreusField_3890`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You never seemed particularly concerned even before, sir. Must say you're very good at hiding your worries.

**中文**

我之前还以为你并不怎么在乎这件事情呢，先生。不得不说，你真的很善于隐藏自己的忧愁。

## `ZagreusField_3891`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh! Well, I suppose now that you're free, you could always see for yourself? It's pretty different as soon as you reach Asphodel. Don't you have someplace or somebody you'd like to see?

**中文**

哦！既然你已经自由了，我猜你也能亲眼看看这个世界了吧？等你到了水仙花平原之后，所有的风景都不一样了。你应该有想见的人，或是想去的地方吧？

## `ZagreusField_3892`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I have to tell you, Sisyphus. Sometimes I wish I knew more about your past. You must have been a great king.

**中文**

这句话我一定得告诉你，西西弗斯。有的时候我在想，我要是对你的过去更了解一些就好了，你生前肯定是个了不起的国王。

## `ZagreusField_3893`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I hear you, sir. Though, whatever you did in your mortal days... you've since more than paid for it, I'm sure.... Wait, is that why you're still here, in Tartarus? Because you think you deserve this?

**中文**

好吧，先生。虽然不知道你生前做过什么……但我敢肯定，你的罪行肯定早就还清了……等等，你就是因为这个才待在塔耳塔罗斯的吗？你觉得这是你应得的下场？

## `ZagreusField_3894`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You heard correctly, Sisyphus! Orpheus is reunited with his muse, at last. Or at least, he gets to visit on the regular, when he isn't busy at my father's house. So you've heard of them, have you?

**中文**

你说得一点都没错，西西弗斯！俄耳甫斯和他的缪斯女神团聚了。或者说，他可以趁圣殿里的工作没那么忙的时候，时不时地去见见她了。原来你听说过他们，是吗？

## `ZagreusField_3895`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

And what routine is that, exactly, sir?

**中文**

你说的日常生活，具体指的是什么，先生？

## `ZagreusField_3896`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, you were right. I was never comfortable performing Father's routine Tartarus inspections. Though those responsibilities of mine didn't last long.

**中文**

哦，你想得没错。父亲让我对塔耳塔罗斯进行例行检查，我从来都不喜欢做这种事情。不过，我也没做多久就是了。

## `ZagreusField_3897`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come now, sir, we both know full well Bouldy's much more than that. You really think I doubt the power of Bouldy after everything we've been through?

**中文**

别逗了，先生，我们都清楚石头可不仅仅只是块石头。我们一起经历了这么多事情，你真的以为我还会怀疑石头的力量吗？

## `ZagreusField_3898`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, Thanatos is doing all right. Much like my father, I think he feels he has some sort of fearsome reputation to uphold. Or maybe he's perpetually stressed over his work. But he's been well lately.

**中文**

哦，塔纳托斯一切都好。他和我父亲很像，我觉得，他认为他需要维持某种令人心生畏惧的气场，也可能是他只是工作压力太大罢了。不过他最近都很好。

## `ZagreusField_3899`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Apparently word gets around, but yes, you could say that I guess. That Theseus, though... he doesn't care for me one bit, let me tell you!

**中文**

这么说就实在是有点夸张了，不过，我猜这么说倒也没错。不过那个忒修斯……实话告诉你吧，他一点都不在乎我！.

## `ZagreusField_3900`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Got you, mate! Twice in a row. I guess Hermes wins his little wager with you, then. You tell him I said hrrrrrnnnggghh!

**中文**

是我赢了，伙计！我已经连续赢两次了。看来，赫尔墨斯和你打的赌是他赢了。你可以告诉它，我说了句喝啊啊啊啊啊啊啊！

## `ZagreusField_3901`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I thought you'd like it, sir. And so, I'm pleased to offer you another! Almost a shame we seldom get such imports from Olympus around here.

**中文**

我想着你会喜欢的，大人。如果你喜欢的话，我愿意再给你拿来一瓶。遗憾的是，我们这里很少能拿到这种来自奥林匹斯山的好东西。

## `ZagreusField_3902`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I thought you'd like it, sir. And so, I'm pleased to offer you another! Almost a shame we seldom get such imports from Olympus around here. I hope I can appreciate it for myself sometime. Once all this is over with, I think.

**中文**

我想着你会喜欢的，大人。如果你喜欢的话，我愿意再给你拿来一瓶。遗憾的是，我们这里很少能拿到这种来自奥林匹斯山的好东西。我希望有机会我也能亲自品尝一番。等这一切都尘埃落定吧。

## `ZagreusField_3903`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nice place you got here, mate!

**中文**

你这地方很不错嘛，伙计！

## `ZagreusField_3904`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

They basically turn into big crystals.

**中文**

他们差不多算是变成大水晶了。

## `ZagreusField_3905`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's do this again sometime!

**中文**

下次有机会再来一场吧！

## `ZagreusField_3906`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm sure the Witches Circle will reconvene soon enough.

**中文**

我敢肯定，用不了多久，那些女巫又会重新碰面。

## `ZagreusField_3907`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Underworld Customs...

**中文**

是冥界海关……

## `ZagreusField_3908`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There you go, Underworld Customs.

**中文**

去吧，冥界海关。

## `ZagreusField_3909`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This one's for you, Underworld Customs.

**中文**

这是给你的，冥界海关。

## `ZagreusField_3910`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Only way to get through Customs.

**中文**

想通过海关，只有一个办法。

## `ZagreusField_3911`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got to get through Customs.

**中文**

我得通过海关才行。

## `ZagreusField_3912`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How about a different set of choices?

**中文**

不如给我些别的东西吧？

## `ZagreusField_3913`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How about a different set of choices, then.

**中文**

不如给我些别的东西吧，怎么样？

## `ZagreusField_3914`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hmm, let's see what else.

**中文**

嗯，让我看看有没有别的吧。

## `ZagreusField_3915`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Any other blessings, maybe?

**中文**

有没有其它的祝福呢？

## `ZagreusField_3916`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hate to be ungrateful here...

**中文**

我不想当个不知感恩的人……

## `ZagreusField_3917`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Erm, got anything else?

**中文**

呃，还有其它的吗？

## `ZagreusField_3918`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How about some other Boons instead.

**中文**

不如给我其它的祝福看看吧。

## `ZagreusField_3919`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey, got anything else?

**中文**

嘿，有别的东西吗？

## `ZagreusField_3920`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Any other Boons for me?

**中文**

还有其它的祝福可以看看吗？

## `ZagreusField_3921`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I would like none of these.

**中文**

这些东西我都不怎么喜欢。

## `ZagreusField_3922`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Charon mate, I know you meet an awful lot of dead, and it probably gets very difficult to remember them all, but... I don't suppose the name Skelly means anything to you, does it?

**中文**

卡戎，我的好伙计，我知道你见过太多太多死人了，估计你很难记住他们所有人的名字，不过……不知道你有没有听说过“骨头”这个名字？有吗？

## `ZagreusField_3923`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ah, right, didn't think so. Forget I asked!

**中文**

啊，是啊，我没想到。忘了我这些话吧！

## `ZagreusField_3924`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Charon? Unrelated to anything whatsoever, I just wanted to say... I'm very grateful to have your support. You've always done your own thing... I never get to see you at the House. But I know you care! An awful lot. So, cheers.

**中文**

是卡戎吗？我没什么事情，我只是想说……我很感激你能支持我。你总是在忙你自己的事情……我从来没在圣殿里见过你。但我知道你关心我！你非常在乎我。所以，谢谢你。

## `ZagreusField_3925`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bone Hydra, you look... different...

**中文**

骸骨九头蛇，你看起来……不太一样了……

## `ZagreusField_3926`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bone Hydra, you look positively smashing!

**中文**

骸骨九头蛇，你看起来真是太拉风了！

## `ZagreusField_3927`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Changed up your look again, have you, Hydra?

**中文**

你又变了模样，是吧，九头蛇？

## `ZagreusField_3928`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Another new look for you I see, Bone Hydra.

**中文**

你又换了新的样子，骸骨九头蛇。

## `ZagreusField_3929`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, look at you, Lernie!

**中文**

哎哟，看看你吧，勒拿！

## `ZagreusField_3930`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

May I say, you look lovely this day or night, Lernie?

**中文**

可以让我说一句吗？你看起来实在是太可爱了，勒拿。

## `ZagreusField_3931`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

May I say, you look lovely this whatever-time-it-is, Lernie?

**中文**

可以让我说一句吗？你看起来真是太可爱了，勒拿。

## `ZagreusField_3932`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whoa that's not good...

**中文**

哇啊，这可不太妙……

## `ZagreusField_3933`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

New tricks, there, Bone Hydra?

**中文**

你是学了新的招数吗，骸骨九头蛇？

## `ZagreusField_3934`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh that's not good.

**中文**

哦，这恐怕不太妙。

## `ZagreusField_3935`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Now that's not nice at all.

**中文**

这可一点都不好。

## `ZagreusField_3936`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I do not appreciate that!

**中文**

我可不喜欢这样！

## `ZagreusField_3937`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh gods, now this?

**中文**

哦，天啊，这么搞我吗？

## `ZagreusField_3938`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right, Doomstone.

**中文**

好了，末日巨岩。

## `ZagreusField_3939`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Doomstone! Hi.

**中文**

你好啊，末日巨岩！

## `ZagreusField_3940`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Come get smashed to bits.

**中文**

我要把你砸成碎片。

## `ZagreusField_3941`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Blasted Doomstone, then.

**中文**

好吧，是该死的末日巨岩。

## `ZagreusField_3942`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll break you again.

**中文**

让我再拆你一次吧。

## `ZagreusField_3943`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You're free, tortured souls trapped in a crystal thing.

**中文**

被困在水晶里的灵魂啊，你们自由了。

## `ZagreusField_3944`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Eugh, that thing.

**中文**

呃，是那玩意儿。

## `ZagreusField_3945`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

One less giant evil crystal, then.

**中文**

又少了一块邪恶的巨型水晶。

## `ZagreusField_3946`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Smashing!

**中文**

粉身碎骨！

## `ZagreusField_3947`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I see you, Sneak.

**中文**

我看到你了，伏击者。

## `ZagreusField_3948`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's go, Sneak!

**中文**

我们开战吧，伏击者！

## `ZagreusField_3949`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You and me, Sneak!

**中文**

来和我单挑吧，伏击者！

## `ZagreusField_3950`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A Wretched Sneak...

**中文**

是邪恶伏击者……

## `ZagreusField_3951`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Come try me, Sneak.

**中文**

来试试我的能耐吧，伏击者。

## `ZagreusField_3952`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Who's laughing now?

**中文**

看看是谁笑到最后了？

## `ZagreusField_3953`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Stay out of my way next time.

**中文**

下次别挡我的路。

## `ZagreusField_3954`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Takes care of that.

**中文**

解决掉了。

## `ZagreusField_3955`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You stab me, I stab you.

**中文**

你想砍我，那我也砍回去。

## `ZagreusField_3956`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Witches Circle!

**中文**

是女巫们！

## `ZagreusField_3957`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Break it up, Witches!

**中文**

四散而逃吧，女巫们！

## `ZagreusField_3958`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mind if I join the fray, Witches?

**中文**

不介意我也加进去吧，女巫？

## `ZagreusField_3959`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well I, for one, had a lovely time.

**中文**

好吧，我还是玩得挺尽兴的。

## `ZagreusField_3960`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bye now, ladies.

**中文**

再见了，女士们。

## `ZagreusField_3961`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's do this again sometime all right?

**中文**

下次有机会再来一次吧，好吗？

## `ZagreusField_3962`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What... he meant Skelly's tooth?

**中文**

什么……他说的是骨头的牙齿吗？

## `ZagreusField_3963`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Strictly business.

**中文**

公事公办。

## `ZagreusField_3964`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No hard feelings.

**中文**

别太难过。

## `ZagreusField_3965`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See you back home.

**中文**

我们回家再见吧。

## `ZagreusField_3966`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bye, Meg.

**中文**

再见，墨纪。

## `ZagreusField_3967`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nice seeing you?

**中文**

很高兴见到你？

## `ZagreusField_3968`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nice seeing you.

**中文**

很高兴见到你。

## `ZagreusField_3969`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, it's been lovely.

**中文**

好吧，她真可爱。

## `ZagreusField_3970`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hope she's not mad.

**中文**

希望她别太生我的气。

## `ZagreusField_3971`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

She's going to be mad.

**中文**

她肯定会大发雷霆的。

## `ZagreusField_3972`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Um. Sorry, wrong chamber!

**中文**

呃，抱歉，我走错地方了！

## `ZagreusField_3973`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

And, I'm locked in.

**中文**

我被锁在里面了。

## `ZagreusField_3974`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Found it fast this time.

**中文**

这次找得还蛮快的。

## `ZagreusField_3975`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Found it already.

**中文**

已经找到了。

## `ZagreusField_3976`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey, a Satyr Sack already.

**中文**

嘿，是萨提尔的麻袋。

## `ZagreusField_3977`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Satyr Sack already, what do you know.

**中文**

你猜怎么着？是萨提尔的麻袋。

## `ZagreusField_3978`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There it is already.

**中文**

它就在那里。

## `ZagreusField_3979`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Eugh, finally.

**中文**

呃，终于找到了。

## `ZagreusField_3980`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There's the Sack, finally.

**中文**

终于找到那个麻袋了。

## `ZagreusField_3981`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Been looking all over for you.

**中文**

我找你找得好苦啊。

## `ZagreusField_3982`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cerberus must be starving.

**中文**

刻耳柏洛斯肯定饿坏了。

## `ZagreusField_3983`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Finally found it.

**中文**

终于找到了。

## `ZagreusField_3984`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thank you, mate.

**中文**

谢谢你，伙计。

## `ZagreusField_3985`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Off we go.

**中文**

我们走。

## `ZagreusField_3986`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

And we're off.

**中文**

我们走吧。

## `ZagreusField_3987`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

**EN**

**EN**

## `ZagreusField_4107`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

## `ZagreusField_4108`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

啊，我之前没想过。

**EN**

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**
