## `ZagreusField_4260`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, you're from Crete, originally, aren't you, Asterius? I know this is a stretch, but... I don't suppose you ever heard of a commander by the name of Schelemeus? Captain of the Cretan second fleet, something like that?

**中文**

你是从克里特岛来的，是吧，阿斯忒里俄斯？我知道这挺麻烦的，不过……你不会刚好听说过某个叫瑟勒米乌斯的指挥官吧？他是克里特岛第二舰队的船长之类的，听说过吗？

## `ZagreusField_4261`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, Schelemeus was a real person, then? But, Skelly said... ah, you know what, it's nothing, Asterius. Shall we?

**中文**

什么？原来瑟勒米乌斯这个名字不是瞎编的吗？可骨头说……啊，算了，不用在意了，阿斯忒里俄斯。咱们可以动手了吗？

## `ZagreusField_4262`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come on, Asterius, we've fought how many times already? What's there to dream about?

**中文**

不是吧，阿斯忒里俄斯，咱们都打过多少次了？这有什么好梦到的？

## `ZagreusField_4263`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, I know what that is, that's a waking-phrase. So I'm to speak it to Malphon. Wait, why are you helping me?

**中文**

嘿，我知道你说的是什么，那是句唤醒咒语。好，那我就对玛尔封说吧。等等，你为什么要帮助我？

## `ZagreusField_4264`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I see you overcome the wild and make peace with death!

**中文**

我见到你征服了荒野，与死亡谈笑风生！

## `ZagreusField_4265`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Just been a little tied up lately is all, but I will get to it, Asterius! I guess you'll want to know the result?

**中文**

我只是最近有点忙而已，不过我会和它说的，阿斯忒里俄斯！我猜你也很好奇结果吧？

## `ZagreusField_4266`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Former property of someone called Gilgamesh. The beast-man he fought, Enkidu... you said they became friends? What said your dream about us on that front, sir?

**中文**

这对拳套曾经的主人叫吉尔伽美什，而他打败的那只兽人叫恩奇都……你说他们后来成为了朋友？你的梦里有没有说过我们以后的关系会怎么样，先生？

## `ZagreusField_4267`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You've never been outside? Oh, right... the labyrinth thing. You lived in a horrible maze all your life. I haven't seen much of the surface, but... it's beautiful. Cold. When I'm there, though, I... feel out of place.

**中文**

你从来没见过外面的世界吗？哦，对了……是那个迷宫，你一辈子都生活在那座可怕的迷宫里。我也没怎么见过地上世界，不过……那里很美好，也很冷。不过，在地上世界的时候，我会……觉得不自在。

## `ZagreusField_4268`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, for me, at least, it happens to mean quite a bit. I'm her son!

**中文**

这么说吧，至少对我而言意义深远，因为我是她的儿子！

## `ZagreusField_4269`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Say, Skelly... or should I say Schelemeus? I happened to have a chat with somebody familiar with that name. Large bull-headed fellow originally from Crete. You said you made it all up! What's going on?

**中文**

我说，骨头……或者说，应该叫你瑟勒米乌斯？我刚好碰到了某个从克里特岛来的牛头人，和他聊了聊，他听说过这个名字。你之前说，那些事情都是你编出来的！这是怎么回事？

## `ZagreusField_4270`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I don't know. I've never met one. Do they all just stand around doing nothing?

**中文**

我不知道，我从来没见过他们，他们全都站在那里无所事事吗？

## `ZagreusField_4271`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, what about Hermes? He gave me the impression you might have been somebody else. Nobody in particular. Not some great sea-captain.

**中文**

等等，那赫尔墨斯呢？他暗示我你可能撒了谎。你可能不是什么重要人物，不是什么伟大的船长。

## `ZagreusField_4272`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Don't change the subject!

**中文**

别转移话题！

## `ZagreusField_4273`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Argh.

**中文**

啊。

## `ZagreusField_4274`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well no, in this case, those rumors must have been quite accurate, it's just, I'm back! Realized I didn't want a future for myself that didn't include crushing you repeatedly like this.

**中文**

老实说，不是，这些传言说得一点都没错，只不过我又回来了！我只是意识到，我可不希望以后没办法再一次一次把你碾死，就像这样。

## `ZagreusField_4275`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Yeah, about that? She's my mother. So maybe watch your fool mouth with me, or else I might just put in a bad word!

**中文**

是吗，真的吗？她可是我的母亲。所以你说话小心点，否则我只要说你几句坏话，你恐怕就要凉了！

## `ZagreusField_4276`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I know a few. As a matter of fact, I heard recently from Hermes, wanted you to know he felt sorry for how things ended up. That something to do with when Orpheus went in here after you?

**中文**

我确实认识几个。说实话，我最近还听到了赫尔墨斯的消息，他想让我告诉你，他对你们之间的事情很遗憾。你来这里之后，俄耳甫斯也来了，是和这件事有关吗？

## `ZagreusField_4277`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

No, wait, you're onto something... I think you must have sensed Orpheus' Distant Memory! He gave it to me as a keepsake of our friendship. Though, something like this... I feel like it should belong to you.

**中文**

不，等等，你说得没错……我觉得肯定是感觉到了俄耳甫斯的遥远记忆！他把这件礼物给了我，作为我们友情的信物。不过像这样的东西……我觉得给你更合适。

## `ZagreusField_4278`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, you mentioned Orpheus... my father had him locked up for a little while for refusing to sing. Being court musician and all. But he's out now, and doing OK! I think.

**中文**

这么说吧，你之前提到的俄耳甫斯……他是宫廷乐师之类的，但他拒绝给我父亲唱歌，所以我父亲把他关了一阵子。不过他已经出来了，我觉得他挺好的。

## `ZagreusField_4279`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, I... well, looking back, all this stuff between you and Orpheus, I realized... well, I hope I wasn't too pushy about the whole thing. It wasn't any of my business to pry into your personal life. I should have asked.

**中文**

哦，我……回头想想你和俄耳甫斯之间的事情，我突然意识到……希望我没有太操之过急吧。我并不应该窥探你们之间的私事，我应该提前和你说一声才对。

## `ZagreusField_4280`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, yes, but... wait. How else are you supposed to eat those things?

**中文**

呃，是啊，不过……等等，洋葱不就是应该生吃的吗？

## `ZagreusField_4281`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I've never met another nymph, now that you mention it. Heard of a few! The goddess Artemis, sounds like she spends more time with nymphs than the other Olympians.

**中文**

被你这么一说，我发现确实没见过其它仙女，不过我听说过几个！阿尔忒弥斯女神似乎更喜欢和仙女们呆在一起，而不是和其他的奥林匹斯众神打交道。

## `ZagreusField_4282`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hey, I know this one...!

**中文**

嘿，我认识这家伙……！

## `ZagreusField_4283`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Eurydice, did you by any chance ever meet a Persephone during the whole thing when Orpheus came down to try to get you?

**中文**

欧律狄刻，俄耳甫斯过来找你，想把你救出冥界的这个过程中，你有没有刚好见过一个叫珀尔塞福涅的人？

## `ZagreusField_4284`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's complicated. And, please keep it quiet. I was just wondering... what was she like? If you recall.

**中文**

这件事情很复杂，希望你不要说出去。我很好奇……她是个什么样子的人呢？如果你还能想起来的话。

## `ZagreusField_4285`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, she is back, and yes, it is good news, thank you. She and I have a lot of making up for lost time left to do, though I think we're off to a pretty decent start.

**中文**

嘿，她是回来了。而且没错，这是个好消息，谢谢。我和她得好好弥补一下错过的时光，不过我觉得已经有了个不错的开端。

## `ZagreusField_4286`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, he's taking it... about as well as he's taken anything ever, now that you mention it. Still very much himself, mind you, but still... it's been a welcome change.

**中文**

哦，既然你提起来了，父亲正在接受这些变化……就像他接受其它的事情一样。我得提醒你，他和之前没什么太大区别，不过……这总归是个可喜的变化。

## `ZagreusField_4287`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I think the experience of trying to rescue you, failing, dying, becoming court musician, refusing to sing, then being locked in solitary confinement, then finding out about you probably did a number on him. Why, is everything all right? You seemed to be getting along last I saw.

**中文**

我猜这可能是因为他试着救你，结果失败了，自己后来也死了，死后成了宫廷乐师，却拒绝唱歌，结果被单独监禁了起来，之后得知了你的事情，可能这一切给他的压力太大了吧。怎么了，一切还好吗？自从上次见到你们之后，你们好像一直相处得很好。

## `ZagreusField_4288`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, no, he's been boasting of my fabricated exploits to you? I've tried to tell him I was only joking with him but now he just thinks I'm being modest.

**中文**

啊，不是吧，他真的把我编出来的传奇故事和你说了？我之前想让他明白，我只是在和他开玩笑而已，但他似乎觉得我只是谦虚而已。

## `ZagreusField_4289`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Eurydice, I was thinking maybe this would benefit your culinary practice. Here, please.

**中文**

欧律狄刻，我觉得这件礼物说不定能提高你的厨艺。来，收下吧。

## `ZagreusField_4290`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Absolutely! Least I could do for all your friendship and support. Always happy to hear your voice when I'm out here.

**中文**

当然了！为了答谢你的友谊与支持，至少这些是我该做的。只要在这里的时候，我总是很高兴能听到你的声音。

## `ZagreusField_4291`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know what, yes, I do, Eurydice. Things have moved so fast for me lately I barely stopped to think about it, though. It helps not having to go through everything alone... just knowing I'll see them again soon.

**中文**

老实说，是啊，欧律狄刻。不过，最近发生的事情实在是太多了，我都没什么机会好好思考这些事情。这样一来，我就不用什么事情都自己扛着了……我知道，我很快就能再见到那个人了。

## `ZagreusField_4292`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I play a bit of the lyre, as a matter of fact! Orpheus himself gave me some pointers on it. Never really tried singing, though. I can't even imagine it, really.

**中文**

老实说，我试过弹奏里拉琴！俄耳甫斯本人也给了我一些建议。不过我还没试过唱歌呢，老实说，我根本想象不出来我唱歌的样子。

## `ZagreusField_4293`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey did Orpheus really use to have curls, and... something called a tan? I've only ever seen him looking... well, how he does now.

**中文**

俄耳甫斯以前真的留着卷发吗，而且……据说还“晒黑”了？我只见过他……呃，他现在的样子。

## `ZagreusField_4294`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I think so, sir. I'll still be passing through, and visiting the surface every opportunity I get. But it seems my place really is here. Besides, it means getting to see you every now and then, like this.

**中文**

我想是的，先生。我还是会路过这里，一有机会就会去地上世界，不过看起来，冥界才是我真正的归宿。另外，这意味着我时不时地能来见你，就像这样。

## `ZagreusField_4295`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's a long story, at this point, indeed. But yes. The Queen... my mother... she's returned. And, it's been really good, having her back.

**中文**

是啊，说来话长。不过，没错，冥后……我的母亲……她回来了。而且有她在身边，我真的很开心。

## `ZagreusField_4296`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, Master Chaos... you should know I have located her, already. Though where she is, I cannot stay for long. So I am visiting, as often as I can. It's... not unlike our visits here. Only, somehow, even harder to get to where she is.

**中文**

哦，卡俄斯君主……我得告诉你，我已经找到她所在之处了。不过她在的地方我没法长待，所以我只能尽可能地一次次去看她。这和……我们之间的见面倒是蛮像的，只是她在的地方更加难以到达。

## `ZagreusField_4297`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Master Chaos, it's Persephone, my mother. She's returned! She's chosen to return. So... things feel different, certainly, with her around, although... I didn't feel any sort of shift.

**中文**

卡俄斯君主，是珀尔塞福涅，我的母亲，她回来了！她是自愿回来的，所以……这里有了她之后，一切肯定都应该不太一样了。不过……我倒没觉得有什么变化。

## `ZagreusField_4298`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Master Chaos, it seems I've developed something of a talent around breaking out of here. Or, there, I mean, the Underworld, so... it's become more of an official role for me, I guess. If escape shouldn't be possible, then someone needs to find the gaps.

**中文**

卡俄斯君主，看起来我多少已经培养出了逃出这个地方的才能，也就是说，逃出冥界。所以……对我而言，逃脱成了一件正式的职务吧，我猜。如果要确保没人能逃出这里的话，那就必须有人能找到看守方面的漏洞才行。

## `ZagreusField_4299`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, I'm glad you asked, it's just... my mother, she's... we're reaching out to the Olympians. Trying to reconnect the family. It's been a while, relatively, since she and Father saw the others, but... in case a gathering occurs, I did wish you to know you're cordially invited, too.

**中文**

哦，很高兴您问了，只是……我的母亲，她……我们正在努力与奥林匹斯众神的联系，想要重新建立家人之间的纽带。她和我父亲见面，已经有一段时间了，不过……万一我们举行聚会的话，我想让您知道，我们也诚挚地邀请您来参加。

## `ZagreusField_4300`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wait, how do you even know all that? And should you really be talking about the Queen that way?

**中文**

等等，你是怎么知道这些事情的？而且你真的该用这种语气谈论冥后的事情吗？

## `ZagreusField_4301`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Next time, definitely, Alecto. We missed you for sure and wish you could have made it!

**中文**

下次你一定要来，阿勒克图。我们都很想你，觉得要是你也能来就好了！

## `ZagreusField_4302`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, you know, I've come to enjoy these visits with you so much, Alecto, that I've made it something of a regular routine. Form good habits, and then stick to them, I say!

**中文**

这么说吧，阿勒克图，我实在是太喜欢见你了，所以我打算把这当成日常修行。养成好习惯，然后坚持住，就是这样！

## `ZagreusField_4303`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You heard correctly, Meg. The part you didn't hear is, I decided to come home. I did what I needed to do. Now... I'm going to do it again.

**中文**

你听到的没错，墨纪。而你没听到的是，我是自己决定回来的。我做了必须要做的事情，而现在……我打算再做一次。

## `ZagreusField_4304`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It isn't us she's hiding from up there. Father let her go. He never wanted to go after her. So I appreciate your concern; but I'll find her again.

**中文**

她想避而不见的人不是我们。是父亲把她放走了，他根本就没想过要去追她回来。所以，我很感谢你的关心，不过我会再次找到她的。

## `ZagreusField_4305`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Father says there's no escape from here, and I like to repeatedly show him he's wrong. Besides, I think getting out there a bit from time to time does me some good.

**中文**

父亲说没人能逃出这个地方，而我很乐于一次又一次地向他证明，他说错了。另外，我觉得时不时地逃出这个地方，对我也有好处。

## `ZagreusField_4306`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Maybe I just like a challenge, ever thought of that? Besides, what do you even care which pacts I sign or don't?

**中文**

或许我只是喜欢挑战，你有没有想过这一点？另外，我签不签惩罚契约，你又干嘛要在乎呢？

## `ZagreusField_4307`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come on, Meg, why so formal all of a sudden? We've been through this countless times. Wait, is it because...?

**中文**

拜托，墨纪，怎么突然就这么正式了？这种事情我们已经做了无数次了。等等，这是因为……？

## `ZagreusField_4308`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Well, it's just... can we really keep the personal and the professional completely separate like this, Meg? It's with mixed emotions that I fight you to the death, only to be happy to see you back at the House.

**中文**

这么说吧，我只是想说……咱们真的可以像这样，把个人感情与专业态度彻底分开吗，墨纪？每次与你争斗至死的时候，我的心里都很纠结。而看到你回到圣殿的时候，我非常开心。

## `ZagreusField_4309`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

She did... so you heard. I don't know what happened. You didn't even get a chance to say goodbye?

**中文**

她真把她赶走了……你没听错。我都不知道之前发生了什么。你们当时连道别的机会都没有吗？

## `ZagreusField_4310`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Why, because the gift you generously gave me may end up being instrumental in your own demise? Sounds straight out of one of those tales the mortals like to go spreading around.

**中文**

怎么？是因为你慷慨赠送予我的礼物，最终可能会促成你的死亡吗？听起来正像是那些凡人们会津津乐道的故事。

## `ZagreusField_4311`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Come on, Meg, you can do anything if you set your mind to it! Though, don't worry, Battie's just here for the show.

**中文**

来吧，墨纪，只要你下定决心，你就能做得到！不过，不用担心，巴蒂只是摆在这里做做样子而已。

## `ZagreusField_4312`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I think I'm going all the way to the top again, Meg. To find my mother. You won't stop me. Get out of my way.

**中文**

我打算再次冲到地上世界，墨纪，去找我的母亲。你是拦不住我的，让开吧。

## `ZagreusField_4313`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Got some unfinished business with the Olympians to take care of, was in the area, figured I'd say hi. Hi, Meg!

**中文**

我和奥林匹斯众神还有些没处理完的事情，所以我必须得来这里，打个招呼才行。你好啊，墨纪！

## `ZagreusField_4314`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Never enough, Meg. You ready for another go at it, too, I take it?

**中文**

我永远都不会觉得腻，墨纪。我猜你也准备好再来一次了，是吧？

## `ZagreusField_4315`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Not wearing them for fashion's sake, here, Meg. You won't mind if I give you my best shot with Malphon here, will you?

**中文**

我戴上这副拳套可不是为了时髦，墨纪。如果我用玛尔封捶你一顿，你应该不会介意，是吧？

## `ZagreusField_4316`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm... feels like a lot, admittedly, I mean, we've been going at this for a while, now. Four hundred and twelve times...?

**中文**

嗯……不得不说，感觉已经有很多次了。我是说，我们已经打了很长时间了，我大概打败了你四百一十二次……？

## `ZagreusField_4317`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You wound me, Meg! You know I like to barge into situations thoughtlessly and recklessly, and sort everything out later. Forced Overtime's the perfect fit!

**中文**

这话就太伤人了，墨纪！你知道我是那种什么都不想就冲锋陷阵的人，之后的事情等之后再说，“强制加班”真是再适合我不过了！

## `ZagreusField_4318`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It takes some getting used to, that's for sure. I'm not sure I'll ever grow completely accustomed to it. Do you miss it, sir?

**中文**

我得花些时间才能适应，这倒是真的，不过我不太确定到底能不能完全适应那边。你怀念凡间吗，先生？

## `ZagreusField_4319`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I was. Although, between us, sir, it's important that you keep that one a secret, please. Important thing is she's here. Did... you happen to meet her, ever?

**中文**

是啊。不过先生，私下里和你说，这件事情请务必保密，拜托了。重要的是她确实在这里，你以前有没有……刚好见过她？什么时候见的都好。

## `ZagreusField_4320`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, you know. Wouldn't want Father to get too comfortable thinking this place is foolproof. It has a terrifyingly ominous reputation to uphold! There is no escape.

**中文**

哦，这么说吧，我可不希望父亲太安心，以为这个地方是铜墙铁壁。必须维护好这个地方的可怕声誉！没人能逃出这里。

## `ZagreusField_4321`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I think I'm managing, thanks, sir. Though, back when you pushed Bouldy rather than talked to him... I suppose you didn't have much of a choice, did you?

**中文**

我觉得我作息安排得很好，多谢了，先生。不过，想想你以前总是在推石头，而不是和他聊天……我猜你那时别无选择，是吗？

## `ZagreusField_4322`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's... beautiful.

**中文**

真……美啊。

## `ZagreusField_4323`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What are these things...

**中文**

这些是什么东西……

## `ZagreusField_4324`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You're not going to cooperate with me on this, are you? Even now, knowing Mother wants to see me again?

**中文**

这件事情你就不能通融一下吗？你明明知道母亲也很想见我一面，不是吗？

## `ZagreusField_4325`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Then, die, already. And get the hell out of my way.

**中文**

那就去死吧，别再挡我的路了。

## `ZagreusField_4326`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Nyx saved me.... So according to the Fates I was never meant to be born, since neither you nor Mother are of the Underworld, something like that? Or was that just another lie you told, to Mother and whoever else?

**中文**

是倪克斯救了我一命……所以根据命运女神的安排，我从一开始就不该生出来，因为你和我母亲都不是在冥界出生的，是因为这样的事情吗？还是说，这只是你对母亲和其他人撒的另一个谎？

## `ZagreusField_4327`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You really believed that? Then why attempt to have a child, anyway?

**中文**

你真的相信那些话吗？那你为什么还想要有个孩子呢？

## `ZagreusField_4328`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The power of the hunt sounds like good company to keep.

**中文**

狩猎的力量，听起来像是个不错的伙伴。

## `ZagreusField_4329`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Caught you unprepared, did I? After Theseus and his Macedonian, I half-expected to see you thundering around on some golden chariot of your own, drawn by sable steeds, that sort of thing. But it's just you.

**中文**

上次被我打了个措手不及，是吧？见过忒修斯和他的马其顿号之后，我或多或少以为，你会乘着由黑色战马拉的金色战车，或是类似的东西四处游荡，可你什么都没乘。

## `ZagreusField_4330`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's go!

**中文**

开战吧！

## `ZagreusField_4331`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wasn't an oversight. You're strong, Father. I want to be as strong, and more. Not that I'm taking after you, or anything.

**中文**

我没疏忽大意。你很强，父亲，我想变得像你一样强，甚至比你更强。这倒不是说我想以你为榜样什么的。

## `ZagreusField_4332`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, don't take it so hard, Father. There's always next time, right? Meaning, this time. Maybe I won't be so fortunate.

**中文**

哦，别这么难过嘛，父亲。总会有下一次机会，是吧？我指的也就是这一次，说不定这次我就没那么幸运了。

## `ZagreusField_4333`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

If only everybody knew you had a mighty son most capable of cutting you down to size, with or without the ability to vanish suddenly.

**中文**

要是每个人都知道你有个厉害的儿子就好了，无论你有没有突然消失的能力，你儿子都能把你揍得鼻青脸肿。

## `ZagreusField_4334`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

What, you're afraid of a little flower, now, Father? One that imbues me with the fiercest might of Olympus, granted, but still! It looks harmless, at least.

**中文**

怎么，你还怕起一朵小花了吗，父亲？这朵花赐予了我整个奥林匹斯山最所向披靡的力量！不过至少它看起来人畜无害。

## `ZagreusField_4335`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

These claws, I'll have you know, Father, belonged once to an ancient god-king known as Gilgamesh. You ever meet?

**中文**

让我来告诉你吧，父亲。这对爪子曾属于某位远古的神王，名字叫做吉尔伽美什。你见过他吗？

## `ZagreusField_4336`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Sure hope this works.... In the name of Hades! Olympus, this is an official message! For you, in all your splendor, Lord Uncle Zeus.

**中文**

希望这能有用……以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山，这是一份官方来信！是给您的，宙斯叔叔，您的神力光彩夺目。

## `ZagreusField_4337`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

All right, here goes. In the name of Hades! Olympus! This is an official message! Uncle Poseidon, hope you can make this out.

**中文**

好了，准备送信吧。以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山，这是一份官方来信！波塞冬叔叔，希望您能弄清楚它的含义。

## `ZagreusField_4338`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

This is it, then. In the name of Hades! Olympus, this is an official message! Lady Athena, I trust you'll quickly figure this one out.

**中文**

好吧，那就这样吧。以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山，这是一份官方来信！雅典娜女士，我相信你很快就能弄清楚它的含义。

## `ZagreusField_4339`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

OK, I think I'm ready. In the name of Hades! Olympus, this is an official message! And I hope it finds you well, Lady Aphrodite!

**中文**

好了，我觉得我准备好了。以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山，这是一份官方来信！希望这封信能安全地交到你的手里，阿弗洛狄忒女士！

## `ZagreusField_4340`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Here goes, then. In the name of Hades! Olympus, this is an official message! Lord Ares, please consider our proposal... once you're able to decipher it.

**中文**

那就这样吧。以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山，这是一份官方来信！阿瑞斯大人，请考虑一下我们的提案吧……如果您能明白它的意思的话。

## `ZagreusField_4341`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Found you, Huntress, here. In the name of Hades! Olympus, this is an official message! Especially for you, Lady Artemis!

**中文**

找到你了，女猎手。奥林匹斯山，这是一份官方来信！是特别送给你的，阿尔忒弥斯女士！

## `ZagreusField_4342`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hope this lives up to your expectations, mate. In the name of Hades! Olympus? This is an official message! Should be a good time, Lord Dionysus!

**中文**

希望这不会让你失望，朋友。以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山，这是一份官方来信！你应该会很觉得很有趣，迪奥尼索司大人！

## `ZagreusField_4343`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I get to be the messenger this time. In the name of Hades! Olympus, this is an official message! Lord Hermes? Got here as quickly as I could.

**中文**

这次要轮到我来送信了。以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山，这是一份官方来信！赫尔墨斯大人在吗？马上赶到这里吧。

## `ZagreusField_4344`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hope Mother knows what she's doing, in the name of Hades! Olympus, this is an official message! Lady Demeter, this is for you.

**中文**

希望妈妈明白她在做什么。以哈迪斯之名！奥林匹斯山，这是一份官方来信！得墨忒尔女士，这是给您的信。

## `ZagreusField_4345`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Father!!

父亲！！

## `ZagreusField_4346`

**EN**

Oh, something's just about always amiss back home, sir. Figured it couldn't hurt to check up on things in Tartarus a bit more frequently.

哦，家里有些事情始终不太对劲，先生。我觉得多检查检查塔耳塔罗斯的情况，也不会有什么坏处。

你的东西不错嘛，伙计。

## `ZagreusField_4442`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Plenty in stock.

**中文**

库存很充足。

## `ZagreusField_4443`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good stuff in stock.

**中文**

你这里有不少好东西。

## `ZagreusField_4444`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You're out of stock, Charon!

**中文**

你的东西都卖光了！

## `ZagreusField_4445`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What else you got, Charon?!

**中文**

你还有别的东西吗，卡戎？！

## `ZagreusField_4446`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sigh

**中文**

叹气

## `ZagreusField_4447`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Scoff

**中文**

嘲笑

## `ZagreusField_4448`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh.

**中文**

哦。

## `ZagreusField_4449`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How does he...

**中文**

他是怎么……

## `ZagreusField_4450`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Gone.

**中文**

走吧。

## `ZagreusField_4451`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bye.

**中文**

再见。

## `ZagreusField_4452`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Wasn't about to start.

**中文**

我还没打算开始呢。

## `ZagreusField_4453`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm getting there.

**中文**

我已经越来越近了。

## `ZagreusField_4454`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No harm in giving it another shot.

**中文**

再试一次也没什么的。

## `ZagreusField_4455`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll see you upstairs, boy.

**中文**

我到上面再找你吧，好狗狗。

## `ZagreusField_4456`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mortals know to make the most of it.

**中文**

凡人都明白，要最大限度地利用一切。

## `ZagreusField_4457`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I wonder if I'll beat you once again.

**中文**

不知道我是不是得再击败你一次。

## `ZagreusField_4458`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Glad I'm on his good side...

**中文**

幸好他毕竟喜欢我……

## `ZagreusField_4459`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I've found my place I think.

**中文**

我觉得我找到了我该去的地方。

## `ZagreusField_4460`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

He's right.

**中文**

他说得没错。

## `ZagreusField_4461`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See you again soon, Meg.

**中文**

回头见吧，墨纪。

## `ZagreusField_4462`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No one gets out.

**中文**

没人能从这里出去。

## `ZagreusField_4463`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, I don't know, the surface is quite nice.

**中文**

哦，我可说不好，上面的世界还挺好的。

## `ZagreusField_4464`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You don't mean that, Father.

**中文**

你不是认真的吧，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_4465`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I am the god of blood.

**中文**

我是血神。

## `ZagreusField_4466`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Guess that's a compliment!

**中文**

我猜那是在夸我吧！

## `ZagreusField_4467`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

How times have changed.

**中文**

时光荏苒啊。

## `ZagreusField_4468`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I've both my parents' eyes.

**中文**

我的眼睛和我的父母都很像。

## `ZagreusField_4469`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got the best instructor who ever lived.

**中文**

我有有史以来最优秀的导师。

## `ZagreusField_4470`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My differences have made me strong, all right.

**中文**

好了，正是因为我与众不同，我才能这么强。

## `ZagreusField_4471`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Here's to my friends and family...

**中文**

这是为了我的朋友与家人……

## `ZagreusField_4472`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I swear, this family...

**中文**

我敢发誓，这个家族……

## `ZagreusField_4473`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Still figuring our boundaries out I guess.

**中文**

我猜，我们依然在努力弄清界限吧。

## `ZagreusField_4474`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You taught me that, at least.

**中文**

至少那些是你教会我的。

## `ZagreusField_4475`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm fond of running, thanks.

**中文**

我还挺喜欢逃跑的，多谢了。

## `ZagreusField_4476`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I will.

**中文**

我会的。

## `ZagreusField_4477`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Set your aim higher, too.

**中文**

你的目标也应该定得高一些。

## `ZagreusField_4478`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's nice enough to visit, anyhow.

**中文**

不管怎么说，看看就很不错了。

## `ZagreusField_4479`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll see what I can do.

**中文**

我看看能做点什么。

## `ZagreusField_4480`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This is our home.

**中文**

这里是我们的家。

## `ZagreusField_4481`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Time to uphold it, then.

**中文**

是时候把它变得更好了。

## `ZagreusField_4482`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A little pride's all right.

**中文**

一点点骄傲也无伤大雅。

## `ZagreusField_4483`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Loaded.

**中文**

装填好了。

## `ZagreusField_4484`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Loaded!

**中文**

装填好了！

## `ZagreusField_4485`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Right.

**中文**

很好。

## `ZagreusField_4486`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's in.

**中文**

弄好了。

## `ZagreusField_4487`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

OK.

**中文**

很好。

## `ZagreusField_4488`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

OK!

**中文**

好了！

## `ZagreusField_4489`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No murder.

**中文**

别说什么谋杀了。

## `ZagreusField_4490`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm innocent.

**中文**

我是无辜的。

## `ZagreusField_4491`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Next time, Tisiphone.

**中文**

下次再见吧，提西福涅。

## `ZagreusField_4492`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See you again I'm sure.

**中文**

我们会再见面的，我敢肯定。

## `ZagreusField_4493`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

She's a bit much.

**中文**

她有点过火了。

## `ZagreusField_4494`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nice chatting.

**中文**

和你谈得很愉快。

## `ZagreusField_4495`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ugh, that one...

**中文**

呃，那个啊……

## `ZagreusField_4497`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

She's tough...

**中文**

她可真难缠……

## `ZagreusField_4498`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got her.

**中文**

干掉她了。

## `ZagreusField_4499`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

She won't like this.

**中文**

她肯定不喜欢这样。

## `ZagreusField_4500`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ahh.

**中文**

啊啊。

## `ZagreusField_4501`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Refreshing!

**中文**

神清气爽！

## `ZagreusField_4502`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Crisp.

**中文**

嘎嘣脆。

## `ZagreusField_4503`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nice and cool.

**中文**

感觉不错。

## `ZagreusField_4504`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's good.

**中文**

很好。

## `ZagreusField_4505`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whew.

**中文**

呼。

## `ZagreusField_4506`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The hunt is on.

**中文**

狩猎开始了。

## `ZagreusField_4507`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm back, Father.

**中文**

我回来了，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_4508`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Been waiting long?

**中文**

等我很久了吗？

## `ZagreusField_4509`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Every blasted time.

**中文**

每次都这样。

## `ZagreusField_4510`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Out of my damn way.

**中文**

别给我挡道。

## `ZagreusField_4511`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm here, Father. Come on.

**中文**

我来了，父亲，来吧。

## `ZagreusField_4512`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You're not going to stop me.

**中文**

你是阻止不了我的。

## `ZagreusField_4513`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...I'll just keep coming back...

**中文**

……我会一次次地回来……

## `ZagreusField_4514`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You're never late, Father.

**中文**

你从来都不会迟到啊，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_4515`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's kind of nice out here.

**中文**

外面的世界还挺不错的。

## `ZagreusField_4516`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Haven't you had enough?

**中文**

你就不觉得腻吗？

## `ZagreusField_4517`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Deep Breath

**中文**

深呼吸

## `ZagreusField_4518`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right... this is it...

**中文**

好了……就这样吧……

## `ZagreusField_4519`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You still don't understand...

**中文**

你还是不明白……

## `ZagreusField_4520`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Escaped again, Father.

**中文**

我又逃出去了，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_4521`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Looks like the realm needs some adjustments still!

**中文**

看起来这个地方还需要调整一下！

## `ZagreusField_4522`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got here as quickly as I could.

**中文**

我以最快的速度赶到这里了。

## `ZagreusField_4523`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Hate to keep you from your parchmentwork.

**中文**

真不想耽误你忙你的文书工作。

## `ZagreusField_4524`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh hi, Father!

**中文**

哦，你好啊，父亲！

## `ZagreusField_4525`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You're looking very stately, there, Father.

**中文**

你看起来很威武啊，父亲。

## `ZagreusField_4526`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You ready for another go?

**中文**

准备好再来一场了吗？

## `ZagreusField_4527`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Think you can beat me this time, Father?

**中文**

你觉得你这次打得过我吗，父亲？

## `ZagreusField_4528`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm going to get you back for last time, you know.

**中文**

你知道的吧，我打算报上一次的仇了。

## `ZagreusField_4529`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Still chilly out here thanks to Grandmother...

**中文**

都是外婆的缘故，这里还是那么冷……

## `ZagreusField_4530`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What?!

**中文**

什么？！

## `ZagreusField_4531`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't kill me, boy!!

**中文**

别杀我啊，乖狗狗！！

## `ZagreusField_4532`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Have mercy, boy!!

**中文**

手下留情啊，乖狗狗！！

## `ZagreusField_4533`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Down, boy!! No treat. No treat!!

**中文**

快坐下，乖狗狗！！别咬我，别咬我！！

## `ZagreusField_4534`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bad boy! Bad!

**中文**

真是个坏狗狗！太坏了！

## `ZagreusField_4535`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Bad boy! I mean.. good boy?!

**中文**

真是个坏狗狗！我是说……好狗狗？！

## `ZagreusField_4536`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't listen to him, boy!

**中文**

别听他的，乖狗狗！

## `ZagreusField_4537`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Don't have to do this, boy!

**中文**

你没必要非这么做不可，乖狗狗！

## `ZagreusField_4538`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Charon, mate? Wanted to thank you very, very much for bringing Mother and me home. Your boat's quite comfortable, you know! Though, I suppose you won't be making a habit of giving me a ride, will you?

**中文**

卡戎老伙计？真的非常非常感谢你把我和母亲带回家。说真的，你的船坐起来真舒服！不过我猜，你不会允许我养成搭便车的习惯，是吧？

## `ZagreusField_4539`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's quite all right, I don't mind the walk.

**中文**

没什么的，我不介意走一走。

## `ZagreusField_4540`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Charon, as I know you serve the realm, I just wanted to say I look forward to continuing to work with you in a somewhat more official capacity. I'll still be breaking out of here like always, but... well, you've probably heard.

**中文**

卡戎，据我所知，您效忠于这个王国。我只是想说，我期待能以某种更加正式的身份继续与你合作。我还是会像以前一样逃出这个地方，不过……嗯，你可能已经听说过了。

## `ZagreusField_4541`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Appreciate your support!

**中文**

感谢你的帮忙！

## `ZagreusField_4542`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hope you enjoyed the feast with the Olympians, Charon! We didn't have much of a chance to chat, though it looked like you and Hermes were having a good time.

**中文**

希望你还喜欢和奥林匹斯众神的盛宴，卡戎！咱们聊的机会不太多，不过看起来，你当时和赫尔墨斯好像聊得很开心。

## `ZagreusField_4543`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We'll have to do it again sometime.

**中文**

咱们下次有机会得再开一次宴会。

## `ZagreusField_4544`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I'm here on official business this time, Tisiphone, so, uh... stand aside! Although, I guess the whole point of this job is that you're meant to try and stop me anyway, isn't that right?

**中文**

我这次是来执行公务的，提西福涅。所以，呃……立正，站好！不过我猜，你这份工作的重点就是无论如何都要阻止我，是这样的吧？

## `ZagreusField_4545`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well then, let's get to work!

**中文**

好吧，那就开始干活吧！

## `ZagreusField_4546`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Oh, sorry that you couldn't make the feast back at the House, Tisiphone. Though, I don't really think it was your kind of scene. Although, Ares was there, he's probably done some murders, right?

**中文**

哦，圣殿里举行宴会的时候你没能赶回来，这真是太遗憾了，提西福涅。不过我猜，你也不喜欢那样的场合。不过阿瑞斯也参加了宴会，他肯定干过不少谋杀这类的事情，是吧？

## `ZagreusField_4547`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Erm, Grandmother? If you don't mind I call you that. Knowing we're related like this fills me with pride. Your power over life... I think I've some of it in me! And I could not have come this far without your help. Please, for everything... this is for you.

**中文**

呃，外婆？希望你不介意我这么叫你。知道你是我的亲人之后，我感到非常骄傲。你掌控生命的力量……我想我也拥有一部分这样的力量！而且，如果不是因为有你的帮助，我也不可能走到这么远。请收下这份心意吧……感谢你做的一切。

## `ZagreusField_4548`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Would be odd of you to start right now!

**中文**

你现在要是对我手下留情了，那才奇怪呢！

## `ZagreusField_4549`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What, you mean like last time?

**中文**

怎么，你想像上次一样吗？

## `ZagreusField_4550`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Think we both know the answer to that one!

**中文**

我猜，咱们都知道这个问题的答案了！

## `ZagreusField_4551`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not really, though, why do you ask?

**中文**

算不上吧，怎么问起这个了？

## `ZagreusField_4552`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I guess we'll find out soon, won't we?

**中文**

我猜我们很快就能找到答案了，是吧？

## `ZagreusField_4553`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'm sure she'll wear you down eventually!

**中文**

我敢肯定，她到最后会让你屈服的！

## `ZagreusField_4554`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Who knows? Mother's full of surprises!

**中文**

谁知道呢？母亲总是充满了惊喜！

## `ZagreusField_4555`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Perish the thought of you being less difficult, Father!

**中文**

你最好别觉得自己能变得没那么烦人，父亲！

## `ZagreusField_4556`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Is that your deepest fear of late, Father?

**中文**

那就是你最近最害怕的事情吗，父亲？

## `ZagreusField_4557`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I think you'll get there, yes!

**中文**

我觉得你做得到，加油！

## `ZagreusField_4558`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

My chambers just aren't as enjoyable to fight!

**中文**

打扫房间可不像打架这么有意思！

## `ZagreusField_4559`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What, so they could look like your boring chambers?

**中文**

怎么，这样它们看起来就能像你那无聊的房间一样了吗？

## `ZagreusField_4560`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Too busy ransacking through your domain, sorry!

**中文**

抱歉，我还得忙着洗劫你的王国呢！

## `ZagreusField_4562`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's funny, you wouldn't be here without Olympus, either!

**中文**

真有意思，要是没了奥林匹斯众神，你也来不了这里！

## `ZagreusField_4563`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You act like you do everything yourself!

**中文**

你说得就好像你什么事情都亲力亲为一样！

## `ZagreusField_4564`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You should be pleased to have their help!

**中文**

有他们帮忙，你应该感到高兴才是！

## `ZagreusField_4565`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

And what's your point, exactly, there?

**中文**

你到底想说什么？

## `ZagreusField_4566`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll leave that one for you to figure out!

**中文**

这个问题的答案，你自己来找吧！

## `ZagreusField_4567`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Who do you think, Father?

**中文**

你觉得是谁呢，父亲？

## `ZagreusField_4568`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, quite a few of them, I'll have you know!

**中文**

哦，有不少呢，你马上就见识到了！

## `ZagreusField_4569`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Certainly not you!

**中文**

反正不是你！

## `ZagreusField_4570`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Last I checked princes were meant to be coddled!

**中文**

我还以为王子就是用来溺爱的呢！

## `ZagreusField_4571`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's called showing affection to your children, Father!

**中文**

这叫“向你的孩子们表达爱意”，父亲！

## `ZagreusField_4572`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whatever will you do about it, Father?

**中文**

那你打算怎么办呢，父亲？

## `ZagreusField_4573`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

What do you even care?

**中文**

你真在乎这件事情吗？

## `ZagreusField_4574`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Quite lovely, Father, how about yourself?

**中文**

那是相当开心，父亲，你呢？

## `ZagreusField_4575`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

There's a refreshing chill now that you mention it!

**中文**

既然你提起来了，那可真是让人神清气爽！

## `ZagreusField_4576`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh I most definitely am, thank you, Father!

**中文**

哦，当然了。谢谢你，父亲！

## `ZagreusField_4577`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It could be going better, I suppose!

**中文**

我猜我还能过得更开心！

## `ZagreusField_4578`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You'll have to let me know when this is over!

**中文**

等我们打完之后，你再来评论评论吧！

## `ZagreusField_4579`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We'll soon find out I think!

**中文**

我觉得咱们很快就知道答案了！

## `ZagreusField_4581`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You know what, probably not!

**中文**

你知道吗？那可未必！

## `ZagreusField_4582`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Was just about to, thanks, Father!

**中文**

我正打算这样呢，谢了，父亲！

## `ZagreusField_4583`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right, because you asked so nicely there!

**中文**

好吧，既然你都诚心诚意地开口了！

## `ZagreusField_4584`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sure. Watch this!

**中文**

没问题，看好了！

## `ZagreusField_4585`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You'll see soon enough!

**中文**

你马上就见识到了！

## `ZagreusField_4586`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, absolutely wonderful, thank you!

**中文**

哦，真的是太棒了，谢谢你！

## `ZagreusField_4587`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It went all right, now that you mention it!

**中文**

既然你提起来了，确实过得还不错！

## `ZagreusField_4588`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Quite pleasant, actually!

**中文**

事实上，真的挺不错的！

## `ZagreusField_4589`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's always pleasant in the Underworld!

**中文**

在冥界的生活总是这么愉悦！

## `ZagreusField_4590`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It fared just great, thank you for asking, there!

**中文**

我抢得相当开心，谢谢你关心！

## `ZagreusField_4591`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Could have gone better, though what can you do?

**中文**

本可以搜刮到更多东西，不过这样也够了吧？

## `ZagreusField_4592`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not bad at all, this time!

**中文**

这次还不错嘛！

## `ZagreusField_4593`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I've had better escape attempts I think!

**中文**

我觉得我之前的纪录更好！

## `ZagreusField_4594`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

And we finally have an activity we can do together!

**中文**

咱们终于有可以一起做的事情了！

## `ZagreusField_4595`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Seems like you have a lot to learn about discipline, huh?

**中文**

关于纪律，看来你要学的东西还很多呢，是吧？

## `ZagreusField_4596`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

We gods do like to fight!

**中文**

我们神就是喜欢战斗！

## `ZagreusField_4597`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Discipline, that's what we're calling it?

**中文**

“纪律”，咱们是这么称呼的吧？

## `ZagreusField_4598`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Why, thanks!

**中文**

哦，谢谢你！

## `ZagreusField_4599`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Good chat!

**中文**

谢谢你这么说！

## `ZagreusField_4600`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Look at that lovely sky!

**中文**

看看那天空，多漂亮！

## `ZagreusField_4601`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Am not!

**中文**

我没有！

## `ZagreusField_4602`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Great!

**中文**

太棒了！

## `ZagreusField_4603`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right, then!

**中文**

好了！

## `ZagreusField_4604`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Save it, then!

**中文**

那就省省吧！

## `ZagreusField_4605`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well thanks for sharing, anyway!

**中文**

总之，感谢你的意见！

## `ZagreusField_4606`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Let's do it then!

**中文**

那就开打吧！

## `ZagreusField_4607`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Same goes for you!

**中文**

你也一样！

## `ZagreusField_4608`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Worry about yourself!

**中文**

还是担心你自己吧！

## `ZagreusField_4609`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Likewise to you!

**中文**

你也一样！

## `ZagreusField_4610`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No plans for that!

**中文**

我可没那个打算！

## `ZagreusField_4611`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Old age, it happens!

**中文**

因为你岁数大了，这种事情是会发生的！

## `ZagreusField_4612`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You've been so busy!

**中文**

因为你一直都太忙了！

## `ZagreusField_4613`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Nope!

**中文**

没门！

## `ZagreusField_4614`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

They're boring, I say!

**中文**

我觉得你的房间太枯燥了！

## `ZagreusField_4615`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

This isn't real work?

**中文**

这还算不上真正的工作吗？

## `ZagreusField_4616`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Just what you've taught me!

**中文**

你就是这么教我的！

## `ZagreusField_4617`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Whatever you say!

**中文**

随便你怎么说吧！

## `ZagreusField_4618`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Really?

**中文**

真的吗？

## `ZagreusField_4619`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Blast you right back!

**中文**

不，去你的！

## `ZagreusField_4620`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Chuckle

**中文**

轻笑

## `ZagreusField_4621`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, cheers!

**中文**

好吧，不说了！

## `ZagreusField_4622`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I know a thing or two!

**中文**

我多少还是知道点的！

## `ZagreusField_4623`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I can tell!

**中文**

看得出来！

## `ZagreusField_4624`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Clearly!

**中文**

显而易见！

## `ZagreusField_4625`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Ominous!

**中文**

真吓人！

## `ZagreusField_4626`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sounds good!

**中文**

听起来不错！

## `ZagreusField_4627`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well I would beg to differ!

**中文**

我保留不同意见！

## `ZagreusField_4628`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mm, no thanks!

**中文**

嗯，不了，谢谢！

## `ZagreusField_4629`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right, I won't!

**中文**

好吧，我没这个打算！

## `ZagreusField_4630`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Fine!

**中文**

好！

## `ZagreusField_4631`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

You're most welcome!

**中文**

不必客气！

## `ZagreusField_4632`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Sure thing!

**中文**

没问题！

## `ZagreusField_4633`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Happy to!

**中文**

我很乐意！

## `ZagreusField_4634`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Always!

**中文**

我就这样！

## `ZagreusField_4635`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Well, carry on!

**中文**

很好，再接再厉！

## `ZagreusField_4636`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thanks for your concern!

**中文**

谢谢你的关心！

## `ZagreusField_4637`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It'll be all right!

**中文**

不会有问题的！

## `ZagreusField_4638`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Not just yet!

**中文**

我才没有呢！

## `ZagreusField_4639`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I was. Although, between us, sir, it's important that you keep that one a secret, please. Did... you happen to meet her, ever?

**中文**

是啊。不过先生，私下里和你说，这件事情请务必保密，拜托了。你以前有没有……刚好见过她？什么时候见的都好。

## `ZagreusField_4640`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Least I'm picking up some coin!

**中文**

起码我还捡了些钱！

## `ZagreusField_4641`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cheers for the coin!

**中文**

有钱真好！

## `ZagreusField_4642`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

The Pact did make the road here a little more fraught than usual. But if it couldn't stop me, I can't imagine it could stop Demeter.

**中文**

惩罚契约确实让这一路过来的旅程变得有点坎坷。不过，如果它连我都拦不住的话，那恐怕就更拦不住得墨忒尔了。

## `ZagreusField_4643`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Hey, I resent that, Father! I'm more than capable of getting here all on my own, now, thanks.

**中文**

嘿，这么说就太不像话了，父亲！我就算只靠我自己，想来这里也绰绰有余，不必客气。

## `ZagreusField_4644`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Does feel a bit different, maybe. Though, Demeter said something about how this spot, in particular, would remain bereft of warmth, as a reminder. Do you think she knows what happened with Mother, really?

**中文**

或许确实感觉不太一样。不过，得墨忒尔说了些关于这个地方的话。她说这里依然要保持严寒，以此提醒人们。你觉得，她真的已经知道发生在母亲身上的事情了吗？

## `ZagreusField_4645`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I suppose you're about to put that learning to practice in another moment here?

**中文**

看来你打算把学到的一切再次应用在实战中了，是吗？

## `ZagreusField_4646`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Urgh... can't... hold... on...

**中文**

呃……已经……撑……不住……

## `ZagreusField_4647`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Grr... I... can't...

**中文**

呃……我……不行……

## `ZagreusField_4648`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Won't... go... back...!

**中文**

不会……回头……！

## `ZagreusField_4649`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

No... not... yet...

**中文**

不……现在……不行……

## `ZagreusField_4650`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I'll... be... back...

**中文**

我会……回来……的……

## `ZagreusField_4651`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Urgh... mmm... ungh...

**中文**

呃……嗯……呃啊……

## `ZagreusField_4652`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Khh... uhhh... urgh...

**中文**

咳……呃……呃啊……

## `ZagreusField_4653`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Mmph... rrkh... ungh...

**中文**

嗯……呃……呃啊……

## `ZagreusField_4654`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You know... I think I'd rather fight you to the death again than try to answer either of those questions, right now.

**中文**

老实说……比起回答你的问题，我现在更希望能和你打个至死方休。

## `ZagreusField_4655`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Extra gemstones...! The Vanquisher's Keep.

**中文**

额外的宝石……！是丰功伟绩。

## `ZagreusField_4656`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

All right, we made it, Malphon. Appreciate you lending me the might of Gilgamesh.

**中文**

好，我们成功了，玛尔封。感谢你借给我吉尔伽美什的力量。

## `ZagreusField_4657`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

To the death!

**中文**

至死方休！

## `ZagreusField_4658`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

I did... but I had to come back. And, not exactly voluntarily. I'll be visiting up there from time to time, though... it looks like I'll be staying here.

**中文**

我是逃出去了……但我还是得回来，而且不是自愿的那种。我会时不时地去地上世界看看的，不过……看起来我以后也只能呆在这里了。

## `ZagreusField_4659`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

It's my mother. She's alive and well! It's just that I can't stay with her up there for very long! So we'll have to catch up... one visit at a time.

**中文**

是我的母亲，她还活着，一切都好！只不过我没办法陪她呆得太久。所以我……每出去一次，才能和她见一次面。

## `ZagreusField_4660`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I know what you mean.

**中文**

我明白你的意思。

## `ZagreusField_4661`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Huh, a little quieter than usual.

**中文**

嗯，比平日里更安静。

## `ZagreusField_4662`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...Oh.

**中文**

……哦。

## `ZagreusField_4663`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Thought Voidstones didn't get this big...

**中文**

竟然会有这么大的虚无宝石……

## `ZagreusField_4664`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That's one big Voidstone.

**中文**

这宝石可真大啊。

## `ZagreusField_4665`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Another big Voidstone, huh.

**中文**

又是一颗大宝石。

## `ZagreusField_4666`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Come on up, big Voidstone.

**中文**

来吧，虚无宝石。

## `ZagreusField_4667`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Come forth, big Voidstone!

**中文**

来呀，虚无宝石！

## `ZagreusField_4668`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

Wouldn't you like to know? Not even Nyx believed that I could come this far without aid from the Olympians. Maybe I just have a knack for proving everybody wrong.

**中文**

你不想知道是怎么回事吗？就连倪克斯都不相信我可以在没有奥林匹斯众神帮助的情况下走出这么远。或许我就是有本事向别人证明：他们都错了。

## `ZagreusField_4669`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

That was the last of the invitations...

**中文**

那是最后一份邀请函了……

## `ZagreusField_4670`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Just need to tell Mother. Once I get home...

**中文**

等我回到家，一定要告诉母亲……

## `ZagreusField_4671`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cough Ugh, I... cold...

**中文**

咳嗽呃，我……好冷……

## `ZagreusField_4672`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cough Ungh...

**中文**

咳嗽呃……

## `ZagreusField_4673`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

They'll just have to try harder next time.

**中文**

他们下次得再努力一点。

## `ZagreusField_4674`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I barge where I please.

**中文**

我想去哪儿就去哪儿。

## `ZagreusField_4675`

**Speaker:** Zagreus

**EN**

You offering to take me fishing, Father? Sounds more pleasant than having you slaughter me again, or the other way around.

**中文**

你是想和我一起钓鱼吗，父亲？听起来比打打杀杀的要好多了。

## `ZagreusField_4676`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Urgh! More obols, more suffering.

**中文**

嗯哼！钱越多，苦越多。

## `ZagreusField_4677`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Asterius!

**中文**

是阿斯忒里俄斯！

## `ZagreusField_4678`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Asterius.

**中文**

阿斯忒里俄斯.

## `ZagreusField_4679`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oh, hi.

**中文**

哦，嗨。

## `ZagreusField_4680`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Bull of Minos!

**中文**

是米诺斯的公牛！

## `ZagreusField_4681`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

The Bull of Minos.

**中文**

米诺斯公牛。

## `ZagreusField_4682`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Rematch?

**中文**

复赛？

## `ZagreusField_4683`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Rematch, then?

**中文**

再打一场，嗯？

## `ZagreusField_4684`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Shall we, sir?

**中文**

可以开始了吗，先生？

## `ZagreusField_4685`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Till next time, sir.

**中文**

下次见吧，先生。

## `ZagreusField_4686`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

See you soon!

**中文**

回见！

## `ZagreusField_4687`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got you, Bull-man.

**中文**

抓到你了，牛头怪。

## `ZagreusField_4688`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Tell Theseus I said hi!

**中文**

跟忒修斯传达我的问候！

## `ZagreusField_4689`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Cheers for the fight!

**中文**

欢呼吧！

## `ZagreusField_4690`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Got him again.

**中文**

抓到他了。

## `ZagreusField_4691`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

I got him.

**中文**

我抓到他了。

## `ZagreusField_4692`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

A warm-up for later.

**中文**

热身而已。

## `ZagreusField_4694`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Huh... to see this each day...

**中文**

啊……如果每天都能见到这样的景色……

## `ZagreusField_4695`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

It's... I...

**中文**

这……我……

## `ZagreusField_4696`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...Beautiful...

**中文**

……真美……

## `ZagreusField_4697`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...The sun...

**中文**

……太阳……

## `ZagreusField_4698`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

...The sun... Helios...

**中文**

……太阳……赫利俄斯……

## `ZagreusField_4699`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Just up ahead...

**中文**

只需一往直前……

## `ZagreusField_4700`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Beyond the frozen overlook... and all that.

**中文**

在冰天雪地之上……

## `ZagreusField_4701`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Oof, it's cold...

**中文**

呼，好冷……

## `ZagreusField_4702`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Just a bit farther...

**中文**

只要再走远一些……

## `ZagreusField_4703`

**Speaker:** ZagreusField

**EN**

Almost there...

**中文**

还差一点...
